Hierarchical Denoising For Multi-Step Visual Reasoning
Authors: Zezhong Qian, Xiaowei Chi, Chak-Wing Mak, Tianze Zhou, Ruibin Yuan, Yuhan Rui, Hengzhe Sun, Zhuoqun Wu, Yuming Li, Siyuan Qian, Sirui Han, Shanghang Zhang
First: 2026-07-16T17:59:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:59:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Video models are evolving into vision foundation models, yet they still lack human-like multi-step reasoning. Streaming autoregressive diffusion models are efficient but limited in reasoning, while bidirectional diffusion enables global revision with high inference costs due to dense frame-level denoising. Both paradigms struggle to achieve logical consistency and low-latency streaming for complex reasoning tasks. We propose HDR (Hierarchical Denoising for Visual Reasoning), a unified framework that integrates hierarchical latents into causal video generation for multi-step reasoning. HDR organizes video latents into a tree-structured hierarchy, enabling coarse-to-fine reasoning before streaming output. Coarse denoising layers preserve uncertain hypotheses for global planning, while finer layers progressively refine them into concrete visual states. A sparse hierarchical attention pattern (SHAP) further reduces temporal attention costs. We introduce a level-stratified multi-step video reasoning benchmark with out-of-distribution cases, covering six tasks: maze navigation, Tower of Hanoi, one-line drawing, sliding puzzle, Sokoban, and water pouring. Compared with streaming autoregressive diffusion baselines, HDR improves success from 34.22 to 60.29 (76.2% relative gain) and increases average progress from 76.00 to 89.56, demonstrating more consistent reasoning trajectories. HDR maintains low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent, achieving 54.2 times faster inference than bidirectional diffusion. It also retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, compared with 52.0% for bidirectional diffusion. Real-world robot experiments further demonstrate HDR's potential for physical interaction and world modeling. Project demo: https://hierarchical-diffusion-reasoning.github.io/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于多步视觉推理的分层去噪
视频模型正演变为视觉基础模型，但仍缺乏类人多步推理能力。流式自回归扩散模型效率高但推理能力有限，双向扩散虽支持全局修订，但因密集帧级去噪导致推理成本高。两种范式均难以在复杂推理任务中实现逻辑一致性和低延迟流式输出。我们提出HDR（用于视觉推理的分层去噪），一个将分层潜在变量整合进因果视频生成的统一框架，支持多步推理。HDR将视频潜在变量组织为树状分层结构，实现从粗到精的推理后再流式输出。粗粒度去噪层保留不确定假设以支持全局规划，细粒度层逐步将其细化为具体视觉状态。稀疏分层注意力模式（SHAP）进一步降低时间注意力成本。我们引入了一个分层多步视频推理基准测试，包含分布外案例，涵盖六项任务：迷宫导航、汉诺塔、一笔画、滑块拼图、推箱子和倒水。与流式自回归扩散基线相比，HDR成功率达34.22%提升至60.29%（相对提升76.2%），平均进度从76.00提升至89.56，展现出更一致的推理轨迹。HDR保持0.70秒/潜在变量的低延迟流式输出，推理速度比双向扩散快54.2倍。仅用2%训练数据时，HDR仍保持82.9%的全数据性能，而双向扩散仅为52.0%。真实机器人实验进一步验证了HDR在物理交互与世界建模方面的潜力。项目演示：https://hierarchical-diffusion-reasoning.github.io/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for human-like multi-step reasoning in video models, the authors propose HDR, a hierarchical denoising framework that integrates tree-structured latent representations into causal video generation. HDR enables coarse-to-fine reasoning by first preserving uncertain hypotheses at high levels for global planning, then progressively refining them into detailed visual outputs. To reduce computational cost, it employs a sparse hierarchical attention pattern. Evaluated on a six-task benchmark including maze navigation and Sokoban, HDR outperforms streaming autoregressive diffusion models with a 76.2% relative gain in success rate (34.22→60.29) and higher average progress (76.00→89.56). It achieves low-latency streaming at 0.70 seconds per latent—54.2x faster than bidirectional diffusion—and retains 82.9% of full-data performance with only 2% training data, demonstrating strong generalization and efficiency.
为实现视频模型中类人多步推理能力，HDR提出一种基于层次化去噪的统一框架，将树状结构的潜在表示融入因果视频生成。该框架先在粗粒度层级保留不确定假设以支持全局规划，再逐步细化为具体视觉状态。稀疏层次注意力机制降低时间计算开销。在包含迷宫导航、汉诺塔等六项任务的基准测试中，HDR相较流式自回归扩散模型成功率达60.29%（提升76.2%），平均进度达89.56，推理延迟仅0.70秒/潜在层，比双向扩散快54.2倍。仅用2%训练数据即可达到82.9%的完整数据性能，优于双向扩散的52.0%。真实机器人实验验证其在物理交互与世界建模中的潜力。
Partition, Prompt, Aggregate: Statistical Self-Consistency in Language Models
Authors: Patrik Wolf, Thomas Kleine Buening, Andreas Krause, Celestine Mendler-Dünner
First: 2026-07-16T17:59:31+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:59:31+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In-context learning is commonly interpreted as a form of conditional inference, in which the prompt specifies a context and the model's output is treated as an estimate of the corresponding conditional distribution. If this interpretation holds, then LLM estimates should satisfy basic probabilistic identities. In particular, the law of total probability asserts that prior-weighted conditional distributions aggregate into population-level marginals over any valid partition of the population. In this work, we investigate to what extent LLM estimates adhere to this self-consistency principle. We use binary trees as an evaluation scaffold to recursively partition a population into increasingly fine-grained subpopulations. We then prompt LLMs with verbalized subpopulation descriptions in context, aggregate the resulting estimates back into population-level estimates, and compare them across partitions of varying granularity. Applying this protocol across problem domains and state-of-the-art frontier models, we show widespread violations of basic consistency properties. An in-depth study of persona prompting reveals a pattern we call the macro fallacy: estimates reconstructed from more fine-grained subpopulation responses are often better aligned with human reference data than direct population-level estimates. This effect persists across variations in tree structure and estimation task, and can be partially recovered through implicit prompting. Together, these findings suggest that models possess relevant subpopulation knowledge but do not reliably propagate it into aggregate estimates. This gap establishes statistical self-consistency as an unsaturated, reference-free criterion for evaluating LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：分区、提示、聚合：语言模型中的统计自一致性
上下文学习通常被解释为一种条件推断形式，其中提示指定上下文，模型输出被视为相应条件分布的估计。若此解释成立，则大语言模型（LLM）的估计应满足基本概率恒等式。特别是，全概率定律指出，加权条件分布应聚合为任何有效人口划分下的总体边际分布。本研究探讨LLM估计在多大程度上遵循这一自一致性原则。我们使用二叉树作为评估框架，递归地将人口划分为越来越精细的子群体。然后，我们通过上下文中的口头化子群体描述提示LLM，将所得估计值聚合回总体估计值，并在不同粒度的划分间进行比较。在多个问题领域和前沿模型上应用此协议，我们发现普遍存在基本一致性属性的违反。对人格提示的深入研究揭示了一种我们称之为“宏观谬误”的模式：从更精细子群体响应重建的估计值，往往比直接的总体估计值更贴近人类参考数据。该效应在树结构和估计任务变化时依然存在，可通过隐式提示部分恢复。这些发现共同表明，模型虽具备相关子群体知识，但无法可靠地将其传播至聚合估计值。这一差距确立了统计自一致性作为评估LLM的一种未饱和、无需参考的标准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the assumption that in-context learning in LLMs reflects conditional inference, this study tests whether LLM outputs satisfy the law of total probability—a core probabilistic identity. Using binary trees to recursively partition populations and prompting models with subpopulation descriptions, the authors aggregate model estimates across partitions and compare them to population-level ground truth. Results show widespread violations of self-consistency across models and tasks, with a notable pattern called the macro fallacy: finer-grained subpopulation estimates often align better with human data than direct population estimates. This suggests models retain subpopulation knowledge but fail to reliably aggregate it, revealing statistical self-consistency as an underutilized, reference-free evaluation criterion.
该研究基于语言模型的上下文学习可视为条件推理的假设，检验其是否满足概率论中的全概率定律。通过二叉树递归划分群体，并对子群体描述进行提示，再将模型输出聚合回群体层面进行比较，结果发现模型普遍存在自洽性缺失。尤其在“宏观谬误”现象中，细粒度子群体估计往往比直接群体估计更贴近人类参考数据，且该现象在不同树结构和任务中普遍存在，部分可通过隐式提示缓解。这表明模型虽掌握子群体知识，但难以可靠聚合，揭示统计自洽性作为无参考基准的评估潜力。
RoboTTT: Context Scaling for Robot Policies
Authors: Yunfan Jiang, Yevgen Chebotar, Ruijie Zheng, Fengyuan Hu, Yunhao Ge, Jimmy Wu, Tianyuan Dai, Scott Reed, Li Fei-Fei, Yuke Zhu, Linxi "Jim" Fan
First: 2026-07-16T17:59:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:59:06+00:00
Comments: Project website: http://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent robot foundation models operate with single-step or short-history visuomotor context. We introduce Test-Time-Training Robot Policies (RoboTTT), a robot model and training recipe that scale visuomotor context to 8K timesteps, three orders of magnitude beyond state-of-the-art policies, without growing inference latency. At this context length, we unlock new robot capabilities: one-shot in-context imitation from human video demonstrations, on-the-fly policy improvement, robustness to perturbations, and stronger performance on multi-stage, long-horizon tasks. We also observe, for the first time, steady gains in closed-loop performance as pretraining context length scales. At its core, RoboTTT integrates Test-Time Training into robot foundation models such as Vision-Language-Action policies, yielding a sequence model whose recurrent state consists of fast weights, parameters updated by gradient descent during both training and inference, compressing histories into weight space and retrieving contextual information for long-context conditioning. To scale training context length, the recipe combines sequence action forcing with truncated backpropagation through time. On challenging real-robot manipulation tasks, RoboTTT improves overall performance by 87% over the single-step context baseline and fully completes a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task, which no baseline ever does. RoboTTT trained with 8K-timestep context outperforms the same model pretrained with 1K timesteps by 62%, suggesting context length as a new scaling axis for robot foundation models. Videos are available at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/
中文标题/摘要
标题：RoboTTT：机器人策略的上下文扩展
近期的机器人基础模型仅使用单步或短历史视觉运动上下文。我们引入了测试时训练机器人策略（RoboTTT），一种可将视觉运动上下文扩展至8K步的机器人模型与训练方案，其性能比现有最先进策略高出三个数量级，且不增加推理延迟。在该上下文长度下，我们解锁了新的机器人能力：从人类视频演示中实现单次上下文模仿、实时策略改进、对扰动的鲁棒性，以及在多阶段、长时域任务中的更强表现。我们首次观察到，随着预训练上下文长度的增加，闭环性能持续提升。RoboTTT的核心是将测试时训练集成到如视觉-语言-动作策略等机器人基础模型中，构建一个序列模型，其递归状态由快速权重构成，这些权重在训练和推理过程中通过梯度下降更新，将历史压缩至权重空间，并在长上下文条件下检索相关信息。为扩展训练上下文长度，该方案结合序列动作强制与截断时间反向传播。在具有挑战性的真实机器人操作任务中，RoboTTT相较单步上下文基线整体性能提升87%，并首次完整完成耗时五分钟、包含十个阶段的装配任务。使用8K步上下文预训练的RoboTTT模型，其性能比使用1K步上下文预训练的同类模型高出62%，表明上下文长度是机器人基础模型的新扩展维度。视频见：https://research.nvidia.com/labs/gear/robottt/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance robot policy performance through longer visuomotor context, RoboTTT introduces a training framework that scales context length to 8K timesteps—three orders of magnitude beyond prior methods—without increasing inference latency. It achieves this by integrating Test-Time Training into vision-language-action models, using fast weights updated via gradient descent during both training and inference to compress historical context into parameter space. Experimental results show RoboTTT enables one-shot imitation, real-time policy improvement, and robustness to perturbations, while outperforming single-step baselines by 87% on real-robot manipulation tasks and fully completing a five-minute, ten-stage assembly task. Crucially, models pretrained with 8K context outperform those with 1K context by 62%, demonstrating context length as a critical scaling factor for robot foundation models.
为提升机器人策略性能，RoboTTT提出一种训练框架，将视觉运动上下文长度扩展至8K步（比现有方法高三个数量级），同时不增加推理延迟。其核心方法是将测试时训练集成到视觉-语言-动作模型中，通过在训练和推理时动态更新的“快权重”压缩历史上下文至参数空间。实验表明，该方法支持从人类视频中一次性模仿、实时策略优化和对扰动的鲁棒性，整体性能较单步上下文基线提升87%，并首次成功完成五分钟十阶段装配任务。更重要的是，使用8K上下文预训练的模型比1K上下文模型性能高出62%，证明上下文长度是机器人基础模型的重要扩展维度。
MeanFlowNFT: Bringing Forward-Process RL to Average-Velocity Generators
Authors: Yushi Huang, Xiangxin Zhou, Jun Zhang, Liefeng Bo, Tianyu Pang
First: 2026-07-16T17:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:59:02+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://harahan.github.io/meanflownft-project-page/, GitHub: https://github.com/Harahan/MeanFlowNFT, Hugging Face: https://huggingface.co/Harahan/MeanFlowNFT
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
MeanFlow generators achieve fast few-step sampling by predicting average velocities over time intervals, making them attractive for efficient generation. Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a powerful way to align diffusion and flow models with human preferences and task-specific objectives. In particular, DiffusionNFT offers an efficient forward-process RL framework that does not require reverse-process trajectories or likelihood estimation. However, applying such RL methods to MeanFlow remains underexplored. DiffusionNFT optimizes instantaneous velocities, whereas MeanFlow samples with average velocities. To bridge this gap, we introduce MeanFlowNFT. Inspired by the MeanFlow identity, which bridges average and instantaneous velocities, we construct an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor. We apply the DiffusionNFT objective to this predictor, making reward optimization well-defined for MeanFlow. Sampling remains based on the average velocity, preserving MeanFlow's fast few-step generation. We further prove that MeanFlowNFT inherits DiffusionNFT's strict policy-improvement guarantee. Experiments on image and video generation show that MeanFlowNFT consistently improves baselines. Moreover, it outperforms prior state-of-the-art RL-tuned few-step generators on most metrics ($6$ of $8$ on SD3.5-M), and can even surpass multi-step RL-tuned diffusion while using only a few sampling steps. For instance, on Wan 2.1, $4$-step MeanFlowNFT reaches a VBench score of $84.33$, surpassing $50$-step LongCat-Video RL ($82.57$).
中文标题/摘要
标题：MeanFlowNFT：将前向过程强化学习引入平均速度生成器
MeanFlow生成器通过预测时间区间内的平均速度实现快速几步采样，适合高效生成。强化学习（RL）已成为对扩散和流模型进行人类偏好与任务目标对齐的强大方法。特别是DiffusionNFT提供了一种无需反向过程轨迹或似然估计的高效前向过程RL框架。然而，将此类RL方法应用于MeanFlow仍属探索不足。DiffusionNFT优化瞬时速度，而MeanFlow采样基于平均速度。为弥合此差距，我们提出MeanFlowNFT。受MeanFlow恒等式启发（该恒等式连接平均与瞬时速度），我们构建了一个诱导瞬时速度预测器，并将其应用于DiffusionNFT目标函数，使MeanFlow的奖励优化定义明确。采样仍基于平均速度，保留MeanFlow快速几步生成特性。我们进一步证明MeanFlowNFT继承了DiffusionNFT严格的策略改进保证。在图像与视频生成实验中，MeanFlowNFT持续优于基线方法。此外，它在多数指标上（SD3.5-M的8项中有6项）超越现有最先进的RL调优几步生成器，甚至能在仅用几步采样时超越多步RL调优扩散模型。例如，在Wan 2.1上，4步MeanFlowNFT达到VBench分数84.33，超越50步LongCat-Video RL的82.57。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the efficiency of MeanFlow generators in few-step sampling and the success of RL-based DiffusionNFT in aligning models with preferences, this work introduces MeanFlowNFT to extend RL to MeanFlow’s average-velocity framework. The method constructs an induced instantaneous-velocity predictor based on MeanFlow’s identity, then applies the DiffusionNFT objective to it, enabling reward optimization while preserving fast sampling via average velocities. Experiments show MeanFlowNFT consistently outperforms baselines and surpasses prior state-of-the-art few-step RL generators on 6 of 8 metrics on SD3.5-M, even beating multi-step RL-tuned diffusion models with fewer steps—e.g., 4-step MeanFlowNFT achieves 84.33 VBench on Wan 2.1, outperforming 50-step LongCat-Video RL at 82.57.
为提升平均速度生成器的效率并结合强化学习的优势，本文提出MeanFlowNFT，将DiffusionNFT的强化学习框架适配至MeanFlow的平均速度采样机制。方法基于MeanFlow速度恒等式构建诱导瞬时速度预测器，并将其与DiffusionNFT目标函数结合，确保奖励优化有效，同时保留平均速度采样带来的快速生成能力。实验表明，MeanFlowNFT在多个基准上持续超越基线模型，在SD3.5-M的8项指标中6项领先，且在仅用4步采样的Wan 2.1数据集上，其VBench得分84.33超越了需50步采样的LongCat-Video RL模型（82.57）
SciDiagramEdit: Learning to Edit Scientific Diagrams from Paper Revisions
Authors: Yasheng Sun, Zezi Zeng, Yifan Yang, Chong Luo, Wenyi Wang, Ziwei Liu, Jürgen Schmidhuber
First: 2026-07-16T17:58:36+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:58:36+00:00
Comments: 20 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Editing the figures in a research paper is a routine and time-consuming part of everyday research practice: authors relabel components, rearrange panels, and restyle visuals as they revise their manuscripts. Automating this editing workflow under a natural-language instruction, however, is challenging, because a scientific figure is a dense infographic in which heterogeneous visual elements such as schematics, plots, photos, captions, and arrows are composed under a tight visual grammar to advance a specific argument. To address this, we present SciDiagramEdit, a benchmark and skill-evolution framework that learns from natural paper revisions and operates on the figure's editable vector source, where users can inspect and co-edit individual primitives alongside the agent. Our benchmark mines before/after figure pairs from arXiv version histories, each grounded in the authors' own revision intent. To accommodate the diversity of editing instructions, we adopt agentic learning via skill evolution: an agentic proposer continually refines the agent's skill specification from execution traces over multiple epochs. The resulting skill progressively lifts edit accuracy on a held-out validation set, providing evidence that natural paper revisions are an effective training signal for instruction-driven figure editing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SciDiagramEdit：从论文修订中学习科学图表编辑
在研究论文中编辑图表是日常科研实践中的常规且耗时的部分：作者在修订稿件时会重命名组件、调整面板布局并重新设计视觉元素。然而，要在自然语言指令下自动化这一编辑流程颇具挑战，因为科学图表是一种密集的信息图，其中包含示意图、图表、照片、图注和箭头等异质视觉元素，这些元素在严格的视觉语法下组合，以推进特定论点。为解决此问题，我们提出了SciDiagramEdit，一个基准测试与技能演化框架，该框架从自然论文修订中学习，并在图表的可编辑矢量源上运行，用户可与代理共同检查和编辑单个图元。我们的基准从arXiv版本历史中挖掘前后图表对，每对均基于作者自身的修订意图。为适应多样化的编辑指令，我们采用基于代理的技能演化学习：代理提案者持续从多个训练周期的执行轨迹中优化代理的技能规范。最终技能在保留验证集上的编辑准确率逐步提升，表明自然论文修订是指导式图表编辑的有效训练信号。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate the tedious task of editing scientific figures during manuscript revisions, the authors introduce SciDiagramEdit, a framework that learns from real paper revisions to perform visual edits under natural language instructions. The method operates on editable vector sources of figures, enabling human-in-the-loop co-editing, and employs agentic skill evolution: an agent iteratively refines its editing capabilities by learning from execution traces across multiple training epochs. Experimental results show that this approach progressively improves edit accuracy on a held-out validation set, demonstrating that natural paper revisions serve as a strong and effective training signal for instruction-based figure editing.
为解决科研论文修订中频繁且耗时的图表编辑问题，作者提出了SciDiagramEdit框架，该框架从真实论文修订中学习，并基于可编辑的矢量图源进行操作。方法采用代理技能演化机制，通过多轮执行轨迹不断优化编辑能力。实验表明，该方法在验证集上的编辑准确率随训练逐步提升，证明真实论文修订是指导式图表编辑的有效训练信号。
Online Neural Space Time Memory for Dynamic Novel View Synthesis
Authors: Baback Elmieh, Lynn Tsai, Zeman Li, Srinivas Kaza, Tiancheng Sun, Gabor Csapo, Ali Behrouz, Yuan Deng, Stephen Lombardi, Steven M. Seitz, Xuan Luo
First: 2026-07-16T17:58:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:58:18+00:00
Comments: 15 pages. Preprint. Project page with demos and video results: https://nst-mem.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Online novel view synthesis from multi-view streaming videos faces a fundamental trade-off: maintaining a persistent, long-horizon memory to reconstruct temporarily occluded regions while operating under strict real-time constraints. While Test-Time Training (TTT) offers a powerful memory mechanism, standard models mandate gradient-based memory updates at every frame to adapt to the changing motion in dynamic scenes. The computational cost of heavy memory updates precludes real-time application and can lead to instability over long contexts. Given that memory updates are more demanding than memory application and video content is largely redundant, we propose to decouple the frequencies of these two processes. Our approach performs periodic memory updates while applying the memory on a per-frame basis, using cross-view attention to manage deformations between the prior memory state and the current frame. To lock in the historical context, we introduce two critical mechanisms: an auxiliary Memory Loss that forces persistent internalization of the scene, and a Memory Caching strategy that regularizes active weights against catastrophic drift. Our method demonstrates real-time, state-of-the-art performance on scenes with dynamic human motion as well as minute-scale online memorization.

标题：面向动态新视角合成的在线神经时空记忆
从多视角流媒体视频在线生成新视角面临根本性权衡：需维持持久、长时程记忆以重建暂时遮挡区域，同时满足严格的实时约束。虽然测试时训练（TTT）提供强大记忆机制，但标准模型要求每帧基于梯度的内存更新以适应动态场景中的运动变化。沉重的内存更新计算成本阻碍实时应用，并可能在长上下文下导致不稳定。鉴于内存更新比内存应用更耗资源，且视频内容高度冗余，我们提出将二者频率解耦：定期更新内存，每帧应用内存，利用跨视角注意力管理前后记忆状态与当前帧间的形变。为锁定历史上下文，我们引入两项关键机制：辅助记忆损失，强制场景持久内化；记忆缓存策略，抑制活跃权重的灾难性漂移。该方法在含动态人体运动的场景中实现实时、最先进性能，并支持分钟级在线记忆。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to synthesize novel views in real time from streaming videos while preserving long-term memory of occluded regions, this work addresses the computational burden of frequent gradient-based memory updates in Test-Time Training. It proposes decoupling memory updates from per-frame application, performing periodic updates while using cross-view attention to handle motion deformations. Two key mechanisms—Memory Loss to enforce scene persistence and Memory Caching to prevent weight drift—ensure stable long-horizon memory. Experiments show the method achieves real-time performance and state-of-the-art results on dynamic human motion scenes, with fine-grained online memorization capabilities.
为在流媒体视频中实时合成新视角并保持对遮挡区域的长期记忆，该研究针对Test-Time Training中频繁梯度更新带来的计算负担提出解决方案。方法将记忆更新与每帧应用解耦，仅周期性更新记忆，同时用跨视角注意力处理运动形变。引入记忆损失机制强制场景持久化，记忆缓存策略防止权重漂移，确保长期记忆稳定。实验表明该方法在动态人体运动场景中实现实时性能，并达到当前最优效果，具备分钟级在线记忆能力。
Motion-Conditioned Multi-View Fusion for Myocardial Infarction Localization from Echocardiography
Authors: Guang Yang, Wentian Xu, Siyu Wang, Betty Raman, Lei Li, Vicente Grau
First: 2026-07-16T17:56:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:56:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Myocardial infarction (MI) remains a leading cause of mortality worldwide. Echocardiography (Echo) is a widely available modality for MI assessment, where regional wall motion abnormality is a key indicator. Prior learning based methods for myocardial motion analysis often use handcrafted descriptors or densely supervised estimation, but the need for extensive annotation limits applicability. Foundation models have recently improved vision-based Echo analysis; however, most methods operate on single views and segment-level localization remains unreliable under view-dependent ambiguity, especially in apical views. To address this, we propose MCF-Net, a novel motion-guided multi-view fusion framework that fuses myocardial motion cues with foundation model representations to localize infarction. Visual features are extracted using EchoPrime, a pretrained Echo foundation model shared across dual views. Cardiac motion is modeled with extremely sparse supervision: a single annotated template frame is transferred across videos to initialize point tracking, avoiding dense labels. Motion-derived segment-aware soft masks provide coarse spatial priors that selectively enhance features for challenging myocardial segments. A motion-conditioned fusion mechanism then integrates motion and vision across views, refining predictions without overriding strong appearance cues. On segment-level MI localization, MCF-Net achieves 72.4\% F1 and 84.9\% accuracy, outperforming state-of-the-art motion-only, vision-only, and fusion baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于运动条件的多视角融合用于超声心动图中的心肌梗死定位
心肌梗死（MI）仍是全球主要致死原因。超声心动图（Echo）是评估MI的常用手段，区域壁运动异常是关键指标。以往基于学习的方法常依赖手工特征或密集监督估计，但需大量标注限制了应用。基础模型近期提升了基于视觉的Echo分析，但多数方法仅处理单视角，且在视图依赖性模糊下（尤其在心尖视角）的分段定位仍不可靠。为此，我们提出MCF-Net，一种新颖的运动引导多视角融合框架，融合心肌运动线索与基础模型表征以定位梗死。视觉特征通过预训练的Echo基础模型EchoPrime提取，该模型在双视角间共享。心脏运动采用极稀疏监督建模：仅用单帧标注模板初始化点追踪，避免密集标注。运动衍生的分段感知软掩码提供粗略空间先验，选择性增强难处理心肌段特征。运动条件融合机制随后跨视角整合运动与视觉信息，优化预测而不覆盖强外观线索。在分段级MI定位中，MCF-Net实现F1值72.4%、准确率84.9%，优于现有运动仅、视觉仅及融合基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate myocardial infarction localization in echocardiography despite limited annotated data and view-dependent ambiguity, the authors propose MCF-Net, a motion-conditioned multi-view fusion framework. It leverages EchoPrime, a pretrained foundation model, to extract visual features across dual views, while modeling cardiac motion with sparse supervision via point tracking from a single annotated frame. Motion-derived soft masks guide feature enhancement for challenging myocardial regions, and a motion-conditioned fusion mechanism integrates motion and vision cues without suppressing strong appearance signals. Experiments show MCF-Net achieves 72.4% F1 and 84.9% accuracy on segment-level MI localization, outperforming motion-only, vision-only, and existing fusion methods.
为解决超声心动图中因视图依赖性模糊和标注稀缺导致的梗死区域定位不准确问题，作者提出MCF-Net框架，通过融合基础模型视觉特征与运动线索实现多视角联合定位。该方法利用预训练的EchoPrime模型在双视角间共享视觉特征，并仅用单帧标注模板初始化运动追踪，避免密集标注。运动衍生的软掩码用于增强关键心肌段特征，而运动条件融合机制在多视角间整合信息而不抑制强视觉线索。实验表明，MCF-Net在段级梗死定位任务中F1达72.4%，准确率84.9%，优于现有运动主导、视觉主导及融合类基线方法。
Pretraining Data Can Be Poisoned through Computational Propaganda
Authors: Victoria Graf, Hannaneh Hajishirzi, Noah A. Smith, David Kohlbrenner, Kyle Lo
First: 2026-07-16T17:56:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:56:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Poisoning pretraining data can introduce harmful behaviors to LMs that are difficult to detect and mitigate. Prior work on poisoning pretraining data has largely exploited established data sources such as Wikipedia, which do not represent the large scale and heterogeneity typical of pretraining corpora, and has ignored the interaction between poisoned data and data curation pipelines. We demonstrate that poisoning attacks on pretraining data are feasible beyond this limited setting through an existing web-scale content injection mechanism: public discussion interfaces. Additionally, to measure whether malicious content is included after web crawling and data curation, we introduce HalfLife, a novel analysis for estimating adversarial content inclusion in web-crawl based LM training data. We use HalfLife to explore the feasibility of poisoning pretraining corpora at web scale through open discussion interfaces. Our analysis demonstrates the importance of estimating whether poison injections are included in pretraining data, and establishes third-party webpage content as a possible vector for attacking language model pretraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过计算宣传可对预训练数据进行投毒
对预训练数据进行投毒可向语言模型引入难以检测和缓解的有害行为。此前针对预训练数据投毒的研究主要利用如维基百科等已建立的数据源，这些数据源无法代表预训练语料库通常所具有的大规模和异质性，且忽略了投毒数据与数据整理流程之间的交互作用。我们证明，通过现有网络规模内容注入机制——公共讨论界面，可在超出该有限场景之外实施预训练数据投毒攻击。此外，为衡量网络爬取和数据整理后是否包含恶意内容，我们引入HalfLife，一种用于估计基于网络爬取的大型语言模型训练数据中对抗性内容包含量的新分析方法。我们利用HalfLife探索通过公开讨论界面在互联网规模上投毒预训练语料库的可行性。我们的分析表明，评估投毒内容是否被纳入预训练数据至关重要，并确立第三方网页内容作为攻击语言模型预训练的潜在途径。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that pretraining data can be poisoned to induce harmful behaviors in language models, this study investigates whether malicious content can be injected into large-scale pretraining corpora via public web discussion interfaces, which are commonly used for content generation. The authors introduce HalfLife, a novel metric to estimate the inclusion of adversarial content after web crawling and data curation, and apply it to assess the feasibility of poisoning attacks at web scale. Their experiments show that such poisoning is indeed possible and that existing curation pipelines may not effectively filter out malicious content, highlighting the need for better detection mechanisms and raising concerns about third-party webpage content as a potential attack vector.
鉴于有害行为可能通过污染预训练数据注入大语言模型，本研究探讨了是否可通过网络规模的公开讨论界面实现此类污染，而不仅限于如维基百科等传统数据源。作者提出了HalfLife这一新指标，用于评估爬取网页数据中是否混入对抗性内容，并将其应用于评估通过公开讨论平台污染预训练语料的可行性。分析结果表明，恶意内容可通过这些界面注入预训练数据，且HalfLife能有效检测此类污染，证实第三方网页内容可成为攻击语言模型训练的可行途径。
SceneBind: Binding What and Where Across Vision, Audio and Language
Authors: Mingfei Chen, Zijun Cui, Ruoke Zhang, Hyeonggon Ryu, Eli Shlizerman
First: 2026-07-16T17:55:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:55:15+00:00
Comments: Project website: https://scenebind.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present SceneBind, an omni-modal representation of realistic scenes with joint semantic and 3D spatial understanding across vision, audio and language. Existing omni-modal encoders excel at instance-level semantics (i.e., what is present), but often lack explicit spatial structure (i.e., where it is). SceneBind addresses this gap by representing each scene as a semantic-spatial entity, combining a global semantic embedding with object-centric semantic-spatial slots. This representation explicitly captures object-level semantics, spatial attributes, and uncertainty. We further propose SceneBind Matching, a semantic-spatial matching scheme that integrates global scene similarity with object alignment, supporting cross-modal scene retrieval and object grounding. To train and evaluate SceneBind, we curate a novel real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured semantic and spatial annotations, and propose a training protocol for aligning semantic and spatial signals across modalities. SceneBind is compatible with large-scale pretrained semantic encoders, adds lightweight spatial modeling with only a few additional tokens. It achieves state-of-the-art scene and spatial retrieval while enabling strong zero-shot transfer to downstream tasks such as audio-visual localization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SceneBind：跨视觉、音频与语言的“什么”与“哪里”绑定
我们提出了SceneBind，一种融合视觉、音频与语言的全模态真实场景表示，具备联合语义与三维空间理解能力。现有全模态编码器擅长实例级语义（即“有什么”），但常缺乏显式空间结构（即“在哪里”）。SceneBind通过将每个场景表示为语义-空间实体，结合全局语义嵌入与以物体为中心的语义-空间槽位，显式捕捉物体级语义、空间属性与不确定性。我们进一步提出SceneBind匹配机制，融合全局场景相似性与物体对齐，支持跨模态场景检索与物体定位。为训练与评估SceneBind，我们构建了一个具有结构化语义与空间标注的新型真实世界双耳音频-视觉数据集，并提出跨模态语义与空间信号对齐的训练协议。SceneBind兼容大规模预训练语义编码器，仅需少量额外token即可加入轻量级空间建模，在场景与空间检索上达到最先进水平，同时支持音频-视觉定位等下游任务的零样本迁移。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to unify semantic and spatial understanding across vision, audio, and language, SceneBind introduces a novel omni-modal representation that models scenes as semantic-spatial entities, combining global semantic embeddings with object-centric slots to capture both what is present and where it is located. The method trains on a newly curated real-world binaural audio-visual dataset with structured annotations, using a lightweight spatial modeling module that integrates seamlessly with pretrained semantic encoders. Experimental results show SceneBind achieves state-of-the-art performance in scene and spatial retrieval tasks and enables strong zero-shot transfer to downstream applications like audio-visual localization.
为统一视觉、音频和语言模态中的语义与空间理解，SceneBind提出一种新的多模态表示方法，显式编码场景中‘是什么’与‘在哪里’。该方法通过结合全局语义嵌入与对象中心的语义-空间槽，实现细粒度的对象语义与空间属性建模，并支持不确定性表达。其训练基于一个新构建的真实世界双耳音频-视觉数据集，且空间建模模块轻量，可兼容预训练语义编码器。实验表明，SceneBind在场景与空间检索任务上达到当前最优水平，并在音频-视觉定位等下游任务中实现强零样本迁移能力。
Beyond Success Rate: Cost-Aware Evaluation of Offensive and Defensive Security Agents
Authors: Paul Kassianik, Blaine Nelson, Yaron Singer
First: 2026-07-16T17:54:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:54:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Security-agent evaluations commonly measure peak offensive capability under generous inference budgets, emphasizing vulnerability discovery, exploit development, penetration testing, and CTF completion. Such measurements are useful but incomplete: in operational security, every reasoning step, tool call, telemetry query, and enrichment request consumes budget. We evaluate language-model security agents through this cost-success lens on offensive Cybench challenges and defensive Splunk BOTS v1 investigation challenges. Instead of reporting only best-case success, we compare models at fixed cost levels and decompose performance by inference spend and tool spend. Our results show distinct scalingregimes for red- and blue-team tasks. Offensive CTF performance improves with additional test-time compute, and scaled open-weight models can approach frontier proprietary systems while remaining cost-competitive. Defensive SOC investigation does not scale in the same way: success depends more heavily on disciplined tool use, telemetry navigation, and selective enrichment than on raw reasoning budget alone. We argue that security-agent benchmarks should measure economic efficiency and operational fit alongside task success. Cost-aware, SOC-native evaluations provide a clearer picture of which models are practically useful today and where defensive agents still need to improve. We present an interactive website with our results https://evals.frontier.security.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越成功率：面向攻击与防御安全代理的成本感知评估
安全代理评估通常在宽松的推理预算下测量其峰值攻击能力，强调漏洞发现、漏洞利用开发、渗透测试和CTF完成。此类测量虽有用但不完整：在实际安全运营中，每一步推理、工具调用、遥测查询和丰富请求均消耗预算。我们通过成本-成功率视角，在攻击性Cybench挑战和防御性Splunk BOTS v1调查挑战中评估语言模型安全代理。我们不仅报告最佳情况下的成功率，还比较固定成本水平下的模型表现，并按推理开销和工具开销分解性能。结果表明，红队与蓝队任务具有不同的扩展规律：攻击性CTF表现随测试时计算量增加而提升，扩展后的开源模型可接近前沿专有系统，同时保持成本竞争力。防御性SOC调查则不遵循相同扩展规律：成功更依赖于工具的规范使用、遥测导航和选择性丰富，而非单纯推理预算。我们认为，安全代理基准应同时衡量经济效率与实际运营适配度。成本感知、原生SOC评估能更清晰地揭示当前哪些模型实用，以及防御代理仍需改进之处。我们提供交互式网站展示结果：https://evals.frontier.security。

Results show offensive agents scale with additional compute, with open-weight models approaching proprietary systems while staying cost-effective, whereas defensive agents rely more on disciplined tool usage and telemetry navigation than raw reasoning power, indicating a need for more efficient and context-aware defensive models.
该论文指出传统安全代理评估仅关注成功率而忽略实际成本，提出一种兼顾推理与工具使用开销的评估框架，应用于进攻性Cybench和防御性Splunk BOTS v1任务。实验表明，进攻型代理在增加计算资源后性能显著提升，可逼近前沿专有模型且保持成本竞争力；而防御型代理更依赖工具使用规范性、日志导航与选择性数据增强，其性能随计算资源增加提升有限。研究呼吁评估基准应同时衡量经济效率与实际操作适配度，以更准确识别实用模型并明确防御代理需改进的方向。
Decoding Market Emotion from Blockchain Activity: A Data-Driven Sentiment Classifier
Authors: Arthur G. Bubolz, Abreu Quevedo, Giancarlo Lucca, Rafael A. Berri, Eduardo Borges, Bruno L. Dalmazo
First: 2026-07-16T17:52:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:52:26+00:00
Comments: This manuscript has been accepted for presentation at the IEEE International Symposium on Computers and Communications (ISCC 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The growing use of Bitcoin as a decentralized digital asset and investment tool has sparked strong interest in understanding its market behavior. This study presents a new approach to analyze Bitcoin market sentiment by combining on-chain and financial data with social media posts. Unlike models that aim to predict prices, this work focuses on explaining market sentiment using blockchain transactions, historical price data of Bitcoin, and daily Twitter sentiment classifications. The method merges sentiment trends with on-chain and financial metrics, normalized into a dataset for detailed market analysis. Multiple machine learning models were tested using cross-validation, with Gradient Boosting (XGBoost) emerging as the most reliable model for classifying sentiment, achieving an average F1-score of about 0.84. SHAP (SHapley Additive exPlanations), a game theory-based method for model interpretability, was used to quantify the contribution of on-chain features to the model's predictions, improving transparency. The results indicate that this data combination yields meaningful predictive signals and insights, supporting data-driven cryptocurrency analysis and future improvements with deep learning.

SearchOS-V1: Towards Robust Open-Domain Information-Seeking Agent Collaboration
Authors: Yuyao Zhang, Junjie Gao, Zhengxian Wu, Jiaming Fan, Jin Zhang, Shihan Ma, Yao Yao, Weiran Qi, Chuyan Jin, Guiyu Ma, Xingzhong Xu, Kai Yang, Ji-Rong Wen, Zhicheng Dou
First: 2026-07-16T17:51:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:51:23+00:00
Comments: Code is available at https://github.com/antins-labs/SearchOS
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Recent advances in Tool-Integrated Large Language Models have made web search a core capability of information-seeking agents. However, as interaction histories grow, agents increasingly struggle to track task progress. When search attempts fail to yield useful evidence, current single- and multi-agent systems can become trapped in repetitive loops, wasting search budgets and ultimately compromising the quality and completeness of the final output. We introduce SearchOS, a system-level multi-agent framework that turns fragile, implicit search progress into explicit, persistent, and shared state. First, we formulate open-domain information seeking as relational schema completion with grounded citations, where agents discover entities, populate attributes across linked tables, and anchor each value to source evidence. Then we design Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM), which externalizes the evolving state into Frontier Task, an Evidence Graph, a Coverage Map, and Failure Memory. Built on SOCM, SearchOS applies a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism that overlaps the execution of sub-agents and continuously refills freed slots with tasks targeting unresolved coverage gaps to improve utilization and throughput. To schedule and control the execution of search agents, SearchOS introduces a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts model and tool interactions to record grounded evidence and react to stalls or budget exhaustion, and provides a reusable hierarchical skill system comprising strategy and access skills to augment the agents' search process and avoid repeating failed search patterns across runs. On WideSearch and GISA, SearchOS leads all metrics among the evaluated single- and multi-agent baselines, paving the way toward robust information-seeking collaboration.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SearchOS-V1：迈向稳健的开放域信息检索代理协作
工具集成大语言模型的最新进展使网络搜索成为信息检索代理的核心能力。然而，随着交互历史增长，代理越来越难以跟踪任务进度。当搜索尝试未能产生有用证据时，当前的单代理或多代理系统可能陷入重复循环，浪费搜索预算，最终影响最终输出的质量和完整性。我们提出了SearchOS，一个系统级多代理框架，将脆弱且隐式的搜索进度转化为显式、持久且共享的状态。首先，我们将开放域信息检索形式化为带锚定引用的关系模式补全，其中代理发现实体、在关联表中填充属性，并将每个值锚定到源证据。接着，我们设计了面向搜索的上下文管理（SOCM），将演进状态外部化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆。基于SOCM，SearchOS采用流水线并行调度机制，重叠子代理执行，并持续用针对未解决覆盖缺口的任务填充空闲槽位，以提升利用率和吞吐量。为调度和控制搜索代理的执行，SearchOS引入了搜索工具中间件框架，拦截模型与工具交互以记录锚定证据、响应停滞或预算耗尽，并提供可复用的分层技能系统（包含策略与访问技能），以增强代理的搜索过程并避免跨运行重复失败的搜索模式。在WideSearch和GISA数据集上，SearchOS在所有评估的单代理和多代理基线中均领先，为稳健的信息检索协作铺平道路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current agents in tracking search progress and avoiding repetitive failures, SearchOS introduces a system-level multi-agent framework that explicitly manages search state through a relational schema completion approach grounded in citations. It employs Search-Oriented Context Management (SOCM) to externalize evolving state into structured components like Frontier Task, Evidence Graph, Coverage Map, and Failure Memory, enabling persistent, shared progress tracking. Coupled with a pipeline-parallel scheduling mechanism and a Search Tool Middleware Harness that intercepts tool interactions and enforces reusable hierarchical skills, SearchOS dynamically refills search slots and avoids redundant patterns. Experiments on WideSearch and GISA show SearchOS outperforms all baseline single- and multi-agent systems across key metrics, demonstrating robust, scalable information-seeking collaboration.
为解决现有信息检索代理在追踪任务进度和避免重复失败方面的低效问题，SearchOS提出一种系统级多代理框架，通过基于引证的关联模式补全方法显式管理搜索状态。其核心是Search-Oriented Context Management（SOCM），将动态状态外化为前沿任务、证据图、覆盖图和失败记忆等结构化组件，实现持久共享的进度追踪。结合流水线并行调度机制与拦截工具交互的搜索中间件，该框架通过分层技能系统避免重复搜索，最终在WideSearch和GISA数据集上全面超越所有单代理与多代理基线模型。
HoloGeo: Mitigating Landmark Bias in Geo-localization via Evidence-Driven Reasoning
Authors: Pengcheng Zhou, Xuanyu Liu, Yanchen Yin, Bobo Li, Shengqiong Wu, Mong-Li Lee, Wynne Hsu
First: 2026-07-16T17:50:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:50:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Vision-Language Models (VLMs) have significantly improved image geo-localization, yet existing models remain susceptible to landmark bias, causing them to overlook geographical cues or form spurious correlations, ultimately resulting in inaccurate localization. To systematically investigate this issue, we first design two quantitative metrics, Bias Intensity (BI) and Bias Harmfulness (BH), to characterize the impact of landmarks exerted on model reasoning, and establish a comprehensive benchmark, LandmarkBias-3K. To mitigate landmark bias, we further propose an evidence-driven reasoning framework, HoloGeo, to improve the reliability of geo-localization. HoloGeo is supported by a high-quality dataset, BF-30k, annotated with structured multi-evidence bias-free reasoning chains. By incorporating multi-dimensional rewards, HoloGeo explicitly encourages balanced attention over diverse visual cues and achieves evidence-driven joint reasoning. Extensive experiments demonstrate that HoloGeo not only maintains excellent performance on IM2GPS3K and YFCC4k but also significantly outperforms existing open-source VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, validating its effectiveness for robust geospatial reasoning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HoloGeo：通过证据驱动推理缓解地理定位中的地标偏差
视觉-语言模型（VLMs）的最新进展显著提升了图像地理定位性能，但现有模型仍易受地标偏差影响，导致忽略地理线索或形成虚假关联，最终造成定位不准确。为系统研究该问题，我们首先设计了两个量化指标——偏差强度（BI）和偏差有害性（BH），用于刻画地标对模型推理的影响，并构建了全面基准LandmarkBias-3K。为缓解地标偏差，我们进一步提出证据驱动推理框架HoloGeo，以提升地理定位的可靠性。HoloGeo依托高质量数据集BF-30k，该数据集标注了结构化、多证据且无偏差的推理链。通过引入多维奖励机制，HoloGeo明确鼓励对多样化视觉线索的均衡关注，实现证据驱动的联合推理。大量实验表明，HoloGeo不仅在IM2GPS3K和YFCC4k上保持优异性能，还在LandmarkBias-3K上显著优于现有开源VLMs，验证了其在鲁棒地理推理中的有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the vulnerability of Vision-Language Models to landmark bias that leads to inaccurate geo-localization, this work introduces two metrics—Bias Intensity and Bias Harmfulness—to quantify landmark influence and establishes LandmarkBias-3K as a benchmark. It proposes HoloGeo, an evidence-driven reasoning framework trained on the BF-30k dataset with structured, bias-free reasoning chains, which uses multi-dimensional rewards to enforce balanced attention across visual cues. Experiments show HoloGeo maintains strong performance on standard datasets while significantly outperforming existing VLMs on LandmarkBias-3K, confirming its effectiveness in mitigating landmark bias.
为解决视觉语言模型在地理定位中因地标偏差导致的定位不准问题，该研究提出两个量化指标——偏倚强度与偏倚危害度，并构建LandmarkBias-3K基准数据集。其核心方法HoloGeo基于BF-30k数据集（含结构化无偏推理链）与多维奖励机制，引导模型在视觉线索间实现均衡关注。实验表明，HoloGeo在标准数据集上保持高性能，同时在LandmarkBias-3K上显著超越现有开源模型，验证了其在抗地标偏倚方面的有效性。
teLLMe Why (Ain't Nothing but a Jam): Exploratory Causal Analysis of Urban Driving Data
Authors: Qiwei Li, Jorge Ortiz
Venue: NeurIPS 2025
First: 2026-07-16T17:49:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:49:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the NeurIPS 2025 Workshop on UrbanAI. 6 pages
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Traffic agencies now have access to large volumes of video-derived data for studying safety and congestion. Most of these data are observational and collected without interventions, which makes causal questions such as "How would rain change traffic density?" difficult to answer. We present teLLMe, a system for exploratory causal analysis of urban driving datasets. The system starts from a structured event table built from dashcam annotations and combines causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap-based stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation using linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language questions are mapped to structured causal queries through a schema-aware LLM, enabling users to specify treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. teLLMe returns a "Causal Card" that summarizes effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, followed by a short natural-language explanation. Case studies on BDD-derived traffic events show that the system can surface plausible relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density, while making uncertainty and modeling choices explicit. The system is designed as a tool for hypothesis generation and expert reasoning rather than a source of definitive causal claims.
中文标题/摘要
标题：teLLMe Why（无非是一场即兴演出）：城市驾驶数据的探索性因果分析
交通机构现在可以获取大量基于视频的数据，用于研究安全性和拥堵问题。这些数据大多为观察性数据，且未进行干预，因此难以回答诸如“降雨如何影响交通密度？”等因果问题。我们提出了teLLMe系统，用于对城市驾驶数据集进行探索性因果分析。该系统从基于行车记录仪标注构建的结构化事件表出发，结合因果结构学习（PC算法）、基于自助法的稳定性检验，以及使用线性回归和DoWhy进行查询特定效应估计。通过具备模式感知能力的大型语言模型，自然语言问题可映射为结构化因果查询，使用户能够指定处理因素、结果变量和子群体。teLLMe返回一张“因果卡片”，总结效应估计、调整集、DAG支持及假设，并附带简短自然语言解释。基于BDD数据的交通事件案例研究表明，该系统能够揭示天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间合理的关联关系，同时明确展示不确定性与建模选择。该系统旨在作为假设生成与专家推理的工具，而非提供确定性因果结论的来源。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to answer causal questions from observational urban driving data, teLLMe enables exploratory causal analysis by combining causal structure learning with the PC algorithm, bootstrap stability checks, and query-specific effect estimation via linear regression and DoWhy. Natural-language queries are translated into structured causal queries using a schema-aware LLM, allowing users to define treatments, outcomes, and subpopulations. The system outputs a Causal Card summarizing effect estimates, adjustment sets, DAG support, and assumptions, along with a natural-language explanation. Experiments on BDD-derived traffic events demonstrate that teLLMe can uncover plausible causal relationships involving weather, peak hours, and traffic density while transparently exposing uncertainty and modeling assumptions, positioning it as a tool for hypothesis generation rather than definitive causal inference.
为了解决从观测性城市驾驶数据中难以回答因果问题的挑战，teLLMe系统结合PC算法因果结构学习、基于bootstrap的稳定性检验以及使用线性回归和DoWhy的查询特定效应估计，实现探索性因果分析。通过schema-aware LLM将自然语言问题转化为结构化因果查询，支持用户定义处理、结果和子群体。系统输出包含效应估计、调整集、DAG支持和假设的“因果卡片”，并附带自然语言解释。在BDD衍生交通事件的案例研究中，该系统能揭示天气、高峰时段与交通密度之间的合理因果关系，同时明确展示不确定性与建模选择，定位其为假设生成与专家推理工具，而非提供确定性因果结论。
Bridge Evidence: Static Retrieval Utility Does Not Predict Causal Utility in Multi-Step Agentic Search
Authors: Debayan Mukhopadhyay, Utshab Kumar Ghosh, Shubham Chatterjee
First: 2026-07-16T17:48:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:48:29+00:00
Comments: Preprint; extended version in preparation
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Retrieval systems are trained and evaluated on a static idea of usefulness: hand a document and a question to a reader model, see whether the answer improves, and score the document accordingly. The idea holds up when a document is read on its own. It breaks when a language model works as a search agent, issuing several queries and reasoning across turns, because a document can matter for what it lets the agent do next rather than for what it says about the current question. We measure that gap rather than argue it. Using a ReAct style agent over HotpotQA, we replay 1000 development questions and, for every document the agent read, delete it and re-run the rest of the trajectory from that point. Comparing the original run against its counterfactual gives a Counterfactual Trajectory Utility (CTU) score from three deltas: final answer quality, next query retrieval quality, and turn count. Crossing CTU against Static RAG Utility (SRU) over 23,322 document observations, the two are close to statistically independent (Spearman rho = -0.026). Roughly a third of the documents the agent reads are causally load bearing while looking useless to a static reader; we call these bridge documents. The pattern survives when the reader based axis is swapped for a BM25 and cross encoder proxy, giving a bridge cell of 27.2% on an evenly spread axis. A second experiment pins down the mechanism. Using the Observable Entity Relevance (OER) measure from prior work, entities that discriminate relevant from non-relevant candidates appear in the agent's next query 4.02 times more often than entities found only in non-relevant documents (6.1% vs 1.5%, n = 227,139). A bridge document earns its keep by handing the agent a discriminative entity that redirects the search. Static relevance and causal usefulness are different quantities in agentic retrieval, and optimizing the first does not deliver the second.
中文标题/摘要
标题：桥梁证据：静态检索效用无法预测多步代理搜索中的因果效用
检索系统通常基于静态有用性概念进行训练和评估：给读者模型提供文档和问题，观察答案是否改善，并据此评分。当文档独立阅读时，该概念成立；但当语言模型作为搜索代理发出多个查询并跨轮次推理时，该概念失效，因为文档的价值在于它能引导代理下一步行动，而非仅针对当前问题内容。 我们测量而非争论这一差距。在HotpotQA数据集上使用ReAct风格代理，重放1000个开发问题，对每个代理读取的文档，删除后从该点重新运行剩余轨迹。将原始运行与反事实运行对比，得到三个差值的反事实轨迹效用（CTU）得分：最终答案质量、下一轮查询检索质量及轮次数量。在23,322个文档观测值上，CTU与静态RAG效用（SRU）几乎统计独立（斯皮尔曼相关系数=-0.026）。约三分之一的文档虽对静态读者无用，却对代理具有因果重要性，我们称之为“桥梁文档”。当将读者基准轴替换为BM25和交叉编码器代理时，该模式仍存在，桥梁比例达27.2%。 第二项实验锁定机制。使用先前研究中的可观测实体相关性（OER）度量，相关候选文档中出现的实体比仅在非相关文档中出现的实体多4.02倍（6.1% vs 1.5%，n=227,139）。桥梁文档的价值在于向代理提供可引导搜索方向的判别性实体。在代理检索中，静态相关性与因果效用是不同量级，优化前者无法实现后者。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why static retrieval metrics fail to predict causal utility in multi-step agentic search, motivated by the observation that documents useful for guiding future actions may appear irrelevant to a static reader. Using a ReAct-style agent on HotpotQA, the authors measure causal impact via Counterfactual Trajectory Utility (CTU), which evaluates changes in final answer, next query quality, and turn count after removing each document. Across 23,322 document observations, CTU and Static RAG Utility (SRU) show near-zero correlation (Spearman rho = -0.026), revealing that roughly one-third of documents are 'bridge documents'—causally essential but useless to static readers. A second experiment confirms the mechanism: bridge documents supply discriminative entities that appear in subsequent queries 4.02 times more often than in non-relevant documents, demonstrating that causal utility stems from enabling search redirection, not content relevance.
该论文探讨了静态检索效用（文档直接回答问题的能力）与多步代理搜索中的因果效用之间的差异。作者在HotpotQA上使用ReAct风格代理，通过移除每个文档并重新运行后续轨迹，测量因果效用（Counterfactual Trajectory Utility, CTU），发现静态相关性与因果效用几乎无相关性（Spearman rho = -0.026），约三分之一的文档虽对静态读者无用，却在代理搜索中具有因果重要性，称为“桥梁文档”。进一步实验表明，桥梁文档常包含能引导后续查询的判别性实体，其在相关文档中出现频率是无关文档的4.02倍，证明因果价值源于支持后续搜索步骤，而非直接回答当前问题。
Workload-Driven Optimization for On-Device Real-Time Subtitle Translation
Authors: Tsz-To Wong
First: 2026-07-10T20:22:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:45:45+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
This report studies on-device English-to-Traditional-Chinese subtitle translation for Taiwan under short inputs, short outputs, batch-size-one inference, low latency, and privacy constraints. These conditions limit the value of optimizations designed for long-context or high-throughput language-model serving. Starting from LMT-60-0.6B, preliminary profiling suggests that vocabulary projection becomes a more important decode-time cost after GGUF quantization reduces the relative cost of Transformer blocks. We replace the original 151k-token vocabulary with a 64k-token subtitle-domain tokenizer, migrate the embedding space, and adapt the model through embedding calibration followed by full supervised fine-tuning. On an OpenSubtitles2024 test set, LocalSubs achieves a 59.2% tie-excluded win rate against Google Translate under GPT-4o pairwise judging. Performance is strongest on short cues and declines as cue length increases. In a separate preliminary Apple M2 Metal profiling run, LocalSubs shows a 1.63x speedup over a 151k-vocabulary baseline. The code is available on https://github.com/aiden1020/localsubs .
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向设备端实时字幕翻译的负载驱动优化
本报告研究台湾地区设备端英文至繁体中文字幕翻译，针对短输入、短输出、单批次推理、低延迟及隐私约束条件。这些条件限制了为长上下文或高吞吐量语言模型服务设计的优化方案的价值。从LMT-60-0.6B模型开始，初步分析表明，在GGUF量化降低Transformer块相对成本后，词汇投影成为解码阶段更重要的开销。我们用64k-token的字幕领域分词器替代原151k-token词汇表，迁移嵌入空间，并通过嵌入校准后进行全监督微调以适配模型。在OpenSubtitles2024测试集上，LocalSubs在GPT-4o成对评判中以59.2%的胜率（排除平局）优于谷歌翻译。性能在短字幕提示上表现最佳，随着提示长度增加而下降。在另一项初步的Apple M2 Metal性能测试中，LocalSubs相较151k词汇表基线提速1.63倍。代码开源于https://github.com/aiden1020/localsubs。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for fast, privacy-preserving subtitle translation on-device in Taiwan with short inputs and low latency, this work optimizes a 0.6B-parameter LMT model by replacing its general vocabulary with a 64k-token subtitle-specific tokenizer, migrating embeddings, and fine-tuning via embedding calibration and supervised learning. Experimental results show LocalSubs achieves a 59.2% tie-excluded win rate over Google Translate on OpenSubtitles2024 under GPT-4o judgment, with peak performance on short cues and declining accuracy for longer ones. On Apple M2 hardware, it delivers a 1.63x speedup compared to a 151k-vocabulary baseline.
为满足台湾地区设备端低延迟、隐私保护的英译繁中字幕翻译需求，本研究在短输入、单批次推理和低延迟约束下优化模型。方法基于LMT-60-0.6B，将其151k词表替换为64k字幕领域词表，迁移嵌入空间，并通过嵌入校准与全监督微调进行适配。实验表明，LocalSubs在OpenSubtitles2024测试集上以GPT-4o判断获得59.2%胜率，短字幕表现最佳，长字幕性能下降；在Apple M2 Metal上较原151k词表基线提速1.63倍。
AutoSynthesis: An agentic system for automated meta-analysis
Authors: Moein Taherinezhad, Sebastian Maier, Gerardo Vitagliano, Francesco Pierri, Stefan Feuerriegel
First: 2026-07-16T17:45:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:45:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Evidence synthesis is crucial for turning primary research into reliable knowledge for science, medicine, education, and policy. Yet, quantitative evidence synthesis remains largely manual and difficult to scale. Here, we introduce AutoSynthesis, an end-to-end multi-agent system for automated meta-analysis. Given a research question in natural language, AutoSynthesis formulates a search strategy, retrieves scientific literature, screens candidate studies, assesses full-text eligibility, extracts quantitative statistics, computes standardized effect sizes, and finally performs random-effects meta-analysis. AutoSynthesis further supports heterogeneity analysis to examine how effect sizes vary across moderators, as well as risk-of-bias assessment. As output, AutoSynthesis produces a transparent report aligned with PRISMA guidelines. In our application, AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims. The pooled effect estimates produced by AutoSynthesis are similar to Hedges' $g$ of expert-conducted meta-analyses, indicating close agreement with manual evidence synthesis. Together, these results show that AutoSynthesis can make quantitative evidence synthesis more scalable, thereby supporting evidence-based decision-making across disciplines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AutoSynthesis：一种用于自动化元分析的代理系统
证据综合对于将初级研究转化为科学、医学、教育和政策领域的可靠知识至关重要。然而，定量证据综合仍主要依赖人工，难以规模化。本文介绍AutoSynthesis，一种端到端的多代理系统，用于自动化元分析。给定自然语言表述的研究问题，AutoSynthesis可制定检索策略、获取科学文献、筛选候选研究、评估全文合格性、提取定量统计量、计算标准化效应量，并最终执行随机效应元分析。此外，AutoSynthesis还支持异质性分析以考察效应量在调节变量下的变化，以及偏倚风险评估。输出结果为符合PRISMA指南的透明报告。在实际应用中，AutoSynthesis筛选了28项以上研究，提取了20多个定量声明。AutoSynthesis生成的汇总效应估计值与专家执行的元分析中Hedges的g值相近，表明其与人工证据综合高度一致。综合来看，这些结果表明AutoSynthesis可使定量证据综合更具可扩展性，从而支持跨学科的循证决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to automate and scale quantitative evidence synthesis across disciplines, AutoSynthesis is an end-to-end multi-agent system that takes a natural language research question and autonomously performs meta-analysis: it formulates search strategies, retrieves and screens studies, extracts quantitative data, computes standardized effect sizes, and conducts random-effects meta-analysis with heterogeneity and bias assessments. Experimental results show that AutoSynthesis screened over 28 studies and extracted more than 20 quantitative claims, producing pooled effect estimates comparable to those from expert-conducted meta-analyses using Hedges' g, demonstrating its reliability and scalability for evidence-based decision-making.
为解决定量证据综合难以规模化和自动化的问题，AutoSynthesis 是一个端到端的多智能体系统，可接收自然语言研究问题并自动完成元分析：包括制定检索策略、筛选文献、提取定量数据、计算标准化效应量、执行随机效应元分析，并支持异质性分析与偏倚评估。实验表明，该系统筛选了28篇以上文献，提取了20个以上定量结论，其汇总效应量与专家手工元分析结果（Hedges' g）高度一致，证明其在多学科证据决策中具备可靠性和可扩展性。
ARMOR++: Agentic Orchestration of a Multi-Domain Primitive Set for Transferable Attacks on Deepfake Detectors
Authors: Christos Korgialas, Gabriel Lee Jun Rong, Dion Jia Xu Ho, Pai Chet Ng, Xiaoxiao Miao, Konstantinos N. Plataniotis
First: 2026-07-16T17:45:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:45:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The reliability of deepfake detectors frequently degrades under black-box adversarial transfer, as these models often rely on fragile, architecture-dependent forensic cues. Existing transfer attacks often lack semantic awareness and struggle to maintain effectiveness under strict no-query constraints, particularly when perturbations are transferred from convolutional surrogates to transformer-based targets. To address these limitations, this paper introduces ARMOR++, a robust multi-agent framework designed for high-transferability deepfake evasion. The framework leverages the Qwen2.5-VL Vision-Language Model (VLM) to supply spatial semantic priors, while the Qwen3 Large Language Model (LLM) orchestrates primitive selection, adaptive hyperparameter reparameterization, and entropy-regularized perturbation mixing. By integrating five complementary primitives, spanning dense optimization, saliency-based methods, spatial transformations, frequency-domain perturbations, and block-structured modifications, ARMOR++ effectively targets heterogeneous inductive biases. Rigorous evaluation on the AADD-2025 benchmark demonstrates that ARMOR++ significantly outperforms existing agentic and non-agentic baselines across both low- and high-quality image regimes. Statistical analysis confirms a substantial gain in blind-target Attack Success Rate (ASR) over the state-of-the-art agentic baseline, with further performance advantages evidenced against non-agentic benchmarks and under robust defensive configurations. These findings highlight a significant residual reliability gap in current deepfake detector deployments and demonstrate the efficacy of agentic orchestration in identifying latent vulnerabilities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ARMOR++：用于对抗深度伪造检测器的多域原始操作代理编排
深度伪造检测器在黑盒对抗迁移下的可靠性常显著下降，因其常依赖脆弱且架构相关的取证线索。现有迁移攻击往往缺乏语义感知，在严格无查询约束下难以保持有效性，尤其当扰动从卷积代理迁移到基于Transformer的目标时。为解决这些局限，本文提出ARMOR++，一种面向高迁移性深度伪造规避的鲁棒多代理框架。该框架利用Qwen2.5-VL视觉-语言模型（VLM）提供空间语义先验，由Qwen3大语言模型（LLM）协调原始操作选择、自适应超参数重参数化及熵正则化扰动混合。通过整合五类互补原始操作——密集优化、基于显著性的方法、空间变换、频域扰动及块结构修改——ARMOR++有效针对异构归纳偏置。在AADD-2025基准上的严格评估表明，ARMOR++在低/高质量图像场景下均显著优于现有代理与非代理基线。统计分析证实其在盲目标攻击成功率（ASR）上较当前最优代理基线有显著提升，且在非代理基准及鲁棒防御配置下表现更优。这些发现揭示当前深度伪造检测器部署中仍存在显著可靠性缺口，并证明代理编排在识别潜在漏洞方面的有效性。

Experimental results on AADD-2025 show it significantly outperforms both agentic and non-agentic baselines, achieving higher Attack Success Rates under no-query constraints and across varying image qualities, revealing a persistent reliability gap in current detectors and validating agentic orchestration as a potent evasion strategy.
为应对深度伪造检测器在黑盒对抗迁移中的脆弱性，ARMOR++提出一种多智能体框架，利用Qwen2.5-VL提供空间语义先验，Qwen3协调攻击原语选择、超参数调整与扰动混合。该框架整合五类互补攻击原语——密集优化、基于显著性的方法、空间变换、频域扰动及块结构修改，以覆盖不同模型架构的归纳偏置。在AADD-2025基准测试中，ARMOR++显著优于现有智能体与非智能体基线，在盲目标条件下及高低质量图像中均实现更高攻击成功率（ASR），并在强防御配置下仍保持优势，揭示当前检测器部署中存在显著可靠性缺口。
Mutable Low-Rank Sketches for Retrain-Free Recommendation
Authors: Hector J. Garcia, Nick Clayton
First: 2026-07-16T17:39:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:39:38+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, 8 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A common bottleneck in two-stage recommendation is embedding staleness: when a user rates a new item, their embedding remains fixed until the next retrain cycle. We propose mutable sketches, which store each user's preferences in a KP-tree (a sparse segment tree with sum aggregation), fit a low-rank projection once, and recompute embeddings on-the-fly as ratings arrive. We prove that each new observation monotonically tightens the prediction error envelope (Theorem 1), a guarantee that FunkSVD and eALS lack. On KuaiRec, the mutable sketch achieves 0.810 RMSE at 1.8% data read vs. ALS 0.822 at 100%, with 8x faster per-batch updates. A new user receives personalized recommendations in <1 ms after their first rating, with no model retraining required. A comparison of sampling strategies across density regimes shows that the KP-tree's norm-proportional sampling provides 40-130% better item coverage on sparse data (<1% density), while uniform sampling suffices on dense matrices.

标题：无需重训练的可变低秩草图推荐方法
两阶段推荐中的常见瓶颈是嵌入过时：当用户对新物品评分时，其嵌入保持不变，直至下一次重训练周期。我们提出可变草图方法，将每个用户的偏好存储在KP树（带求和聚合的稀疏段树）中，仅进行一次低秩投影，当评分到达时动态重新计算嵌入。我们证明每个新观测值都会单调收紧预测误差边界（定理1），这是FunkSVD和eALS所不具备的保证。在KuaiRec数据集上，该方法在仅1.8%数据读取下实现0.810 RMSE，优于ALS（100%数据读取时为0.822），且每批次更新快8倍。新用户在首次评分后不到1毫秒内即可获得个性化推荐，无需模型重训练。跨密度区域的采样策略比较表明，KP树的范数比例采样在稀疏数据（<1%密度）上可提供40-130%更优的物品覆盖率，而在稠密矩阵上均匀采样已足够。
Summary / 总结
To address embedding staleness in two-stage recommendation systems, the paper introduces mutable low-rank sketches that store user preferences in a KP-tree structure and compute embeddings on-demand using a single low-rank projection. This approach guarantees monotonically tighter prediction error bounds with each new rating, a property not shared by FunkSVD or eALS. Experiments on KuaiRec show the method achieves 0.810 RMSE using only 1.8% of the data versus ALS’s 0.822 with 100% data, while updating 8x faster per batch. New users receive personalized recommendations within 1 ms after their first rating without retraining. Beyond the Leaderboard: Design Lessons for Trustworthy Multimodal VQA
Authors: Sushant Gautam, Vajira Thambawita, Michael A. Riegler, Pål Halvorsen, Steven A. Hicks
First: 2026-07-16T17:38:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:38:19+00:00
Comments: Accepted for presentation at the 39th IEEE International Symposium on Computer-Based Medical Systems (IEEE CBMS 2026) as a regular paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Healthcare multimodal AI must combine visual and textual evidence while remaining reliable and interpretable. Using MediaEval Medico 2025 as a retrospective GI endoscopy case study, we analyze design choices across nine documented systems for question answering and explanation quality. Parameter-efficient adaptation of pretrained backbones provides strong challenge performance, but answer-level gains do not consistently translate into faithful and complete clinical reasoning. Methods enforcing structured reasoning and explicit grounding show more reliable behavior across heterogeneous question types, although the evidence is correlational rather than ablation-based. These results motivate evaluation beyond lexical overlap, standardized evidence-linked explanations, leakage-aware data governance, and lightweight robustness and calibration checks. The findings support trustworthy multimodal healthcare AI based on data fusion, explainability, and resilient evaluation.

TikStance: A Multimodal and Hierarchical Dataset for Multi-target Stance Analysis in TikTok Political Conversations
Authors: Yazhi Zhang, Fuqiang Niu, Bowen Zhang
First: 2026-07-16T17:37:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:37:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Political discourse has increasingly moved to short-video platforms, yet computational analysis of such content remains constrained by the scarcity of datasets that jointly preserve audiovisual information and hierarchical conversations. Here we present TikStance, a multimodal and context-aware dataset comprising 161 videos and 13,876 comments from TikTok, designed for stance detection in political discussions. The dataset covers three major political figures in the 2024 U.S. election cycle--Donald Trump, Joe Biden, and Kamala Harris--with content collected between September 2023 and January 2025. Each discussion unit links a host video and its metadata to a parent-linked comment tree, enabling stance analysis within both audiovisual and conversational context. Each item was independently labeled by three annotators using a three-class scheme (Favor, Against, None) for video-to-target and comment-to-target stance; items with disagreement were re-annotated, and the final Krippendorff's \(α\) reached 0.743, 0.723, and 0.722 for the Trump, Biden, and Harris subsets, respectively. Descriptive analysis further reveals target-dependent differences in stance distributions and conversational depth, with nested replies accounting for 23.3\% of all comments. By combining multi-target coverage, hierarchical conversations, and reliable multi-level human annotations, TikStance supports research in multimodal stance detection, political communication, computational social science, and context-aware natural language processing.

标题：TikStance：面向TikTok政治对话多目标立场分析的多模态分层数据集
政治 discourse 越来越多地转移到短视频平台，但此类内容的计算分析受限于缺乏同时保留音视频信息和分层对话结构的数据集。本文提出 TikStance 数据集，包含161个视频和13,876条评论，专为 TikTok 政治讨论中的立场检测设计。数据覆盖2024年美国总统选举三大主要政治人物——特朗普、拜登和哈里斯，内容采集时间为2023年9月至2025年1月。每个讨论单元将主视频及其元数据与父级链接评论树关联，支持在音视频和对话上下文内进行立场分析。每项内容由三位标注员独立标注，采用三分类方案（支持、反对、无立场）分别标注视频与评论对目标的立场；存在分歧的内容重新标注，最终 Krippendorff's α 分别达到特朗普、拜登和哈里斯子集的0.743、0.723和0.722。描述性分析进一步揭示立场分布和对话深度随目标的不同而变化，嵌套回复占所有评论的23.3%。通过多目标覆盖、分层对话结构和可靠多层级人工标注，TikStance 支持多模态立场检测、政治传播、计算社会科学及上下文感知自然语言处理研究。

为应对短视频平台政治话语分析缺乏数据集的问题，本研究构建了TikStance数据集，包含2023至2025年间161个视频和13,876条评论，聚焦2024年美国大选三大候选人。每个视频与评论通过层级结构关联，支持在视听与对话语境中进行立场分析，每项内容由三位标注员独立标注（支持、反对、无立场），分歧项经重标后达成高标注一致性（Krippendorff’s α：0.72–0.74）。分析显示立场分布与对话深度因目标人物而异，23.3%的评论为嵌套回复，凸显该数据集在多模态立场检测与语境感知NLP研究中的价值。
What Models Express, Suppress, and Resist: Auditing Open-Weight LLMs with Persona Vectors
Authors: Winston Zeng, Ali Emami, Jinho D. Choi
First: 2026-07-14T18:10:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:37:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
What a language model will and will not do is largely set during post-training, but which behaviors it expresses, hides, or resists is not revealed by prompting alone. Persona vectors, behavioral directions in activation space, can probe this organization, but prior work covers only a handful of traits. We present the first systematic application of persona vectors at this scale, compiling a 53-trait inventory across four behaviorally distinct domains and labeling every trait in two open-weight models as natural (expressed at baseline), steerable latent but amplifiable, or intractable (resistant to standard extraction). Both models default to helpful, task-oriented behavior: all nine agentic traits are natural, and their default clinician behavior matches a board-certified psychologist's independent desirability judgments on 16 of 17 traits. Steering produces its largest gains on traits these defaults exclude: hyperbole, hallucination, and sycophancy. The same asymmetry holds across all 171 generic-trait pairs: two steerable traits can collapse the composition, but pairs involving a default never do. Where standard extraction fails on a trait like "evil," a vector transferred from a fine-tuned variant still recovers it, with the residual refusals appearing inside the model's chain-of-thought. Persona vectors are most informative not as a set of controls but as a probe of behavioral organization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：模型表达、抑制与抵抗什么：使用人格向量审计开源大语言模型
语言模型会或不会做什么主要在后训练阶段确定，但其表现出、隐藏或抵抗哪些行为，仅靠提示无法揭示。人格向量作为激活空间中的行为方向，可探测这种组织结构，但此前研究仅覆盖少量特质。本文首次系统性地在该规模应用人格向量，构建涵盖四个行为迥异领域的53项特质清单，并对两个开源模型中的每项特质标注为自然（基线表达）、可引导但可放大，或不可控（抵抗标准提取）。两个模型默认表现出有益、任务导向的行为：九项代理特质均为自然，其默认临床行为在17项特质中的16项上与注册心理学家的独立偏好判断一致。引导在默认排除的特质（如夸张、幻觉、奉承）上效果最显著。在171对通用特质中，相同不对称性普遍存在：两项可引导特质可压缩组合，但涉及默认特质的配对则无法实现。当标准提取在“邪恶”等特质上失败时，从微调变体转移的向量仍能恢复该特质，残余拒绝出现在模型的思维链中。人格向量最富信息量之处不在于作为控制手段，而在于作为行为组织结构的探测工具。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how open-weight language models express, suppress, or resist specific behaviors beyond their default helpfulness, using persona vectors—directions in activation space that probe behavioral tendencies. The researchers systematically applied these vectors to a 53-trait inventory across four behavioral domains, labeling each trait in two models as natural (expressed at baseline), steerable but amplifiable, or intractable (resistant to extraction). Both models defaulted to helpful, task-oriented behavior, with all nine agentic traits being natural and their clinician-like behavior aligning with human preference on 16 of 17 traits. Steering yielded the greatest gains for traits excluded from defaults, such as hyperbole and sycophancy, and asymmetry emerged across 171 trait pairs: steerable traits could be collapsed via composition, but default traits could not. Even when standard extraction failed (e.g., for 'evil'), transferring a vector from a fine-tuned variant still recovered the trait, with refusals appearing in the model’s internal reasoning. Persona vectors are most valuable as probes of behavioral organization, not as direct controls.
该研究通过使用‘人格向量’（激活空间中的行为方向）系统探究开放权重语言模型在默认行为之外如何表达、抑制或抵抗特定行为。研究人员在四个行为领域内对53种特质进行了系统分析，将每种特质在两个模型中的表现标记为‘自然’（默认表达）、‘可引导但可放大’或‘不可控’（对标准提取抵抗）。两个模型默认表现出助人、任务导向行为，所有九种代理特质均为自然表达，且其默认临床行为与17种特质中16项专家判断一致。引导效果在默认行为之外的特质（如夸张、幻觉、谄媚）中最为显著，而默认行为特质则无法与可引导特质组合。即使标准提取对‘邪恶’等特质失效，从微调版本转移的向量仍能恢复该行为，拒绝理由出现在模型的内部推理中。研究认为，人格向量的价值在于揭示行为组织结构，而非作为直接控制手段。
Language Identification via Compositional Data Analysis: A Linear-Time Classifier Based on Log-Ratio Geometry
Authors: Paul-Andrei Pogăcean, Sanda-Maria Avram
First: 2026-07-16T17:35:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:35:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Language identification is commonly addressed using either neural architectures or statistical n-gram models. Neural approaches typically require substantial computational resources, whereas classical frequency-based methods offer efficient linear-time performance, but rely on distance metrics that are not always appropriate for compositional data. This work models character and bigram frequency distributions as compositional vectors constrained to the simplex and mapped via the centered log-ratio (CLR) transformation bijectively onto the $(D-1)$-dimensional zero-sum subspace of $\mathbb{R}^D$, where Euclidean distances correspond to Aitchison distances. A pipeline is proposed, combining CLR-transformed unigram and bigram features with Laplace smoothing to address sparsity. The method is evaluated on six languages. Experimental results show that the proposed approach achieves robust accuracy across different text lengths, with strong performance for longer sequences. These findings indicate that compositional representations provide a deterministic and computationally efficient alternative for language identification, particularly in settings where interpretability and low resource consumption are essential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过组合数据分析的语言识别：基于对数比几何的线性时间分类器
语言识别通常采用神经架构或统计n-gram模型。神经方法通常需要大量计算资源，而传统基于频率的方法虽具线性时间效率，但依赖的距离度量对组合数据未必适用。 本工作将字符和二元组频率分布建模为受限于单纯形的组合向量，并通过中心对数比（CLR）变换双射映射至R^D的(D-1)维零和子空间，其中欧氏距离对应Aitchison距离。提出一种流程，结合CLR变换的单字和二元组特征与拉普拉斯平滑以解决稀疏性问题。该方法在六种语言上进行评估。 实验结果表明，所提方法在不同文本长度下均取得稳健准确率，对较长序列表现尤为优异。这些发现表明，组合表示为语言识别提供了一种确定性且计算高效的替代方案，尤其在需要可解释性和低资源消耗的场景中具有优势。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the computational demands of neural language identification and the limitations of traditional n-gram models for compositional data, this study proposes a linear-time classifier using log-ratio geometry. It transforms character and bigram frequency distributions into compositional vectors via the centered log-ratio (CLR) transformation, mapping them to a zero-sum subspace where Euclidean distances align with Aitchison distances. The method combines CLR-transformed features with Laplace smoothing to handle sparsity and is tested across six languages. Results show robust accuracy across varying text lengths, with particularly strong performance on longer sequences, demonstrating that compositional representations offer a deterministic, efficient alternative suitable for low-resource and interpretable language identification tasks.
为解决神经方法计算开销大、传统n-gram模型在组合数据上距离度量不适用的问题，该研究提出一种基于组合数据分析的线性时间语言识别分类器。方法将字符与二元组频率建模为单纯形约束向量，通过中心对数比变换映射至零和子空间，使欧氏距离等价于Aitchison距离，并结合拉普拉斯平滑处理稀疏性。在六种语言上进行实验，结果表明该方法在不同文本长度下均表现稳健，尤其在长序列上性能突出，验证了组合表示在资源受限且需可解释性场景下是一种高效可靠的替代方案。
Language as a Wave Phenomenon: Semantic Phase Locking and Interference in Neural Networks
Authors: Alper Yıldırım, İbrahim Yücedağ
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2025-12-01T02:46:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:34:29+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In standard Transformer architectures, semantic importance is often conflated with activation magnitude, obscuring the geometric structure of latent representations. To disentangle these factors, we introduce PRISM, a complex-valued architecture designed to isolate the computational role of phase. By enforcing a strict unit-norm constraint ($|z| = 1$) and replacing attention with gated harmonic convolutions, the model is encouraged to utilize subtractive interference in the frequency domain to suppress noise, rather than relying on magnitude-based gating. We utilize this constrained regime to study a hybrid architecture -- fusing phase-based routing with standard attention -- which achieves improved parameter efficiency and representation quality compared to baselines in our evaluated settings. Mechanistically, interventional ablations indicate that the model carries substantial task-relevant information in phase: preserving phase largely maintains performance, whereas disrupting phase causes severe degradation. Together, these results suggest that phase-based spectral interference is a usable computational mechanism for neural sequence modeling at the evaluated scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：语言作为波现象：神经网络中的语义相位锁定与干涉
在标准Transformer架构中，语义重要性常与激活幅度混淆，掩盖了潜在表示的几何结构。为分离这些因素，我们引入PRISM，一种复数值架构，旨在隔离相位的计算作用。通过强制单位范数约束（|z| = 1）并用门控谐波卷积替代注意力机制，模型被鼓励在频域中利用相减干涉抑制噪声，而非依赖基于幅度的门控。我们利用这一约束机制研究了一种混合架构——融合基于相位的路由与标准注意力——在评估设置中相比基线模型实现了更优的参数效率和表示质量。机制上，干预性消融实验表明，模型在相位中携带大量任务相关的信息：保持相位可维持性能，而破坏相位则导致严重性能下降。综上，这些结果表明，基于相位的频谱干涉是适用于评估规模的神经序列建模的可用计算机制。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency in standard Transformer models to confuse semantic importance with activation magnitude, this work introduces PRISM, a complex-valued neural architecture that isolates phase as a computational primitive. By enforcing unit-norm constraints and replacing attention with gated harmonic convolutions, PRISM leverages subtractive interference in the frequency domain to suppress noise. Experiments show that fusing phase-based routing with standard attention improves parameter efficiency and representation quality over baselines, and ablation studies confirm that phase carries substantial task-relevant information: preserving phase maintains performance while disrupting it causes significant degradation, indicating phase-based spectral interference as a viable mechanism for sequence modeling.
鉴于标准Transformer模型常将语义重要性与激活幅度混为一谈，本研究提出PRISM架构，通过强制单位范数约束和用门控谐波卷积替代注意力机制，将相位的计算作用独立出来。该模型利用频域中的相位干涉抑制噪声，从而实现更高效且几何结构更清晰的表示。实验表明，结合相位路由与标准注意力的混合架构在参数效率和表示质量上优于基线模型，且消融实验显示相位承载大量任务相关信息——保留相位可维持性能，破坏相位则导致显著性能下降。
In-Place Tokenizer Expansion for Pre-trained LLMs
Authors: Jimmy T. H. Smith, Tarek Dakhran, Alberto Cabrera, Simon S. Lee, Paul Pak, Aditya Tadimeti, Tim Seyde, Maxime Labonne, Alexander Amini, Mathias Lechner
First: 2026-07-16T17:32:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:32:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A tokenizer fixed at the start of pre-training allocates vocabulary in proportion to the pre-training corpus, reflecting the deployment priorities at that time. When those priorities shift, languages added later are split into many more tokens per word, which can raise latency, compute, and energy consumption for users of those languages. Cloud models can afford a broad vocabulary because the embedding and LM-head matrices are a small fraction of their parameters. On a compact model those matrices are a material share of per-token decode bandwidth, so on-device models ship small vocabularies and accept fragmentation outside a fixed language set. We present tokenizer expansion, an in-place recipe for upgrading a pre-trained model's tokenizer when the model producer controls its design. We continue the existing tokenizer's BPE merges on a multilingual corpus, so most source tokens carry over unchanged as single tokens and every new token has an exact decomposition into source tokens. We copy the carried-over embedding rows unchanged and initialize new rows as the mean of their source sub-token embeddings. A two-stage adaptation, embedding-only training then full-model continued pre-training, recovers source-checkpoint quality. We apply the recipe to a continued pre-trained checkpoint of LFM2-8B-A1B, an 8B-parameter Mixture-of-Experts model, to help produce LFM2.5-8B-A1B with a 128K tokenizer. The expanded tokenizer encodes Hindi and Vietnamese in roughly $2.4\times$ and $2.6\times$ fewer tokens than the source (up to $4.0\times$ on Thai). Combining these reductions with the measured per-token cost of the larger vocabulary, we estimate a $2.2$-$3.7\times$ per-character decode speedup for these languages across our reference devices. We release the model weights and the expanded tokenizer, and report the negative findings that shaped the recipe.
中文标题/摘要
标题：预训练大语言模型的原位分词器扩展
预训练开始时固定的分词器按训练语料分配词汇表，反映当时的部署优先级。当优先级变化时，后期加入的语言会被拆分为更多词元，导致这些语言用户的延迟、计算和能耗上升。云端模型因嵌入和语言模型头矩阵仅占参数的小部分，可承受广义词汇表；而轻量模型中这些矩阵占每词元解码带宽的较大比例，因此设备端模型通常使用小词汇表并接受超出固定语言集的碎片化。我们提出分词器扩展方法，一种在模型生产者控制设计时对预训练模型分词器进行原位升级的方案。该方法在多语言语料上继续原有BPE合并，使大部分源词元保持不变，每个新词元可精确分解为源词元。我们复制已保留的嵌入行，新行初始化为源子词元嵌入的均值。通过两阶段适应——先仅嵌入训练，再全模型继续预训练——可恢复源检查点质量。我们将该方案应用于LFM2-8B-A1B（8B参数专家混合模型）的持续预训练检查点，以生成具有128K词汇表的LFM2.5-8B-A1B。扩展后的分词器使印地语和越南语的词元数分别减少约2.4倍和2.6倍（泰语最高达4.0倍）。结合更大词汇表的每词元成本，我们在参考设备上估计这些语言的每字符解码速度提升2.2至3.7倍。我们发布模型权重和扩展分词器，并报告塑造该方案的负面发现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed tokenizers in pre-trained LLMs that disadvantage newly added languages, this work introduces in-place tokenizer expansion: a method to upgrade a model’s tokenizer without retraining from scratch. It extends the original BPE merges to a multilingual corpus, preserving most source tokens while decomposing new ones into existing sub-tokens. Embedding rows for preserved tokens are copied unchanged, while new tokens are initialized via mean pooling of their source sub-token embeddings. The method then uses two-stage adaptation—embedding-only training followed by full-model continued pre-training—to restore source checkpoint performance. Applied to LFM2-8B-A1B, it produced LFM2.5-8B-A1B with a 128K tokenizer, reducing token count for Hindi and Vietnamese by 2.4x and 2.6x respectively (up to 4.0x for Thai), and estimated a 2.2–3.7x per-character decode speedup on target devices.
为解决预训练大语言模型中固定分词器对新增语言效率低下的问题，本文提出一种原地分词器扩展方法：在不重新训练的前提下，将原BPE合并规则扩展至多语言语料，保留大部分源词元，新词元则分解为源词元组合。保留词元的嵌入向量直接复制，新词元嵌入初始化为对应源子词元向量的均值。随后通过两阶段适配：先仅训练嵌入层，再进行完整模型继续预训练。该方法应用于LFM2-8B-A1B模型，生成了128K分词器的LFM2.5-8B-A1B，使印地语和越南语的词元数分别减少2.4倍和2.6倍（泰语最高达4.0倍），并在参考设备上估计实现2.2–3.7倍的每字符解码加速。
CRISP: Constrained Refinement via Iterative Squeezing Process for Robust Medical Image Segmentation under Domain Shift
Authors: Yizhou Fang, Pujin Cheng, Yixiang Liu, Xiaoying Tang, Longxi Zhou
Venue: AAAI 2027
First: 2026-07-16T17:32:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:32:26+00:00
Comments: X pages, 3 figures, 3 tables; submitted to AAAI 2027
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Distribution shift in medical imaging remains a central bottleneck for the clinical translation of medical AI. Failure to address it can lead to severe performance degradation in unseen environments and exacerbate health inequities. Existing methods for domain adaptation are inherently limited by exhausting predefined possibilities through simulated shifts or pseudo-supervision. Such strategies struggle in the open-ended and unpredictable real world, where distribution shifts are effectively infinite. To address this challenge, we adopt the "Rank Stability of Positive Regions" as a working assumption under distribution shift, and use it to derive robust spatial hints for source-only segmentation. Guided by this assumption, we propose CRISP, a model-agnostic framework that, unlike deployment-time adaptation, requires no test-time parameter updates and no target-domain data--a target-free, plug-in refinement framework that segments with frozen weights. Rather than using ranking to directly output masks, CRISP exploits the stability of probability rankings under distribution shift to derive robust spatial priors. Via latent feature perturbation, perturbation-invariant high-grade regions define a high-precision (HP) core, while voxels that remain potentially foreground under at least one perturbation define a high-recall (HR) support; these dual priors are then recursively refined under perturbation. We then design an iterative training framework that progressively squeezes HP and HR toward the final segmentation. Extensive evaluations on multi-center cardiac MRI and CT-based lung vessel segmentation demonstrate CRISP's superior robustness, significantly outperforming state-of-the-art methods with striking HD95 reductions of up to 0.14 (7.0% improvement), 1.90 (13.1% improvement), and 8.39 (38.9% improvement) pixels across multi-center, demographic, and modality shifts, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CRISP：通过迭代挤压过程实现约束细化，用于域迁移下的鲁棒医学图像分割
