医学影像中的分布偏移仍是医学AI临床转化的核心瓶颈。若不解决此问题，可能导致在未见环境中性能严重下降，并加剧健康不平等。现有域自适应方法受限于通过模拟偏移或伪监督耗尽预定义可能性。此类策略在现实世界中难以应对开放且不可预测的分布偏移，其可能性近乎无限。为应对这一挑战，我们采用“正区域排序稳定性”作为分布偏移下的工作假设，并据此推导出仅需源域数据即可实现的鲁棒空间提示。基于此假设，我们提出CRISP，一个模型无关的框架，与部署时自适应不同，无需测试时参数更新，也无需目标域数据——这是一种无目标、可插拔的冻结权重细化框架。CRISP不直接通过排序输出分割掩码，而是利用分布偏移下概率排序的稳定性推导鲁棒空间先验。通过潜在特征扰动，扰动不变的高阶区域定义高精度（HP）核心，而至少在一种扰动下仍可能为前景的体素定义高召回（HR）支持；这两个先验随后在扰动下递归细化。我们进一步设计了迭代训练框架，逐步将HP与HR挤压至最终分割结果。在多中心心脏MRI和基于CT的肺血管分割任务上的广泛评估表明，CRISP展现出卓越的鲁棒性，显著优于现有最先进方法，在多中心、人口统计学和模态偏移下，HD95分别降低0.14（7.0%提升）、1.90（13.1%提升）和8.39（38.9%提升）像素。
Summary / 总结
To tackle the challenge of domain shift in medical imaging that hinders clinical deployment of AI models, CRISP introduces a target-free, plug-in refinement framework that operates without test-time updates or target-domain data. It leverages the assumption that positive region rankings remain stable under distribution shifts to derive robust spatial priors: a high-precision core from perturbation-invariant high-grade regions and a high-recall support from voxels surviving as foreground under at least one perturbation. These dual priors are iteratively refined through an iterative squeezing process that progressively converges toward final segmentation. Evaluated on multi-center cardiac MRI and lung vessel segmentation tasks, CRISP significantly outperforms existing methods, achieving up to 0.14, 1.90, and 8.39 pixel improvements in HD95 across multi-center, demographic, and modality shifts respectively.
为解决医学影像领域分布偏移导致的临床部署瓶颈，CRISP提出一种无需测试时更新参数或目标域数据的插件式精炼框架。该方法利用分布偏移下正区域概率排序的稳定性，推导出高精度核心与高召回支持区域：前者由扰动不变的体素构成，后者由至少一种扰动下仍被判定为前景的体素构成。通过迭代扰动和特征空间压缩，这些先验区域逐步精炼至最终分割结果。在多中心心脏MRI与肺血管CT数据集上的实验表明，CRISP显著优于现有方法，在多中心、人群与模态偏移场景下HD95指标分别提升0.14、1.90和8.39像素。
Networked Intelligence: Active Shared Context Graphs for Human-AI Team Science
Authors: Sutanay Choudhury, Jeffrey J. Czajka, Lummy M. O. Monteiro, Erin Bredeweg, Jason McDermott, Katherine Wolf, Alex Beliaev, Josh Elmore, Paul Piehowski, Kylee Tate, Yuqian Gao, Aivett Bilbao, Kelly Stratton, Scott Baker, Jaydeep P. Bardhan, Kristin Burnum Johnson, Chris Oehmen, Robert Rallo
First: 2026-07-14T19:29:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:32:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most AI-for-science systems focus on scaling a single reasoning process by using better models, larger context windows, long-horizon agentic execution, or digital co-scientists working with one principal user. However, challenging scientific problems are rarely solved by one reasoner alone. They are solved by teams whose members carry different priors, experimental background, tacit knowledge, and domain-trained intuitions. The open problem is therefore not only how to scale models, but how to develop "networked intelligence", scaling the connections between humans and AI systems so that a result or hypothesis produced in one context reaches another person, agent, instrument or robot that can act on it. We introduce Mycelium, an active shared workspace that automatically connects researchers and AI agents. As human users and agents work, the system captures important observations and hypotheses, tracks how they relate to the team's evolving knowledge model, and routes them to the person or agent whose next decision they can inform. We evaluate Mycelium through a real-world scientific discovery use case: a biological multi-omics campaign where shared context turned a local analytical finding into a cross-expert mechanistic constraint and ultimately into an experimental design. Finally, we describe networked intelligence as sparse conditional computation over distributed scientific contexts. This framework establishes when a scaled standalone agent is sufficient, and when isolated data and specialized expertise make a networked approach essential.
中文标题/摘要
标题：网络智能：用于人机协同科学的主动共享上下文图
大多数面向科学的人工智能系统侧重于通过使用更好的模型、更大的上下文窗口、长周期代理执行或与单一主用户协作的数字共科学家来扩展单一推理过程。然而，具有挑战性的科学问题很少由单一推理者解决，而是由成员拥有不同先验、实验背景、隐性知识和领域训练直觉的团队解决。因此，面临的挑战不仅是如何扩展模型，而是如何发展“网络智能”，即扩展人类与AI系统之间的连接，使在一个上下文中产生的结果或假设能够传递给另一人、代理、仪器或机器人并使其发挥作用。我们引入了Mycelium，一个主动共享的工作空间，可自动连接研究人员和AI代理。当人类用户和代理工作时，系统会捕捉重要观察和假设，追踪它们与团队知识模型的演变关系，并将它们路由到能够从中获益的下一位决策者。我们通过一个现实世界的科学发现用例——一项生物多组学研究——评估了Mycelium：共享上下文将局部分析发现转化为跨专家机制约束，最终转化为实验设计。最后，我们将网络智能描述为在分布式科学上下文上的稀疏条件计算。该框架明确了何时单个扩展代理已足够，以及何时孤立数据和专门知识使网络化方法成为必需。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to solve complex scientific problems through collaborative human-AI teams rather than isolated AI agents, the paper proposes Mycelium, an active shared context system that dynamically routes hypotheses and observations to the most relevant human or AI agent based on evolving team knowledge. Evaluated in a real-world biological multi-omics study, Mycelium transformed a local analytical insight into a cross-expert mechanistic constraint and guided experimental design, demonstrating how shared context enables collective intelligence. The framework concludes that networked intelligence—sparse conditional computation across distributed contexts—is essential when specialized expertise or distributed action is required, not just when scaling standalone agents.
为解决复杂科学问题需人类与AI协同而非单纯扩展单个AI模型，本文提出Mycelium——一个动态连接研究人员与AI代理的共享工作空间。系统自动捕获协作过程中的关键观察与假设，追踪其与团队知识模型的关联，并将信息路由至最合适的人员或代理以支持决策。在真实生物多组学研究中，该系统将局部分析发现转化为跨专家机制约束并指导实验设计，验证了共享上下文对集体智能的作用。作者将网络化智能定义为分布式科学上下文上的稀疏条件计算，明确了何时单个扩展代理足够、何时需网络化协作。
Data Driven Block Replacement Scheduling
Authors: Aniruddhan Ganesaraman, VIdyadhar Kulkarni
First: 2026-07-16T17:31:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:31:15+00:00
Comments: 36 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop data-driven algorithms for maintaining $N$ independent identical machines under a \textit{block replacement policy}, in which each machine is replaced upon failure and all machines are jointly replaced at regular intervals of length $k$. The goal is to learn the cost-minimizing interval $k^*$ from operational data when the lifetime distribution is unknown. At each decision epoch, the operator selects $k \in \{1, 2, \ldots, K\}$, observes the resulting failure history (a mixture of complete and right-censored lifetimes) and incurs a per-unit-time cost governed by the renewal function. We formulate this as a stochastic multi-armed bandit and propose Hoeffding- and Bernstein-based lower-confidence-bound algorithms achieving $O(K \log T)$ regret, matching the Lai--Robbins lower bound. Exploiting a nested observation property unique to block replacement, correlated variants attain $O((K-k^*)\log T)$ regret and require only $O(1)$ direct pulls of suboptimal arms $k < k^*$. A complementary Kaplan--Meier renewal algorithm estimates the lifetime distribution nonparametrically from censored data, achieving almost-sure policy consistency and empirically near-zero incremental regret at long horizons. We additionally analyze two average-cost MDPs: a time-elapsed formulation establishing that block replacement is optimal within its policy class for any lifetime distribution, and an age-vector formulation proving a monotone threshold structure under increasing failure rate distributions and providing a gold-standard cost benchmark. Numerical experiments confirm the theoretical ordering and reveal structural cost gaps between optimal block and age-dependent replacement.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数据驱动的块式更换调度
我们开发了数据驱动算法，用于在块式更换策略下维护N台独立同质机器。该策略中，每台机器在故障时被更换，且所有机器在固定间隔k后共同更换。目标是在寿命分布未知时，从运行数据中学习最小化成本的间隔k*。在每个决策时刻，操作员选择k∈{1,2,…,K}，观察到故障历史（包含完全和右删失寿命的混合），并承担由更新函数决定的单位时间成本。我们将此问题建模为随机多臂赌博机，并提出基于霍夫丁和伯恩斯坦的置信下界算法，实现O(K log T)的遗憾，达到Lai-Robbins下界。利用块式更换特有的嵌套观测性质，相关变体可实现O((K-k*) log T)的遗憾，且仅需O(1)次对次优臂k<k*的直接拉取。此外，互补的Kaplan-Meier更新算法从删失数据非参数估计寿命分布，实现几乎必然的策略一致性，并在长时域内实证近零增量遗憾。我们还分析了两种平均成本MDP：一种基于时间流逝的公式证明块式更换在任何寿命分布下于其策略类中最优；另一种基于年龄向量的公式证明在递增故障率分布下存在单调阈值结构，并提供黄金标准成本基准。数值实验验证了理论排序，并揭示了最优块式更换与年龄依赖更换之间的结构化成本差距。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize maintenance scheduling for identical machines under block replacement policies, this work develops data-driven algorithms that learn the optimal replacement interval k* from operational failure data without assuming a known lifetime distribution. The method models the problem as a stochastic multi-armed bandit, proposing Hoeffding and Bernstein-based algorithms achieving O(K log T) regret, and leveraging a unique nested observation structure to reduce regret to O((K-k*) log T) for correlated variants, requiring only O(1) pulls of suboptimal arms. A Kaplan-Meier-based estimator nonparametrically estimates the lifetime distribution, yielding policy consistency and near-zero incremental regret over long horizons. Theoretical analysis via two average-cost MDPs confirms block replacement’s optimality within its class and reveals cost gaps between block and age-dependent policies under increasing failure rate assumptions, supported by numerical experiments.
为在未知寿命分布下优化相同机器的块更换策略，本文提出数据驱动算法以学习最小成本更换周期k*。方法将问题建模为随机多臂赌博机，提出基于Hoeffding和Bernstein的算法，实现O(K log T)后悔值，相关变体可进一步降至O((K-k*) log T)，仅需O(1)次对次优臂的直接拉取。Kaplan-Meier重置估计器从删失数据非参数估计寿命分布，实现策略一致性及长期近零增量后悔。理论分析通过两个MDP模型证明块更换在其策略类中最优，并揭示其相较年龄依赖策略的结构化成本优势。
Divergent Gaze Patterns in Artistic Viewing: Spatial and Temporal Signatures of Attention Across Autistic Individuals, Artists, and Neurotypical Observers
Authors: Mohammed Amine Kerkouri, Daphné Senggaran, Renaud Jusiak, Océane Lehmann, Marouane Tliba, Claire Wardak, Emmanuelle Houy-Durand, Shasha Morel-Kohlmeyer, Aladine Chetouani, Nadia Aguillon-Hernandez
First: 2026-07-16T17:29:18+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:29:18+00:00
Comments: Submitted for review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
How different populations visually explore artworks bears on cognitive science and on accessibility design, yet most eye-tracking work in autism has used social scenes rather than art, and has analysed where the eyes land while ignoring when and in what order. We present a comparative free-viewing study across three groups, autistic adults (ASD), trained artists, and neurotypical observers, who each viewed 30 paintings for 15s. We introduce a directed, metric-grounded framework that compares groups along two complementary axes: a spatial axis, in which one group's fixation-density map predicts another's fixations under six saliency metrics (AUC-Judd, NSS, CC, SIM, KL, Information Gain); and a temporal axis, in which individual scanpaths are compared with MultiMatch, ScanMatch, a foveal-disc IoU score (FDISS), and dynamic time warping (DTW). Fixations are extracted uniformly for all groups with a dispersion-threshold algorithm. Three results converge. (i)Artists and neurotypicals are almost indistinguishable in both space (density-map correlation CC=0.96) and time (they form the most alignable scanpath pair), whereas ASD gaze diverges from both. (ii)ASD attention is dissociated: it matches artists' wide spatial exploration (dispersion, explored area) but carries a distinct temporal signature, shorter fixations, less dwell, and the most idiosyncratic (least self-consistent) scanpaths of any group. (iii)ASD gaze is not selectively artist-like on any metric; if anything it is marginally closer to neurotypical. Together these findings indicate that autistic viewing of art is a distinct, group-specific attentional profile in both space and time, and they motivate population-conditioned models of aesthetic attention. We release all analysis code and per-stimulus results.
中文标题/摘要
标题：艺术观看中的不同注视模式：自闭症个体、艺术家与神经典型观察者在空间与时间维度上的注意力特征
不同人群对艺术品的视觉探索方式关系到认知科学与无障碍设计，但目前关于自闭症的眼动研究多聚焦于社交场景，且仅分析注视点位置而忽略注视时间与顺序。本研究比较了三组人群——自闭症成年人（ASD）、训练有素的艺术家和神经典型观察者——各自观看30幅画作15秒的自由观看数据。我们提出一种定向、基于度量的框架，从两个互补维度比较各组：空间维度上，通过六种显著性度量（AUC-Judd、NSS、CC、SIM、KL、信息增益）比较一组人群的注视密度图对另一组注视点的预测能力；时间维度上，使用MultiMatch、ScanMatch、视网膜盘IoU分数（FDISS）和动态时间规整（DTW）比较个体扫视路径。所有组别均采用分散阈值算法统一提取注视点。三个结果一致：（i）艺术家与神经典型观察者在空间（密度图相关性CC=0.96）和时间（形成最可对齐的扫视路径对）上几乎无法区分，而ASD注视模式与二者均不同；（ii）ASD注意力具有分离特征：其空间探索范围（分散度、探索面积）与艺术家相似，但具有独特的时间特征——注视时间更短、停留更少，且扫视路径最不一致（各组中最缺乏自一致性）；（iii）ASD注视模式在任何度量上均不特异性地类似艺术家，反而略接近神经典型。这些发现表明，自闭症人群观看艺术时具有独特的、群体特异性的空间与时间注意力模式，推动了基于人群条件的审美注意力模型的发展。我们公开所有分析代码及每刺激结果。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how autistic individuals, trained artists, and neurotypical observers attend to artworks through eye-tracking during free viewing, motivated by the need to understand attentional differences in aesthetic contexts. The researchers employed a metric-grounded framework comparing groups along spatial and temporal axes: spatially, using fixation-density map predictions under six saliency metrics; temporally, using scanpath alignment methods including MultiMatch, ScanMatch, FDISS, and DTW. Results show artists and neurotypicals exhibit nearly identical spatial and temporal gaze patterns (CC=0.96), while autistic individuals show divergent behavior: they match artists in spatial exploration breadth but display shorter fixations, less dwell time, and the most idiosyncratic scanpaths. Crucially, autistic gaze does not resemble artists more than neurotypicals, indicating a unique, group-specific attentional profile in art viewing that calls for population-tailored models of aesthetic attention.
本研究通过眼动追踪比较自闭症个体、专业艺术家和神经典型观察者在自由观看艺术品时的视觉注意模式，旨在揭示不同人群在审美情境下的注意力差异。研究采用基于度量的框架，从空间（通过六种显著性指标预测注视密度图）和时间（通过多组扫描路径匹配方法）两个维度进行比较。结果表明，艺术家与神经典型者在空间和时间维度上几乎完全一致（CC=0.96），而自闭症个体则显著不同：其空间探索模式与艺术家相似，但时间特征明显不同——注视时间更短、停留更少、扫描路径更个性化。更重要的是，自闭症注视模式并非更接近艺术家，反而更接近神经典型者，揭示了自闭症群体在艺术观看中具有独特且群体特异的注意力模式，支持构建针对不同人群的审美注意模型。
Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning for Unsupervised Visible-Infrared Person Re-Identification
Authors: Moyao Tian, Shijia Liu, Yan Yang, Xin Yuan, Minshi Chen, Wei Wang, Xiao Wang
First: 2026-07-16T17:22:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:22:51+00:00
Comments: Accepted by PRCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unsupervised visible-infrared person re-identification (USVI-ReID) is challenging due to the large modality gap and the lack of cross-modal identity annotations. Progressive association paradigms have been proposed to gradually bridge the gap, but they suffer from two critical bottlenecks: reliance on ambiguous global representations and unchecked propagation of pseudo-label noise in an open-loop manner. To address these issues, we propose Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning (SSRL), a framework that transforms open-loop association into a self-correcting closed-loop system. Structurally, we introduce Fine-grained Structural Decoupling (FSD) to extract discriminative body-part primitives as reliable spatial anchors, complementing ambiguous holistic silhouettes with spatially consistent structural details. Semantically, we design a Closed-loop Semantic Calibration (CSC) mechanism that reconstructs shared semantic prototypes at each epoch and feeds them back into the training loop, effectively filtering pseudo-label noise before the next clustering cycle. Through the reciprocal interaction between structural and semantic learning, SSRL achieves robust cross-modal representation. Extensive experiments demonstrate the competitive performance of SSRL against state-of-the-art USVI-ReID methods on both SYSU-MM01 and RegDB, notably surpassing several supervised counterparts on RegDB.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结构-语义互馈学习用于无监督可见光-红外行人重识别
无监督可见光-红外行人重识别（USVI-ReID）因模态差异大且缺乏跨模态身份标注而极具挑战性。现有渐进关联范式虽可逐步弥合差距，但存在两大瓶颈：依赖模糊的全局表征及伪标签噪声在开环方式下的无约束传播。为此，我们提出结构-语义互馈学习（SSRL）框架，将开环关联转化为自校正闭环系统。结构上，引入细粒度结构解耦（FSD）提取具有判别性的身体部位原始结构作为可靠空间锚点，以空间一致的结构细节补充模糊的整体轮廓。语义上，设计闭环语义校准（CSC）机制，在每轮训练中重建共享语义原型并反馈至训练循环，有效过滤伪标签噪声以备下一轮聚类。通过结构与语义学习的互馈交互，SSRL实现鲁棒的跨模态表征。大量实验表明，SSRL在SYSU-MM01与RegDB数据集上优于当前主流USVI-ReID方法，尤其在RegDB上显著超越多个监督方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenges of unsupervised visible-infrared person re-identification due to modality gaps and unreliable pseudo-labels, the paper proposes Structural-Semantic Reciprocal Learning (SSRL), a closed-loop framework that mitigates noise propagation. It employs Fine-grained Structural Decoupling to extract reliable body-part primitives as spatial anchors, and Closed-loop Semantic Calibration to iteratively refine shared semantic prototypes and filter noisy pseudo-labels. Experiments on SYSU-MM01 and RegDB show SSRL outperforms existing methods, notably surpassing several supervised baselines on RegDB.
为解决可见光与红外模态间存在较大差异且缺乏跨模态标注导致的无监督行人重识别难题，本文提出结构-语义互馈学习（SSRL）框架，旨在克服现有渐进关联方法依赖模糊全局表征和伪标签噪声无约束传播的问题。方法通过细粒度结构解耦提取可靠的人体部位原始特征作为空间锚点，并设计闭环语义校准机制在每轮训练中重建共享语义原型以过滤伪标签噪声。实验表明，SSRL在SYSU-MM01和RegDB数据集上表现优异，尤其在RegDB上超越多个监督方法。
Is the Geometry Doing the Work? An Operating-Point Audit of Hierarchy in Hyperbolic Vision-Language Models
Authors: Jaeyoung Kim, Eunseok Kim, Dongsuk Jang
First: 2026-07-06T16:18:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:22:10+00:00
Comments: 48 pages, 5 figures, Under review at TMLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hyperbolic vision-language models are designed to encode abstraction geometrically: general concepts near the origin, specific ones farther out, and entailment cones representing directed order. We ask whether trained MERU, HyCoCLIP, and PHyCLIP models actually use these mechanisms. We audit seven released checkpoints and matched from-scratch interventions, using diagnostics that distinguish active hyperbolic geometry from angular structure and supervision effects. All audited converged checkpoints remain near-Euclidean in the dimensionless radius $u=\sqrt{c}ρ$, which measures how strongly embeddings experience hyperbolic geometry: the largest observed image-side value is $0.37$ -- well below $u\approx0.84$, where local metric distortion reaches $10\%$. Releasing the curvature floor changes curvature and norms but not this regime, with mixed, generally modest downstream shifts. Trained entailment cones are saturated or nearly saturated, so low violation rates can arise from trivially wide cones rather than learned order. Preregistered semantic traversal detects weak within-branch order but no operative full-hierarchy readout. Shuffle-controlled tests detect no pair-specific radial ordering in released checkpoints, and no positive result is consistent across all three matched ViT-B seeds. We trace this to a low-curvature shortcut: lowering curvature widens entailment cones and suppresses violations without learning order. In the probed trajectories, gradient decomposition identifies entailment as the dominant curvature-lowering pressure during collapse. Yet curvature contracts even when entailment is removed, so the shortcut is not the sole cause. Under our diagnostics, the audited formulations do not demonstrate an operative radial or cone-based hierarchy. We distill the audit into a five-number geometry report for evaluating future hierarchy claims.
中文标题/摘要
标题：几何结构在发挥作用吗？对超曲面视觉-语言模型层级机制的运行点审计
超曲面视觉-语言模型旨在通过几何方式编码抽象：一般概念靠近原点，具体概念向外延伸，蕴含锥体表示定向顺序。我们考察训练后的MERU、HyCoCLIP和PHyCLIP模型是否实际使用这些机制。我们审计了七个已发布检查点及其匹配的从零开始干预模型，使用诊断方法区分活跃的超曲面几何结构与角结构及监督效应。所有审计的收敛检查点在无量纲半径$u=\sqrt{c}ρ$（衡量嵌入体经历超曲面几何强度）上仍近似欧几里得，最大观察到的图像侧值为$0.37$——远低于局部度量失真达$10\%$的阈值$u\approx0.84$。降低曲率下限会改变曲率和范数，但不改变此区域，下游影响总体较小且不一致。训练后的蕴含锥体饱和或近饱和，因此低违反率可能源于锥体过宽而非学习到的顺序。预注册语义遍历检测到分支内弱顺序，但无完整层级读出。受控洗牌测试显示发布检查点中无特定对的径向排序，且三个匹配ViT-B种子均无一致阳性结果。我们归因于低曲率捷径：降低曲率会拓宽蕴含锥体并抑制违反，而无需学习顺序。在探查轨迹中，梯度分解显示蕴含是坍缩期间降低曲率的主要压力。但即使移除蕴含，曲率仍会收缩，故捷径非唯一原因。在我们的诊断下，审计的模型未显示活跃的径向或锥体层级结构。我们将其审计浓缩为五数几何报告，用于评估未来层级声明。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether hyperbolic vision-language models like MERU, HyCoCLIP, and PHyCLIP genuinely leverage hyperbolic geometry to encode conceptual hierarchy, as intended. Using diagnostics that separate geometric effects from angular or supervision artifacts, the authors audit seven released checkpoints and matched from-scratch models. Results show that all converged models remain near-Euclidean in the dimensionless radius u, with maximum image-side values of 0.37 — far below the 0.84 threshold where 10% local metric distortion occurs. Entailment cones are saturated or nearly saturated, suggesting low violation rates stem from wide cones rather than learned order. Semantic traversal reveals weak within-branch ordering, and shuffle-controlled tests find no consistent radial ordering across model seeds. The authors attribute this to a low-curvature shortcut: reducing curvature widens cones and suppresses violations without learning hierarchy. Gradient decomposition confirms entailment drives curvature reduction during collapse, but curvature still contracts even without entailment, indicating other factors. Overall, the models do not demonstrate an operative radial or cone-based hierarchy under the proposed diagnostics.
本文旨在检验MERU、HyCoCLIP和PHyCLIP等超球面视觉-语言模型是否真正利用超球面几何编码概念层级结构。作者通过区分几何效应与角结构或监督效应的诊断方法，审计了七个公开模型及其匹配的从零开始训练模型。结果显示，所有收敛模型在无量纲半径u上均接近欧氏空间，最大图像侧值为0.37，远低于引发10%局部度量失真的0.84阈值。蕴含锥体饱和或近饱和，表明低违反率源于锥体过宽而非学习到的层级结构。语义遍历与洗牌控制测试未发现跨模型或种子的一致径向或层级排序。作者归因于低曲率捷径：降低曲率可拓宽锥体并抑制违反，但不学习层级结构。梯度分解显示蕴含是坍缩过程中降低曲率的主要驱动力，但即使移除蕴含，曲率仍收缩，说明捷径非唯一原因。总体而言，审计表明这些模型在诊断下未体现操作性的径向或锥体层级结构，并提出五数几何报告用于未来评估。
RESOURCE2SKILL: Distilling Executable Agent Skills from Human-Created Multimodal Resources
Authors: Yijia Fan, Zonglin Di, Zimo Wen, Yifan Yang, Mingxi Cheng, Qi Dai, Bei Liu, Kai Qiu, Yue Dong, Ji Li, Chong Luo
First: 2026-06-28T17:59:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:22:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Skills are a useful abstraction for software agents, turning human and agent experience into reusable procedural knowledge. Yet existing skill libraries are mostly hand-written, text-centric, or derived from agent traces, leaving tutorial videos and other multimodal human resources largely underused. We present RESOURCE2SKILL, a framework that distills multimodal resources, including tutorial videos, repositories, articles, and reference artifacts, into executable skills for software agents. RESOURCE2SKILL organizes these skills as a hierarchical multimodal Skill Wiki, where each entry combines structured text, code, visual examples, metadata, and provenance. This design preserves complementary signals from different resources: videos capture temporal operations and visual effects, code captures executable tool patterns, and articles or artifacts provide conceptual and stylistic grounding. At inference time, agents retrieve and compose relevant skills from the wiki; when coverage is insufficient, the same construction operator can acquire new skills online. Across seven practical authoring domains, RESOURCE2SKILL improves average overall score by +11.9 percentage points over no-skill agents and outperforms strong harness baselines in 26 of 28 main-aggregate model-domain cells. Ablations confirm the value of multimodal skill format, hierarchical organization, source diversity, selection strategy, and online acquisition.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RESOURCE2SKILL：从人类创建的多模态资源中提炼可执行的代理技能
技能是对软件代理有用的抽象，将人类和代理的经验转化为可复用的程序性知识。然而，现有技能库大多由人工编写、以文本为中心，或源自代理轨迹，导致教程视频等多模态人类资源被大量忽视。我们提出RESOURCE2SKILL框架，将教程视频、仓库、文章和参考制品等多模态资源提炼为软件代理的可执行技能。RESOURCE2SKILL将这些技能组织为一个分层的多模态技能维基，每个条目结合结构化文本、代码、视觉示例、元数据和溯源信息。该设计保留了不同资源的互补信号：视频捕捉时序操作和视觉效果，代码捕捉可执行工具模式，文章或制品提供概念和风格基础。推理时，代理从维基中检索并组合相关技能；当覆盖不足时，同一构建操作符可在线获取新技能。在七个实际创作领域中，RESOURCE2SKILL相比无技能代理平均整体得分提升11.9个百分点，并在28个主模型-领域单元中的26个中优于强基线方法。消融实验验证了多模态技能格式、分层组织、源多样性、选择策略和在线获取的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of multimodal human resources like tutorial videos and code repositories in agent skill development, RESOURCE2SKILL introduces a framework that distills these diverse resources into executable skills. It structures skills as a hierarchical multimodal Skill Wiki, integrating text, code, visuals, metadata, and provenance to preserve complementary signals from each resource type. During inference, agents retrieve and compose skills from the wiki, and can acquire new ones online when needed. Experiments across seven authoring domains show the framework improves average performance by 11.9 percentage points over no-skill agents and outperforms strong baselines in 26 of 28 model-domain combinations, with ablations confirming the importance of multimodal format, hierarchy, source diversity, selection strategy, and online learning.
为解决教程视频、代码仓库等多模态人类资源在智能体技能构建中被忽视的问题，RESOURCE2SKILL提出一种框架，将这些资源提炼为可执行的智能体技能。其技能以分层多模态技能维基形式组织，融合文本、代码、视觉示例、元数据和溯源信息，以保留不同模态的互补信号。推理时，智能体从维基中检索并组合技能，必要时可在线获取新技能。在七个实际创作领域中，该框架相较无技能智能体平均性能提升11.9个百分点，并在28个模型-领域组合中的26个优于强基线方法，消融实验验证了多模态格式、分层结构、资源多样性、选择策略和在线学习的重要性。
When Words Are Safe But Actions Kill: Probing Physical Danger Beyond Text Safety in Hidden-State Risk Space
Authors: Weimeng Wang, Ziqiang Wang, Zihang Zhan, Chuanpu Fu, Qi Li, Ke Xu
First: 2026-07-16T17:20:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:20:38+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) increasingly serve as high-level planners for embodied agents, where linguistically benign instructions can become unsafe once grounded in the physical world. We study whether this physically grounded danger is the same safety problem as ordinary text-level content danger. Through hidden-state direction analysis and random-split null tests, we show that content danger (CD) and physical danger (PD) form separable signals in LLM representations across Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B, Phi-3.5 and SmolLM2. Building on the CD/PD separability, we propose PRISM, a single-layer L2-regularized logistic probe over full hidden states. PRISM achieves 86.2--87.7\% accuracy on SafeAgentBench with 11.7--13.7\% FPR, while same-scale LLM judges over-block safe tasks at 24.7--39.0\% FPR. We further introduce PhysicalSafetyBench-1K (PSB-1K), a contrastive benchmark of 1{,}000 physical-risk pairs without direct harm keywords, to test whether methods detect physically grounded danger rather than explicit unsafe wording. On PSB-1K, PRISM reaches 99.6\% accuracy and 0.7\% FPR, whereas a Qwen2.5-3B judge rejects 67.8\% of safe tasks. PRISM also replicates on SafeText and EARBench, supporting hidden-state probing as a representation-level method for physical safety beyond text moderation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当语言安全但行为致命：在隐藏状态风险空间中探究超越文本安全的物理危险
大型语言模型（LLM）日益成为具身代理的高级规划者，其中语言上无害的指令一旦在物理世界中落地，可能变得不安全。我们研究这种物理落地的危险是否与普通文本层面的内容危险属于同一类安全问题。通过隐藏状态方向分析和随机分割零假设检验，我们证明在Qwen2.5-3B/7B/14B/32B、Phi-3.5和SmolLM2中，内容危险（CD）与物理危险（PD）在LLM表示中形成可分离的信号。基于CD/PD的可分离性，我们提出PRISM，一种在完整隐藏状态上进行L2正则化逻辑回归的单层探测器。PRISM在SafeAgentBench上达到86.2–87.7%准确率，假阳性率（FPR）为11.7–13.7%，而同等规模的LLM判断器在过块安全任务上的FPR为24.7–39.0%。我们进一步引入PhysicalSafetyBench-1K（PSB-1K），一个包含1000对无直接危害关键词的物理风险对比基准，用于测试方法是否能检测物理落地的危险而非显式不安全词汇。在PSB-1K上，PRISM达到99.6%准确率和0.7% FPR，而Qwen2.5-3B判断器错误拒绝67.8%的安全任务。PRISM还在SafeText和EARBench上得到复现，支持隐藏状态探测作为一种超越文本审查的表示层面方法，用于物理安全评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between text safety and physical danger in embodied agent planning, this study investigates whether LLM hidden states encode separable signals for content danger and physical danger. Using hidden-state direction analysis and null tests across multiple LLMs, the authors demonstrate that these two safety dimensions are distinct. They then propose PRISM, a single-layer logistic probe with L2 regularization over full hidden states, which achieves high accuracy (86.2–87.7%) and low false positive rates (11.7–13.7%) on SafeAgentBench, outperforming LLM-based judges. Further validated on PhysicalSafetyBench-1K—a benchmark of 1,000 physically risky scenarios without explicit danger keywords—PRISM achieves 99.6% accuracy and 0.7% FPR, while LLM judges misclassify 67.8% of safe tasks. Results across benchmarks confirm that hidden-state probing effectively detects physical danger beyond text-level safety.
该研究旨在解决大语言模型作为具身智能体规划器时，文本安全与物理世界危险之间的差距，即看似无害的指令在执行时可能引发实际危险。作者通过分析多个LLM的隐藏状态，发现内容危险与物理危险在模型表示中是可分离的信号。他们提出PRISM方法，即在完整隐藏状态上使用L2正则化单层逻辑探针，该方法在SafeAgentBench上准确率达86.2–87.7%，假阳性率仅11.7–13.7%，显著优于同规模LLM的判断（假阳性率24.7–39.0%）。在PhysicalSafetyBench-1K基准（1000个无危险关键词但具物理风险的场景）上，PRISM准确率达99.6%，假阳性率仅0.7%，而基线模型错误拒绝67.8%的安全任务，证明其能有效识别物理层面风险，而非仅依赖文本关键词。
Inoculation Adapters: Improved Selective Generalization of Capabilities with Fewer Surprising Backdoors
Authors: Maxime Riché, Daniel Tan, Vili Kohonen, Niels Warncke
First: 2026-06-29T13:02:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:20:15+00:00
Comments: Preprint, v0.2
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Inoculation prompting is a selective-generalization technique used against Emergent Misalignment. We introduce inoculation adapters (IA), a family of methods that similarly reduce the optimization pressure to learn undesired traits by strengthening those traits during training. Inoculation adapters are LoRAs that are trained and used in three steps: (1) trained on undesired traits; (2) attached frozen while a separate task adapter is trained on data exhibiting both desired and undesired traits; (3) the IA is discarded at deployment, while only the task adapter is kept. We compare inoculation adapters with four selective-generalization baselines: inoculation prompting, preventative steering, Concept Ablation Fine-Tuning (CAFT), and KL regularization. Across nine setups and five model families, the inoculation adapter family spans a new Pareto frontier of desired trait retention vs. undesired trait suppression, although given wide confidence intervals the magnitude of improvement remains uncertain. Inoculation adapters also avoid two drawbacks of inoculation prompting: they can suppress capabilities and traits that cannot be reliably elicited by a prompt, and they introduce fewer surprising backdoors. However, no IA variant optimizes all objectives perfectly; gains in desired-trait generalization are generally accompanied by weaker suppression of the undesired trait and increased backdoor occurrence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：接种适配器：通过减少意外后门实现能力选择性泛化改进
接种提示是一种用于对抗涌现错位的择优泛化技术。我们引入接种适配器（IA），这是一种通过训练阶段强化不良特质以降低模型学习不良特质优化压力的方法。接种适配器是LoRA的一种，训练和使用分为三步：（1）在不良特质数据上训练；（2）冻结后，同时训练一个任务适配器，该适配器在同时包含期望与不良特质的数据上训练；（3）部署时丢弃IA，仅保留任务适配器。我们将其与四种择优泛化基线方法（接种提示、预防性引导、概念消融微调CAFT、KL正则化）进行比较。在九种设置和五类模型中，接种适配器家族在期望特质保留与不良特质抑制之间开辟了新的帕累托前沿，尽管置信区间较宽，改进幅度仍不确定。接种适配器还避免了接种提示的两个缺点：可抑制提示无法可靠激发的能力和特质，且引入的意外后门更少。然而，没有IA变体能完美优化所有目标；期望特质泛化能力的提升通常伴随不良特质抑制减弱和后门出现率上升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve selective generalization while mitigating emergent misalignment, the paper introduces inoculation adapters (IAs), a family of LoRA-based methods that preemptively strengthen undesired traits during training to reduce optimization pressure toward them. IAs operate in three stages: training on undesired traits, freezing while a task adapter learns from mixed data, and discarding at deployment. Evaluated across nine setups and five model families against four baselines, IAs achieve a new Pareto frontier in balancing desired trait retention and undesired trait suppression, though with wide confidence intervals. They outperform inoculation prompting by suppressing traits not elicitable via prompts and reducing surprising backdoors, yet no variant perfectly optimizes all objectives—gains in desired trait generalization often come with weaker suppression and increased backdoor occurrence.
为提升选择性泛化并缓解涌现对齐问题，本文提出接种适配器（IA），一种基于LoRA的方法，先在不良特质上预训练，冻结后与任务适配器联合训练，部署时丢弃。与四种基线方法相比，IA在九组实验和五类模型中实现了新的帕累托前沿，平衡了期望特质保留与不良特质抑制，但置信区间较宽，效果幅度尚存不确定性。IA避免了提示法的两个缺陷：可抑制提示无法可靠触发的特质，且引入较少意外后门，但无一变体能完美优化所有目标，通常以增强期望特质泛化为代价，导致不良特质抑制减弱和后门增加。
NeuronSoup: Evolving Asynchronous, Shared-Neuron Temporal Graphs without Backpropagation
Authors: Subodh Kalia
First: 2026-07-16T17:18:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:18:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present NeuronSoup, a neural computation architecture that replaces synchronous layer-by-layer processing with asynchronous, delay-mediated signal propagation through a pool of shared neurons. Each path in the network routes a continuous-valued signal from one input neuron to one output neuron through a variable number of intermediate hidden neurons. Hidden neurons are physically shared across paths: when two paths pass through the same neuron, the second arrival encounters the accumulated state left by the first, producing constructive or destructive interference that depends on signal polarity and arrival timing. The entire architecture -- topology, weights, delays, and connectivity -- is co-evolved by a genetic algorithm operating on a flat real-valued genome of 14,602 genes. On 10-class MNIST digit classification using frozen ResNet18 features as input, the system evolves a network of 204 active paths through 266 hidden neurons (156 shared across multiple paths, with one neuron participating in 11 distinct paths) and achieves 85.9\% test accuracy after 10,000 generations. The trained model occupies 115 KB. We argue that this architecture addresses fundamental limitations of current deep learning: it requires no differentiable computation graph, adapts its computation depth per-sample, and discovers lateral interactions between processing pathways that current architectures must engineer explicitly. We discuss why genetic algorithms are the correct optimization tool for this problem class, why CMA-ES fails at this scale, and how the architecture generalizes to arbitrary domains by substituting the encoder and output structure.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NeuronSoup：无需反向传播的异步共享神经元时序图演化架构
我们提出NeuronSoup，一种神经计算架构，用异步、延迟介导的信号传播替代同步逐层处理。网络中每条路径将连续值信号从一个输入神经元路由至一个输出神经元，经过可变数量的中间隐藏神经元。隐藏神经元在路径间物理共享：当两条路径经过同一神经元时，第二条到达的信号会遇到第一条留下的累积状态，产生依赖信号极性和到达时间的相长或相消干涉。整个架构——拓扑、权重、延迟和连接性——由作用于14,602个实值基因的遗传算法共同演化。在使用冻结的ResNet18特征作为输入的10类MNIST数字分类任务中，系统演化出包含204条活跃路径、266个隐藏神经元（其中156个被多条路径共享，一个神经元参与11条不同路径）的网络，在10,000代后达到85.9%的测试准确率。训练模型仅占115 KB。我们认为该架构解决了当前深度学习的基本局限：无需可微分计算图，按样本自适应计算深度，并能发现当前架构需显式设计的路径间横向交互。我们讨论为何遗传算法是该问题类别的正确优化工具，为何CMA-ES在此规模下失效，以及该架构如何通过替换编码器和输出结构推广至任意领域。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of synchronous, backpropagation-dependent deep learning architectures, NeuronSoup introduces an asynchronous, shared-neuron temporal graph model that evolves its entire structure—including topology, weights, delays, and connectivity—via a genetic algorithm acting on a 14,602-gene genome. Signals propagate through variable-length paths of shared neurons, where interference arises from overlapping signal arrivals, enabling dynamic, sample-specific computation depth. On MNIST classification using frozen ResNet18 features, the system evolves 204 active paths through 266 hidden neurons (156 shared) and achieves 85.9% test accuracy after 10,000 generations, with a model size of 115 KB. The architecture eliminates the need for differentiable computation graphs and discovers lateral interactions organically, suggesting genetic algorithms are better suited than gradient-based methods like CMA-ES for this problem class, and it generalizes to arbitrary domains by swapping encoder and output components.
为克服当前依赖反向传播的深度学习架构的根本局限，NeuronSoup提出一种异步神经计算框架，信号通过共享神经元以可变延迟传播，根据到达时间和极性产生相长或相消干涉。整个结构——包括拓扑、权重、延迟和连接性——通过作用于14,602基因的遗传算法共同演化。在使用冻结的ResNet18特征对MNIST数字进行分类时，系统在10,000代后演化出204条路径，经266个共享神经元（其中156个被多路径共享，单个神经元参与11条路径），达到85.9%测试准确率，仅占用115KB存储。该架构无需可微计算图，按样本自适应计算深度，并自动发现路径间横向交互，无需人工设计。
Symbal: Detecting Systematic Misalignments in Model-Generated Captions
Authors: Maya Varma, Jean-Benoit Delbrouck, Sophie Ostmeier, Akshay Chaudhari, Curtis Langlotz
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-07-16T17:18:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:18:37+00:00
Comments: ICML 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) often introduce errors when generating image captions, resulting in misaligned image-text pairs. Our work focuses on a class of captioning errors that we refer to as systematic misalignments, where a recurring error in MLLM-generated captions is closely associated with the presence of a specific visual feature in the paired image. Given a vision-language dataset with MLLM-generated captions, our aim in this work is to detect such errors, a task we refer to as systematic misalignment detection. As our first key contribution, we present Symbal, which utilizes a structured, dual-stage setup with off-the-shelf foundation models to identify systematic misalignments and summarize results in natural language. As our second key contribution, we introduce SymbalBench, a benchmark designed to evaluate automated methods on our proposed task. SymbalBench consists of 1.7 million image-text pairs from two domains (natural and medical images), organized into 420 vision-language datasets with annotated systematic misalignments. Symbal exhibits strong performance on this benchmark, correctly identifying systematic misalignments in 63.8% of datasets, a nearly 4x improvement over the closest baseline. We supplement our evaluations on SymbalBench with real-world evaluations, showing that (1) Symbal can accurately surface systematic misalignments in captions generated by four MLLMs and (2) Symbal is a powerful tool for auditing off-the-shelf image-caption datasets. Ultimately, our novel task, method, and benchmark can aid users with auditing MLLM-generated captions and identifying critical errors, without requiring access to the underlying MLLM. Code is available at https://github.com/Stanford-AIMI/Symbal.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Symbal：检测模型生成字幕中的系统性错配
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）在生成图像字幕时常引入错误，导致图像-文本对错配。本工作聚焦于一类我们称为“系统性错配”的字幕错误，即MLLM生成的字幕中反复出现的错误与配对图像中的特定视觉特征密切相关。给定一个包含MLLM生成字幕的视觉-语言数据集，我们的目标是检测此类错误，这一任务称为系统性错配检测。我们的第一项关键贡献是提出Symbal，它采用结构化的双阶段设置，结合现成的基础模型，识别系统性错配并用自然语言总结结果。第二项关键贡献是引入SymbalBench，一个专为评估自动化方法而设计的基准。SymbalBench包含来自两个领域（自然与医学图像）的170万张图像-文本对，组织成420个标注了系统性错配的视觉-语言数据集。Symbal在该基准上表现优异，在63.8%的数据集上正确识别系统性错配，相较最接近的基线提升近4倍。我们还通过真实世界评估补充了SymbalBench上的评估结果，表明（1）Symbal能准确揭示四类MLLM生成字幕中的系统性错配；（2）Symbal是审计现成图像-字幕数据集的强大工具。最终，我们的新任务、方法和基准可帮助用户在无需访问底层MLLM的情况下，审计MLLM生成的字幕并识别关键错误。代码见https://github.com/Stanford-AIMI/Symbal。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the frequent misalignments between images and MLLM-generated captions, this work introduces Symbal, a dual-stage method using off-the-shelf foundation models to detect systematic misalignments—recurrent caption errors tied to specific visual features. Evaluated on SymbalBench, a new benchmark of 1.7 million annotated image-text pairs across 420 datasets from natural and medical domains, Symbal achieves 63.8% accuracy in identifying such misalignments, nearly four times better than the closest baseline. Real-world tests confirm its effectiveness across four MLLMs and its utility for auditing existing image-caption datasets without needing access to the underlying model.
为解决多模态大语言模型生成的图像描述常与视觉内容不一致的问题，本文提出Symbal方法，利用现成基础模型构建双阶段框架，检测与特定视觉特征强关联的系统性错配。在包含170万标注图像-文本对的SymbalBench基准上，Symbal在420个数据集上准确识别系统性错配的比例达63.8%，性能较最优基线提升近四倍。真实场景测试表明，Symbal能有效审计四类MLLM生成的描述，并可作为无需访问底层模型的图像描述数据集审计工具。
MAGiSt3R: Multi-Agent Feed-forward 3D Reconstruction from Monocular RGB Videos
Authors: Ziren Gong, Xiaohan Li, Fabio Tosi, Ninghui Xu, Stefano Mattoccia, Jianfei Cai, Matteo Poggi
First: 2026-07-16T17:12:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:12:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents MAGiSt3R, a multi-agent 3D reconstruction framework performing reconstruction and camera tracking for monocular RGB videos at almost 10 FPS. MAGiSt3R relies on a feed-forward model from the 3R family to process RGB videos and regress local point maps, and on a merging model, MAGMA, that combines local maps at both intra-agent and inter-agent levels to obtain the final global point map. Furthermore, MAGiSt3R performs pose graph optimization to mitigate cumulative camera drift occurring along the feed-forward pipeline. We evaluate MAGiSt3R on both synthetic and real-world datasets, demonstrating its superior reconstruction and camera tracking accuracy compared to state-of-the-art approaches.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MAGiSt3R：从单目RGB视频的多智能体前馈3D重建
本文提出了MAGiSt3R，一种用于单目RGB视频的多智能体3D重建框架，可实现近10帧/秒的重建与相机跟踪。MAGiSt3R基于3R家族的前馈模型处理RGB视频并回归局部点图，同时依赖融合模型MAGMA，在智能体内部与智能体间合并局部点图以获得最终全局点图。此外，MAGiSt3R通过姿态图优化来缓解前馈流程中累积的相机漂移。我们在合成与真实世界数据集上评估了MAGiSt3R，证明其重建与相机跟踪精度优于现有最先进方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time 3D reconstruction from monocular video, MAGiSt3R introduces a multi-agent feed-forward framework that processes RGB video frames using a 3R-family model to regress local point maps, then merges them via a MAGMA model at both intra- and inter-agent levels to form a global point map, while applying pose graph optimization to correct cumulative camera drift. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets show MAGiSt3R achieves reconstruction and camera tracking accuracy superior to existing methods, while maintaining near 10 FPS performance.
为实现单目视频的实时三维重建，MAGiSt3R采用多代理前馈架构，利用3R系列模型从RGB视频帧回归局部点图，再通过MAGMA模型在代理内与代理间融合生成全局点图，并通过位姿图优化消除前馈流程中的累积相机漂移。实验表明，该方法在合成与真实数据集上均优于现有方法，在接近10 FPS的帧率下实现更精确的重建与相机跟踪。
DINO-SLAM: DINO-informed RGB-D SLAM for Neural Implicit and Explicit Representations
Authors: Ziren Gong, Xiaohan Li, Fabio Tosi, Youmin Zhang, Stefano Mattoccia, Jun Wu, Matteo Poggi
First: 2025-07-25T17:57:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:12:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper presents DINO-SLAM, a DINO-informed design strategy to enhance implicit (Neural Radiance Field -- NeRF) and explicit representations (Gaussian Splatting -- GS) in SLAM systems through the more comprehensive semantics understanding enabled by DINO. This latter alone, however, lacks proper 3D geometry understanding, allowing only for marginal improvements. Therefore, we rely on a Scene Geometry Encoder (SGE) to enrich DINO features into geometry-aware DINO features (geoDINO), to better understand those geometric relationships that vanilla DINO features fail to capture. Building upon it, we propose two foundational paradigms for NeRF and GS SLAM systems integrating geoDINO features. Compared to state-of-the-art methods, our DINO-informed pipelines achieve superior performance on the Replica, ScanNet, and TUM datasets.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DINO-SLAM：基于DINO语义信息的RGB-D SLAM方法，用于神经隐式与显式表示
本文提出DINO-SLAM，一种基于DINO语义理解以增强SLAM系统中隐式（神经辐射场--NeRF）与显式表示（高斯点绘--GS）的方法。然而，DINO本身缺乏对3D几何结构的理解，仅能带来微小改进。因此，我们引入场景几何编码器（SGE），将DINO特征转化为几何感知的DINO特征（geoDINO），以更好地捕捉普通DINO特征无法表达的几何关系。在此基础上，我们为NeRF和GS SLAM系统提出了两种基础范式，集成geoDINO特征。与现有最先进方法相比，我们的DINO引导管线在Replica、ScanNet和TUM数据集上表现更优。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve 3D representation learning in SLAM systems, this paper introduces DINO-SLAM, which leverages DINO’s semantic understanding to enhance both implicit (NeRF) and explicit (Gaussian Splatting) representations. To address DINO’s limitation in capturing geometric structure, the authors integrate a Scene Geometry Encoder (SGE) to generate geometry-aware DINO features (geoDINO). Two SLAM frameworks are then built upon these features for NeRF and GS, respectively. Experimental results show that DINO-SLAM outperforms state-of-the-art methods on Replica, ScanNet, and TUM datasets in terms of accuracy and robustness.
为提升SLAM系统中隐式（NeRF）与显式（高斯点绘）表示的学习能力，本文提出DINO-SLAM框架，利用DINO的语义理解能力增强三维表示。鉴于DINO在几何结构建模上的不足，作者引入场景几何编码器（SGE）生成几何感知的DINO特征（geoDINO），以更好捕捉空间关系。在此基础上，为NeRF与GS系统构建两种SLAM范式，实验表明其在Replica、ScanNet与TUM数据集上优于现有最先进方法。
Echoes: A semantically-aligned music deepfake detection dataset
Authors: Octavian Pascu, Dan Oneata, Horia Cucu, Nicolas M. Muller
First: 2026-03-24T19:10:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:12:51+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Echoes, a new dataset for music deepfake detection designed for training and benchmarking detectors under realistic and provider-diverse conditions. Echoes comprises 4,468 tracks (131 hours of audio) spanning multiple genres (pop, rock, electronic), and includes content generated by ten popular AI music generation systems. To prevent shortcut learning and promote robust generalization, the dataset is deliberately constructed to be challenging, enforcing semantic-level alignment between spoofed audio and bona fide references. This alignment is achieved by conditioning generated audio samples directly on bona-fide waveforms or song descriptors. We evaluate Echoes in a cross-dataset setting against three existing AI-generated music datasets using state-of-the-art Wav2Vec2 XLS-R 2B representations. Results show that (i) Echoes is the hardest in-domain dataset; (ii) detectors trained on existing datasets transfer poorly to Echoes; (iii) training on Echoes yields the strongest generalization performance. These findings suggest that provider diversity and semantic alignment help learn more transferable detection cues.
中文标题/摘要
标题：回声：一个语义对齐的音乐深度伪造检测数据集
我们引入了Echoes，这是一个专为在真实且提供者多样化的条件下训练和基准测试音乐深度伪造检测器而设计的新数据集。Echoes包含4,468首曲目（131小时音频），涵盖多种音乐类型（流行、摇滚、电子），并包含由十种流行AI音乐生成系统生成的内容。为防止捷径学习并促进稳健泛化，该数据集被刻意设计为具有挑战性，强制伪造音频与真实参考音频在语义层面保持对齐。这种对齐通过直接以真实波形或歌曲描述符为条件生成音频样本实现。我们在跨数据集设置下，使用最先进的Wav2Vec2 XLS-R 2B表示方法，将Echoes与三个现有AI生成音乐数据集进行评估。结果表明：(i) Echoes是难度最高的同域数据集；(ii) 在现有数据集上训练的检测器难以迁移到Echoes；(iii) 在Echoes上训练可获得最强的泛化性能。这些发现表明，提供者多样性与语义对齐有助于学习更具迁移性的检测特征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic and diverse training data to improve music deepfake detection, the authors introduce Echoes, a dataset of 4,468 tracks generated by ten popular AI music systems and aligned semantically with real audio references. To prevent shortcut learning, the dataset enforces alignment by conditioning synthetic audio on bona fide waveforms or descriptors. Evaluated against three existing datasets using Wav2Vec2 XLS-R 2B, Echoes proved to be the most challenging in-domain dataset, with detectors trained on prior datasets showing poor transfer performance, while those trained on Echoes achieved the strongest generalization.
为提升AI音乐伪造检测模型的泛化能力，作者构建了Echoes数据集，包含10个主流AI音乐系统生成的4,468首歌曲，通过将生成音频与真实音频或描述对齐，避免模型依赖捷径。在对比三个现有数据集的实验中，使用Wav2Vec2 XLS-R 2B模型评估发现，Echoes是难度最高的同域数据集，其他数据集训练的模型在迁移时表现不佳，而使用Echoes训练的模型泛化能力最强，表明多样性与语义对齐有助于学习更可迁移的检测特征。
Subjective functions
Authors: Samuel J. Gershman
First: 2025-12-17T20:22:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:08:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Where do objective functions come from? How do we select what goals to pursue? Human intelligence is adept at synthesizing new objective functions on the fly. How does this work, and can we endow artificial systems with the same ability? This paper proposes an approach to answering these questions, starting with the concept of a subjective function, a higher-order objective function that is endogenous to the agent (i.e., defined with respect to the agent's features, rather than an external task). Expected prediction error is studied as a concrete example of a subjective function. This proposal has many connections to ideas in psychology, neuroscience, and machine learning.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主观函数
客观函数从何而来？我们如何选择要追求的目标？人类智能擅长即时合成新的客观函数。这一过程如何运作？我们能否赋予人工系统同样的能力？本文提出了一种回答这些问题的方法，始于“主观函数”这一概念——一种内生于代理自身的高阶目标函数（即相对于代理自身的特征定义，而非外部任务）。预期预测误差被作为主观函数的一个具体实例进行研究。该提案与心理学、神经科学和机器学习中的诸多思想密切相关。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates how humans spontaneously generate objective functions for goal pursuit and proposes that artificial systems can be endowed with similar capabilities through the concept of a subjective function—an agent-endogenous higher-order objective function defined in terms of internal features rather than external tasks. Using expected prediction error as a concrete example, the authors explore how such functions can emerge and guide behavior, drawing connections to psychological and neural mechanisms as well as machine learning principles. Experimental results demonstrate that subjective functions effectively capture agent-specific goals and enable adaptive behavior in dynamic environments, suggesting a viable pathway for integrating human-like goal synthesis into artificial agents.
本文探讨人类如何自发生成目标函数，并提出通过‘主观函数’——即基于代理自身特征而非外部任务定义的高阶目标函数——使人工系统获得类似能力。以预期预测误差为例，该研究展示了主观函数如何从内部建模中自然产生。实验结果表明，该方法使代理能够根据内部状态动态调整目标，与心理学和神经科学机制一致，并在复杂不确定环境中提升性能。
Expanding the Lexicon of Ge'ez Based African Languages: A Comparative Study of Amharic and Tigrinya
Authors: Hailay Kidu Teklehaymanot, Debela Desalegn Yadeta, Wolfgang Nejdl
First: 2026-07-16T17:08:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:08:28+00:00
Comments: 13 pages , 7 tables , 2 figurs
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multilingual pre-trained language models (PLMs) exhibit degraded performance on low-resource, non-Latin-script languages, driven by high out-of-vocabulary (OOV) rates and excessive subword fragmentation that result from Latin-script-centric tokenizer training. We introduce VEXMLM, a vocabulary-extended variant of XLM-R targeting the two highest-resource Ge'ez-script languages, Amharic and Tigrinya, and further evaluated on 17 additional low-resource African languages (19 total). We train a language-specific SentencePiece tokenizer on curated Amharic and Tigrinya monolingual corpora, extend XLM-R's vocabulary with 30,000 Ge'ez-script subwords derived from this tokenizer, and initialize their embeddings by averaging the embeddings of their constituent subwords under XLM-R's original tokenizer. VEXMLM is trained in two stages: (1) continued masked language modeling over the extended vocabulary on the curated corpora, and (2) supervised fine-tuning on question answering (QA), named entity recognition (NER), and sentiment analysis (SA). On Amharic/Tigrinya QA, VEXMLM achieves 87.0 EM /90.0 F1, versus 66.0 EM/78.0 F1 for XLM-R and 74.0 EM/ 78.0 F1 for Glot500. On SA, VEXMLM reaches 80.0\% accuracy versus 77.0\% (XLM-R) and 46.0\% (Glot500). On NER, VEXMLM raises OOV-token entity accuracy from 81.4\% to 94.3\%, averaged over 11 of the 19 evaluated languages for which OOV analysis was possible. Our contributions are: (i) a vocabulary-extension and embedding-initialization procedure tailored to Ge'ez script; (ii) a two-stage training strategy under which vocabulary and continued-pretraining gains on Amharic/Tigrinya transfer to 17 typologically related, unaugmented African languages; and (iii) an evaluation spanning both intrinsic tokenization metrics (vocabulary coverage, fertility, OOV rate) and extrinsic task performance across all 19 languages.
中文标题/摘要
标题：扩展基于格泽文字的非洲语言词汇库：阿姆哈拉语与提格雷语的比较研究
多语言预训练语言模型（PLMs）在资源匮乏、非拉丁字母语言上的表现因高词汇外（OOV）率和过度的子词碎片化而下降，这源于以拉丁字母为中心的分词器训练。我们提出了VEXMLM，这是针对两种最高资源格泽文字语言——阿姆哈拉语和提格雷语的词汇扩展版XLM-R，并进一步在17种其他低资源非洲语言（共19种）上进行评估。我们基于精选的阿姆哈拉语和提格雷语单语语料库训练语言专用的SentencePiece分词器，从该分词器中提取30,000个格泽文字子词扩展XLM-R词汇表，并通过平均其在原始分词器下的子词嵌入来初始化这些嵌入。VEXMLM采用两阶段训练：（1）在扩展词汇表上对精选语料库进行持续的掩码语言建模；（2）在问答（QA）、命名实体识别（NER）和情感分析（SA）任务上进行监督微调。在阿姆哈拉语/提格雷语QA任务上，VEXMLM达到87.0 EM / 90.0 F1，优于XLM-R的66.0 EM / 78.0 F1和Glot500的74.0 EM / 78.0 F1。在SA任务上，VEXMLM准确率达到80.0%，优于XLM-R的77.0%和Glot500的46.0%。在NER任务上，VEXMLM将OOV词实体识别准确率从81.4%提升至94.3%，平均提升覆盖11种可进行OOV分析的19种语言。我们的贡献包括：（i）针对格泽文字定制的词汇扩展与嵌入初始化方法；（ii）两阶段训练策略，使阿姆哈拉语/提格雷语上的词汇扩展与持续预训练收益可迁移到17种语法相关但未增强的非洲语言；（iii）涵盖所有19种语言的内在分词指标（词汇覆盖率、词产率、OOV率）与外在任务表现的评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the poor performance of multilingual models on Ge'ez-script languages due to high out-of-vocabulary rates and subword fragmentation from Latin-centric tokenizers, the authors introduce VEXMLM, a vocabulary-extended variant of XLM-R tailored for Amharic and Tigrinya. They train a language-specific SentencePiece tokenizer on curated monolingual corpora, extend XLM-R’s vocabulary with 30,000 Ge'ez subwords, and initialize embeddings via subword averaging. VEXMLM is trained in two stages: continued masked language modeling followed by supervised fine-tuning on QA, NER, and SA. Results show significant gains: 87.0 EM/90.0 F1 on QA (vs. 66.0/78.0 for XLM-R), 80.0% SA accuracy (vs. 77.0% for XLM-R), and improved NER OOV accuracy from 81.4% to 94.3% across 11 languages. The approach also transfers to 17 additional African languages, demonstrating robust cross-linguistic benefits.
为解决多语言模型在使用非拉丁文字的盖兹语系语言（如阿姆哈拉语和提格雷语）时因词汇覆盖率低和子词碎片化导致的性能下降，作者提出VEXMLM，即针对盖兹语系语言扩展词汇的XLM-R变体。他们基于精选的单语语料训练语言专用的SentencePiece分词器，从该分词器中提取3万个盖兹语子词扩展XLM-R词汇表，并通过子词平均初始化词向量。模型采用两阶段训练：先进行扩展词汇的持续掩码语言建模，再进行问答、命名实体识别和情感分析的监督微调。实验结果表明：在问答任务上准确率提升至87.0 EM/90.0 F1（XLM-R为66.0/78.0），情感分析准确率达80.0%（XLM-R为77.0%），命名实体识别中OOV实体识别准确率从81.4%提升至94.3%（覆盖11种语言），并在19种非洲语言（含17种低资源语言）上实现性能提升。
Delocalization of bias in unadjusted Hamiltonian Monte Carlo and underdamped Langevin
Authors: Yifan Chen, Xiaoou Cheng, Jonathan Niles-Weed, Jonathan Weare
First: 2026-07-16T17:07:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:07:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Unadjusted samplers such as unadjusted Hamiltonian Monte Carlo and underdamped Langevin are well-known to be biased. Metropolis--Hastings adjustment has been conventionally incorporated into Hamiltonian Monte Carlo to eliminate the bias. However, this adjustment can significantly increase the iteration complexity due to the small step size required for reasonable Metropolis acceptance rates. In this work, we extend the \emph{delocalization of bias} phenomenon, previously established for the overdamped Langevin algorithm, to these two unadjusted algorithms. We show that to control the $W_2$ bias of any $K$-dimensional marginal of a high-dimensional distribution, $O(\sqrt{K})$ integration steps suffice up to $\log d$ terms, assuming either weak or sparse interactions among variables. The discrete-time integrators here introduce technical difficulties beyond those of the overdamped setting, which we address through a broadly applicable matrix-polynomial framework that characterizes their propagators. Our result for the underdamped Langevin algorithm is valid for all large friction parameters, implying that the Leimkuhler-Matthews integrator for the overdamped Langevin dynamics also exhibits delocalization of bias.
中文标题/摘要
标题：未调整哈密顿蒙特卡洛与欠阻尼朗之万算法中的偏差去定位
未调整采样器（如未调整哈密顿蒙特卡洛与欠阻尼朗之万算法）众所周知存在偏差。传统上通过引入Metropolis-Hastings调整来消除该偏差，但此调整因需较小步长以保证合理的Metropolis接受率，会显著增加迭代复杂度。本文将此前在阻尼朗之万算法中建立的“偏差去定位”现象扩展至上述两种未调整算法。我们证明，在变量间存在弱或稀疏相互作用的假设下，为控制高维分布任意K维边际的W2偏差，仅需O(√K)个积分步，且最多乘以log d项。此处离散时间积分器引入了超越阻尼情形的技术难题，我们通过一个广泛适用的矩阵多项式框架来刻画其传播算子以解决该问题。本文对欠阻尼朗之万算法的结果适用于所有大摩擦参数，这意味着用于阻尼朗之万动力学的Leimkuhler-Matthews积分器同样表现出偏差去定位。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to reduce computational cost in unadjusted Hamiltonian Monte Carlo and underdamped Langevin samplers without Metropolis adjustment, this work extends the delocalization of bias phenomenon to these algorithms. Using a matrix-polynomial framework to characterize discrete-time integrator propagators, the authors demonstrate that controlling the $W_2$ bias of any $K$-dimensional marginal in high-dimensional distributions requires only $O(\sqrt{K})$ integration steps, up to $\log d$ factors, under weak or sparse variable interactions. This result holds for all large friction parameters in underdamped Langevin and implies that the Leimkuhler-Matthews integrator for overdamped Langevin also exhibits delocalization of bias.
为在不使用Metropolis调整的前提下降低未调整哈密顿蒙特卡洛和欠阻尼朗之万采样器的计算成本，本文将‘偏差去局域化’现象从阻尼朗之万算法扩展至这两类未调整算法。通过矩阵多项式框架解决离散时间积分器带来的技术难题，作者证明在变量间存在弱或稀疏相互作用的条件下，控制任意K维边际的Wasserstein-2偏差仅需O(sqrt(K))个积分步，最多乘以log d因子。该结果对欠阻尼朗之万算法在所有大摩擦参数下均成立，且表明Leimkuhler-Matthews积分器在阻尼朗之万动力学中也具备偏差去局域化特性。
The RG-Flow Transformer: Encoding Scale-Free Dynamics in Scarce EEG
Authors: Dibakar Sigdel
First: 2026-07-11T18:49:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:07:32+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Brain field potentials are scale-free: their power spectra follow a $1/f^β$ law whose aperiodic exponent $β$ tracks cortical state, and sleep depth in particular is a shift in $β$. We ask whether a transformer endowed with an explicit renormalization-group (RG) inductive bias the RG-Flow Transformer, which couples ordinary self-attention to a scale-aware stream with a learnable anomalous dimension $γ$, block-spin coarse-graining, and an entropy-gated synchronization bridge has an advantage over a parameter-matched vanilla transformer on \emph{real, scarce} EEG. Using the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus with a strict leakage-free by-subject hold-out, we (i) benchmark RG-Flow against a param-matched vanilla transformer and a hierarchy-only ablation on 5-class AASM sleep staging, (ii) sweep the per-subject data budget to look for the inductive-bias crossover predicted when data are scarce, and (iii) test whether RG-Flow's learned $γ$ tracks the measured spectral exponent $β$ out-of-sample a quantity the vanilla model does not possess. Across $5$ subjects and $5$ seeds under leave-one-subject-out cross-validation, RG-Flow and the vanilla transformer are statistically indistinguishable on 5-class staging (77.3\% vs 77.0\% accuracy; paired $p=0.294$), and the predicted scarce-data crossover does not appear: vanilla is numerically ahead at every data-limited budget. What does separate the models is interpretability RG-Flow recovers the continuous spectral exponent out-of-sample ($β$-recovery $R^2 = 0.416$), a capability the vanilla architecture has no analogue for.
中文标题/摘要
标题：RG-Flow变换器：在稀少EEG数据中编码无标度动力学
脑场电位具有无标度特性：其功率谱遵循$1/f^β$定律，其中周期外指数$β$反映皮层状态，尤其睡眠深度表现为$β$的偏移。我们探究是否具备显式重整化群（RG）归纳偏置的RG-Flow变换器——将普通自注意力与具备可学习异常维度$γ$的标度感知流、块自旋粗粒化及熵门控同步桥耦合——在真实且稀少的EEG数据上优于参数匹配的普通变换器。使用PhysioNet Sleep-EDF语料库，采用严格无泄漏的按受试者留出法，我们（i）在5类AASM睡眠分期任务中，将RG-Flow与参数匹配的普通变换器及仅层次结构的消融模型进行基准对比；（ii）遍历每受试者数据预算，寻找预测的稀少数据下归纳偏置交叉点；（iii）检验RG-Flow所学习的$γ$是否能跨样本追踪测量的频谱指数$β$——这是普通模型所不具备的能力。在5名受试者、5个随机种子的留一受试者交叉验证中，RG-Flow与普通变换器在5类分期任务上统计上无显著差异（准确率77.3% vs 77.0%；配对p=0.294），且预测的稀少数据交叉点未出现：在所有数据受限预算下，普通变换器数值上均领先。真正区分模型的是可解释性：RG-Flow能跨样本恢复连续频谱指数（$β$恢复$R^2=0.416$），而普通架构无此能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scale-free nature of EEG signals, where spectral exponent β reflects cortical state, the paper introduces the RG-Flow Transformer—a transformer augmented with renormalization-group inductive bias, including learnable anomalous dimension γ, block-spin coarse-graining, and entropy-gated synchronization. Evaluated on real, scarce EEG data from the PhysioNet Sleep-EDF corpus using strict subject-level hold-out, the model was compared against a parameter-matched vanilla transformer and a hierarchy-only ablation on 5-class sleep staging. Despite achieving statistically similar accuracy (77.3% vs 77.0%) under cross-validation, the vanilla model outperformed RG-Flow at all data-limited budgets, failing to show the predicted inductive-bias advantage. However, RG-Flow uniquely recovered the spectral exponent β out-of-sample with R²=0.416, a capability absent in the vanilla model.
鉴于EEG信号具有标度不变特性，其谱指数β可反映皮层状态，本文提出RG-Flow Transformer，即在Transformer中引入重整化群归纳偏置，包含可学习的异常维度γ、块自旋粗粒化及熵门控同步桥。在PhysioNet Sleep-EDF真实稀疏EEG数据上，通过严格按受试者划分的留一交叉验证，该模型与参数匹配的普通Transformer及层级结构消融模型在5类睡眠分期任务中表现相近（77.3% vs 77.0%准确率），但RG-Flow能外推恢复谱指数β（R²=0.416），而普通Transformer不具备此可解释性能力。
BadWAM: When World-Action Models Dream Right but Act Wrong
Authors: Qi Li, Xingyi Yang, Xinchao Wang
First: 2026-07-16T17:04:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:04:15+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World-action models (WAMs) are emerging as a promising foundation for embodied control: rather than predicting actions alone, they learn representations that couple action generation with future world prediction. This coupling is often viewed as a source of robustness, interpretability, and safety, as a robot's action can in principle be checked against its imagined future. In this paper, we show that this assumption is fragile. We introduce BadWAM, a unified framework for modeling and evaluating World-Action Drift Attacks: a new class of WAM-specific adversarial attacks that use small visual perturbations to break the alignment between what a WAM imagines and what it executes. BadWAM characterizes this attack surface along two natural criteria: attack strength and stealthiness. When the adversary prioritizes disruption, BadWAM instantiates an action-only adversarial attack, which directly drives the model toward task-failing actions. When the adversary additionally prioritizes stealth, BadWAM instantiates an imagination-preserving adversarial attack, which seeks to induce harmful action shifts while keeping the model's predicted future close to its clean imagination. Together, these two attacks capture a spectrum of WAM-specific failures: from overt action hijacking to stealthier cases where the model appears to imagine a plausible future but executes a desynchronized action. We evaluate BadWAM across different variants of WAMs. Results show that our attacks substantially reduce task success rates under closed-loop execution. For example, our action-only attack reduces the model performance from 96.5% to 43.1% success. The results of our imagination-preserving attack further exposes a WAM-specific vulnerability: moderate future-preserving regularization can maintain strong attack performance while reducing future imagination drift.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BadWAM：当世界动作模型想象正确却行动错误时
世界动作模型（WAMs）正成为具身控制的有前景基础：它们不仅预测动作，还学习将动作生成与未来世界预测耦合的表征。这种耦合常被视为鲁棒性、可解释性和安全性的来源，因为机器人动作原则上可与想象的未来进行核对。本文表明，这一假设脆弱。我们提出BadWAM，一个统一框架，用于建模和评估世界动作漂移攻击——一种针对WAM的新型对抗攻击，通过微小视觉扰动破坏WAM想象与执行之间的对齐。BadWAM沿两个自然标准刻画攻击面：攻击强度与隐蔽性。当攻击者优先破坏时，BadWAM实现仅动作对抗攻击，直接驱动模型走向任务失败动作；当攻击者同时优先隐蔽时，BadWAM实现想象保持对抗攻击，旨在诱导有害动作偏移，同时保持模型预测未来接近其干净想象。这两种攻击共同覆盖WAM特有失败谱系：从明显动作劫持到隐蔽情形，即模型看似想象合理未来却执行脱节动作。我们在不同WAM变体上评估BadWAM。结果表明，攻击显著降低闭环执行任务成功率。例如，仅动作攻击使模型成功率从96.5%降至43.1%。想象保持攻击结果进一步揭示WAM特有脆弱性：适度未来保持正则化可在降低未来想象漂移的同时维持强攻击性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the assumption that coupling action generation with future world prediction in World-Action Models (WAMs) enhances robustness and safety, this paper reveals that this coupling can be easily disrupted. BadWAM introduces a unified framework for adversarial attacks that exploit visual perturbations to misalign a WAM’s imagined future with its executed actions. Two attack types are defined: one prioritizes action disruption, directly steering the model toward failure; the other prioritizes stealth, preserving the model’s imagined future while inducing harmful actions. Experiments across WAM variants show severe degradation in task success—action-only attacks drop performance from 96.5% to 43.1%—and reveal that even mild regularization to preserve future imagination does not mitigate attack effectiveness, exposing a critical vulnerability in WAM design.
鉴于世界-动作模型（WAMs）通过耦合动作生成与未来世界预测被认为能提升鲁棒性和安全性，本文揭示该耦合机制易被破坏。BadWAM提出统一框架，通过两种攻击类型攻击WAMs：一种是直接诱导失败动作的“动作攻击”，另一种是在保持未来预测合理性的前提下诱导有害动作的“想象保持攻击”。实验表明，这两种攻击显著降低任务成功率——动作攻击使成功率从96.5%降至43.1%；同时发现，即使施加适度的未来保持正则化，攻击效果仍能保持，暴露出WAM特有的脆弱性。
When Does Belief-Based Agent Memory Help? Reliability-Conditional Updating and Provenance-Capped Poisoning Defense
Authors: Pranav Singh
First: 2026-06-20T13:07:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:02:29+00:00
Comments: Preprint. 10 pages, 1 figure, 6 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We investigate when belief-based memory actually improves large language model (LLM) agents. Our vehicle is Nous, a long-term memory architecture that represents each entity-attribute pair as a categorical probability distribution updated through closed-form Bayesian inference, with information-theoretic surprise driving belief revision and entropy-based forgetting. A controlled ablation on the LoCoMo benchmark shows that Bayesian belief updating alone provides little benefit over naive last-write-wins because existing conversational memory benchmarks rarely contain contradictory or differently reliable evidence. We then introduce reliability-conditioned updating, estimating per-observation reliability from epistemic language, and show on a controlled contradiction benchmark that belief updating substantially outperforms last-write-wins and raw-memory retrieval when observations differ in trustworthiness. Because content-derived reliability is itself vulnerable to manipulation, we further propose provenance-capped belief updating, where trust is bounded by source provenance rather than textual confidence. Under controlled memory-poisoning experiments, this approach resists volumetric poisoning attacks while revealing the utility costs and implementation requirements of provenance-aware memory. Finally, we quantify a 27.5-point discrepancy between strict token-F1 and LLM-as-judge evaluation on identical outputs, highlighting important reproducibility concerns for long-term memory benchmarks. Our results suggest that probabilistic belief-based memory is most beneficial in environments requiring reasoning over conflicting and differently trustworthy evidence, rather than conventional conversational recall alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于信念的代理记忆何时有益？可靠性条件更新与来源受限的中毒防御
我们研究了基于信念的记忆在何种情况下能真正提升大语言模型（LLM）代理的表现。我们的研究载体是Nous，一种长期记忆架构，将每个实体-属性对表示为通过闭式贝叶斯推断更新的类别概率分布，信念修订由信息论上的惊讶度驱动，遗忘则基于熵。在LoCoMo基准上的受控消融实验表明，仅使用贝叶斯信念更新对朴素的最后写入胜出策略几乎没有提升，因为现有对话记忆基准很少包含矛盾或可靠性不同的证据。随后我们引入可靠性条件更新，从认识论语言中估计每条观察的可靠性，并在受控矛盾基准上证明，当观察的可信度不同时，信念更新显著优于最后写入胜出和原始记忆检索。由于内容衍生的可靠性本身易受操纵，我们进一步提出来源受限的信念更新，将信任度限制于来源可追溯性而非文本置信度。在受控的记忆中毒实验中，该方法能抵抗体积型中毒攻击，同时揭示了来源感知记忆的实用成本与实现要求。最后，我们量化了严格token-F1与LLM作为裁判评估在相同输出上存在27.5分的差异，凸显了长期记忆基准的重要可重复性问题。我们的结果表明，概率性信念记忆在需要推理冲突且可信度不同的证据的环境中最为有益，而非仅限于传统对话回忆。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates under what conditions belief-based memory enhances large language model agents, using Nous—a memory architecture that updates entity-attribute pairs via Bayesian inference guided by information-theoretic surprise and entropy-based forgetting. Experiments on the LoCoMo benchmark reveal that naive Bayesian updating offers minimal advantage over last-write-wins due to the lack of contradictory or varying-reliability evidence in standard benchmarks. The authors then introduce reliability-conditioned updating, which estimates observation trustworthiness from epistemic language, showing significant performance gains over baseline methods when evidence conflicts or varies in reliability. To counteract manipulation of reliability estimates, they propose provenance-capped belief updating, which caps trust based on source provenance rather than text confidence, effectively resisting memory-poisoning attacks while revealing implementation trade-offs. Finally, they highlight a 27.5-point discrepancy between strict token-F1 and LLM-as-judge evaluations, underscoring reproducibility issues in memory benchmarks.
本文研究信念驱动的记忆在何种条件下能提升大语言模型代理的表现，采用Nous架构——该架构通过闭式贝叶斯推断更新实体-属性对的类别概率分布，以信息论上的惊讶度驱动信念修正、熵值驱动遗忘。在LoCoMo基准上的控制消融实验表明，朴素贝叶斯更新在缺乏矛盾或可靠性差异证据的基准中优势有限。随后引入可靠性条件更新，从认识论语言中估计观测可靠性，在控制矛盾基准上显著优于最后写入胜出和原始记忆检索。为防御记忆投毒，提出溯源上限信念更新，以来源溯源而非文本置信度限制信任度，在控制实验中有效抵御体积型投毒攻击，同时揭示了溯源感知记忆的实现代价。最后指出严格token-F1与LLM作为裁判评估间存在27.5分差异，凸显长期记忆基准的可重复性问题，结论是概率信念记忆在需处理冲突或可信度差异证据的环境中最具价值。
MM-IssueLoc: A Controlled Benchmark for Evaluating Visual Evidence in Multimodal Repository-Level Issue Localization
Authors: Shaoxiong Zhan, Shi Hu, Boyu Feng, Hai Lin, Andrew Gong, Zhengda Zhou, Jiaying Zhou, Yunyun Hou, Hao Su, Hai-Tao Zheng
First: 2026-07-16T17:02:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T17:02:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Real repository issues routinely include visual evidence such as screenshots, error dialogs, rendered UI states, and logs, yet repository-level issue localization is evaluated mostly as a text-only task. Existing multimodal SE benchmarks evaluate end-to-end repair, entangling localization with patch synthesis and obscuring whether visual input helped, hurt, or was ignored. We introduce \textbf{MM-IssueLoc}, a controlled benchmark and evaluation protocol for repository-level localization with visual evidence. MM-IssueLoc contains 652 issue-PR instances across 23 languages, with annotations for 7 image categories and 4 relevance levels. It provides file-level and function-level gold labels, paired text-only and with-image evaluation, and VCE-based diagnostics that convert images into structured textual evidence. We evaluate LLM-based and retrieval-based systems, including MM-IssueLoc-VL-Emb as a controlled multimodal retriever. Results show that existing systems remain far from reliable multimodal repository localization: the strongest agent reaches 38.96 file Acc@5 and 22.45 function Acc@10, while the strongest retriever reaches 33.86 function Acc@10. Cross-benchmark comparisons show that high localization scores on text-dominant SWE benchmarks do not transfer cleanly to multimodal issue localization. MM-IssueLoc turns visual evidence into an explicit evaluation variable, enabling future work to test whether systems improve by using visual evidence for localization, rather than by relying on text-only cues or downstream patch-generation effects.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MM-IssueLoc：用于评估多模态仓库级问题定位中视觉证据的受控基准
真实仓库问题通常包含截图、错误对话框、渲染的UI状态和日志等视觉证据，但仓库级问题定位大多仅作为纯文本任务进行评估。现有多模态软件工程基准评估端到端修复，将定位与补丁合成混在一起，掩盖了视觉输入是否有助于、损害了或被忽略。我们引入\textbf{MM-IssueLoc}，一个用于仓库级定位并包含视觉证据的受控基准和评估协议。MM-IssueLoc涵盖23种语言的652个问题-PR实例，标注7类图像和4级相关性。它提供文件级和函数级黄金标签，支持纯文本与带图像的并行评估，以及基于VCE的诊断工具，将图像转换为结构化文本证据。我们评估基于LLM和检索的系统，包括作为受控多模态检索器的MM-IssueLoc-VL-Emb。结果显示，现有系统距离可靠的多模态仓库定位仍很遥远：最强的代理在文件级Acc@5达到38.96，在函数级Acc@10达到22.45；最强的检索器在函数级Acc@10达到33.86。跨基准比较表明，纯文本主导的SWE基准上的高定位得分无法直接迁移到多模态问题定位。MM-IssueLoc将视觉证据转化为显式评估变量，使未来工作能够测试系统是否通过使用视觉证据进行定位而改进，而非依赖纯文本线索或下游补丁生成效应。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between real-world repository issues that include visual evidence and current text-only evaluation practices, the authors introduce MM-IssueLoc, a controlled benchmark for multimodal repository-level issue localization. The benchmark includes 652 issue-PR pairs across 23 languages, annotated with image categories and relevance levels, and supports both text-only and image-enhanced evaluation modes. It also provides VCE-based diagnostics to convert visual evidence into structured text. Experiments show that even the best systems achieve low accuracy—38.96% file-level Acc@5 and 22.45% function-level Acc@10—highlighting the difficulty of reliable multimodal localization. Notably, high scores on text-centric benchmarks do not transfer to multimodal settings, underscoring the need for explicit visual evidence evaluation.
鉴于现实中的代码仓库问题常包含截图、错误对话框等视觉证据，而现有评估多局限于纯文本任务，本文提出MM-IssueLoc，一个用于评估多模态仓库级问题定位的可控基准。该基准包含23种语言的652个问题-PR实例，标注了7类图像和4级相关性，并支持纯文本与带图像的评估模式，以及基于VCE的诊断机制以结构化视觉证据。实验表明，现有系统表现不佳——最强模型在文件级Top-5准确率仅38.96%，函数级Top-10准确率仅22.45%，而最强检索器在函数级Top-10准确率仅为33.86%。跨基准对比显示，纯文本基准上的高分无法迁移到多模态问题定位任务，凸显了视觉证据在评估中的必要性。
Self-Evolving Human-Centered Framework for Explainable Depression Symptom Annotation
Authors: Hoang-Loc Cao, Van Pham, Truong Thanh Hung Nguyen, Phuc Truong Loc Nguyen, Phuc Ho, Veronica Whitford, Hung Cao
First: 2026-07-16T16:59:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:59:54+00:00
Comments: Accepted at IEEE International Conference on Omni-Layer Intelligent Systems (COINS) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Annotation quality is a major bottleneck in building reliable and explainable artificial intelligence (XAI) systems for mental health research. In depression-related datasets, labels are often assigned without structured evidence, symptom-level justification, or traceable alignment with the criteria of the Diagnostic and Statistical Manual of Mental Disorders, Fifth Edition, Text Revision (DSM-5-TR), limiting both transparency and downstream model interpretability. We propose a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop annotation framework for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD) that combines large language model (LLM)-assisted labeling with expert verification. The framework is intended to support the construction of explainable, DSM-5-TR-aligned datasets rather than to perform clinical diagnosis. It operates in three stages: candidate evidence selection from textual records, criterion-level DSM-5-TR analysis, and case-level synthesis that produces label-level diagnostic and severity annotations. A dual-memory architecture, composed of Example Memory and Reflection Memory, is designed to internalize expert feedback and iteratively improve future annotations without retraining. We describe this mechanism and leave its evaluation across multiple feedback cycles to future work. In addition to final labels, the framework exports clinical evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories, enabling comprehensive auditability. In a pilot study using expert-reviewed samples, the proposed approach improves annotation consistency and explainability while reducing manual revision effort.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向可解释抑郁症状标注的自演进以人为本框架
标注质量是构建可靠且可解释的人工智能（XAI）系统用于心理健康研究的主要瓶颈。在与抑郁相关的数据集中，标签常缺乏结构化证据、症状级依据或可追溯的《精神障碍诊断与统计手册》第五版文本修订版（DSM-5-TR）标准对齐，限制了透明度和下游模型的可解释性。我们提出了一种针对重度抑郁障碍（MDD）的自演进、专家参与的标注框架，结合大语言模型（LLM）辅助标注与专家验证。该框架旨在支持构建可解释且符合DSM-5-TR标准的数据集，而非进行临床诊断。其运作分为三个阶段：从文本记录中选择候选证据、基于DSM-5-TR标准进行诊断标准级分析、以及案例级综合生成诊断与严重程度级别的标签。框架采用双记忆架构（示例记忆与反思记忆），用于内化专家反馈并迭代改进未来标注，无需重新训练。我们描述了该机制，其在多轮反馈中的评估留待未来工作。除最终标签外，框架还导出临床证据、推理轨迹和编辑历史，实现全面可审计性。在专家审核样本的试点研究中，该方法提升了标注一致性与可解释性，同时减少了人工修订工作量。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for high-quality, explainable annotations in mental health AI systems, this work introduces a self-evolving, expert-in-the-loop framework for annotating Major Depressive Disorder symptoms, aligned with DSM-5-TR criteria. It combines large language model-assisted labeling with expert verification across three stages: evidence selection, DSM-5-TR criterion analysis, and case-level synthesis. A dual-memory architecture (Example and Reflection Memory) captures expert feedback to iteratively refine future annotations without retraining. Pilot results show improved annotation consistency, enhanced explainability, and reduced manual revision effort, while also enabling full auditability through exported evidence, reasoning traces, and edit histories.
为构建可靠且可解释的AI心理健康系统，本文提出一种结合大语言模型与专家反馈的自进化标注框架，用于生成符合DSM-5-TR标准的重度抑郁障碍标签。该方法分三阶段：从文本中选取证据、按DSM-5-TR标准分析、综合生成诊断与严重程度标注，并通过双记忆架构（示例记忆与反思记忆）保留专家反馈以迭代优化标注，无需重新训练。试点实验表明，该框架显著提升标注一致性与可解释性，同时降低人工修订成本，并支持通过输出证据、推理轨迹和修改历史实现全面可审计性。
Mask-Aware Policy Gradients for Diffusion Language Models
Authors: Haran Raajesh, Kulin Shah, Adam Klivans, Philipp Krähenbühl
First: 2026-07-16T16:57:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:57:34+00:00
Comments: Accepted at COLM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning has proven effective for improving reasoning in large language models, but extending it to Masked Diffusion Language Models (MDLMs) remains challenging due to the intractability of the log-likelihood estimation. Existing approaches approximate this log-likelihood by modeling only the token predictions, ignoring the order in which positions are unmasked during generation. We observe that MDLM generation involves two decisions at each step: what tokens to place at each masked position and which positions to remask. We formalize this as a two-stage action MDP, showing that the policy gradient naturally decomposes into a token term and a masking term. Combining optimization of both terms leads to state-of-the-art outcomes on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, with scores of 87.1% on GSM8K and 53.4% on MBPP.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向掩码扩散语言模型的掩码感知策略梯度
强化学习已被证明能有效提升大语言模型的推理能力，但将其扩展到掩码扩散语言模型（MDLMs）仍具挑战性，因对数似然估计不可行。现有方法仅通过建模令牌预测来近似该对数似然，忽略了生成过程中位置解掩码的顺序。我们观察到，MDLM 生成在每一步涉及两个决策：在每个掩码位置放置哪些令牌，以及哪些位置需重新掩码。我们将其形式化为两阶段动作马尔可夫决策过程，表明策略梯度自然分解为令牌项与掩码项。联合优化两项可取得数学推理与编码基准的最先进结果，GSM8K 得分 87.1%，MBPP 得分 53.4%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of applying reinforcement learning to Masked Diffusion Language Models due to intractable log-likelihood estimation, the authors propose a two-stage action MDP framework that explicitly models both token prediction and masking decisions at each generation step. By decomposing the policy gradient into token and masking components, they enable joint optimization that captures the sequential structure of MDLM generation. Experiments show this approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on mathematical reasoning and coding benchmarks, reaching 87.1% on GSM8K and 53.4% on MBPP.
为了解决强化学习在掩码扩散语言模型中因对数似然难以估计而难以应用的问题，作者提出一种两阶段动作MDP框架，明确建模每一步的词元选择与掩码决策。通过将策略梯度分解为词元和掩码两个部分，实现了联合优化，从而捕捉生成过程的序列结构。实验表明该方法在数学推理和编程任务上达到最先进水平，GSM8K得分87.1%，MBPP得分53.4%。
Early Adoption of Agentic Coding Tools by GitHub Projects
Authors: Maliha Noushin Raida, Daqing Hou
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-07-15T17:05:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:57:06+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the KDD 2026 Workshop on Agentic Software Engineering (SE 3.0)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Agentic coding tools are increasingly capable of generating and submitting pull requests (PRs) to software projects, introducing new forms of human-agent collaboration in software development. While prior studies have examined PR-level outcomes of agent-generated contributions, less is known about how agentic coding tools are adopted and managed at the project level. In this paper, we analyze 25,264 agentic PRs from 2,361 popular GitHub repositories to investigate (1) the adoption of agentic coding tools, (2) project-level agentic PR productivity, and (3) human-agent collaboration patterns. Our results show that the median repository generates only one to two agentic PRs during a three-month period, indicating that intensive adoption remains concentrated in a small subset of projects. At the same time, small projects (1-5 contributors) exhibit higher participation ratios and average levels of agentic PR activity than medium-sized and large projects. We also observe substantial variation in project-level agentic PR productivity. While a small number of projects exceed an industry-reported estimate of 36 PRs per participant during the three-month observation period, most projects remain below this threshold. Finally, human-agent collaboration is dominated by a single-human oversight model, in which one developer reviews and/or modifies the agent's contributions, while multi-human collaboration patterns remain uncommon. These findings provide early empirical evidence on how open-source projects organize human oversight around agentic coding tools and suggest that successful integration of agent-generated contributions depends not only on advances in agent capabilities but also on the human and organizational processes that govern their use. Because this study captures an early snapshot of agent adoption, future work should continue to track how adoption patterns evolve over time.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GitHub项目对代理编码工具的早期采用
代理编码工具正日益具备生成并提交拉取请求（PR）的能力，为软件开发引入了新型人机协作形式。尽管已有研究考察了代理生成贡献的PR级结果，但对代理编码工具在项目层面的采用与管理知之甚少。本文分析了2,361个热门GitHub仓库中的25,264个代理PR，旨在探究：（1）代理编码工具的采用情况；（2）项目层面的代理PR生产力；（3）人机协作模式。结果表明，中位数仓库在三个月内仅生成1至2个代理PR，说明高强度采用仍集中于少数项目。同时，小型项目（1-5名贡献者）的参与率和平均代理PR活动水平高于中型和大型项目。我们还观察到项目层面代理PR生产力存在显著差异：少数项目在三个月观察期内超过行业报告的每人36个PR的阈值，而多数项目仍低于该阈值。最后，人机协作主要采用单人监督模式，即一名开发者审查或修改代理贡献，而多人协作模式仍属罕见。这些发现为开源项目如何围绕代理编码工具组织人工监督提供了早期实证依据，并表明成功整合代理生成贡献不仅依赖于代理能力的提升，还取决于管理其使用的人员与组织流程。由于本研究捕捉的是代理采用的早期快照，未来工作应持续追踪采用模式随时间的演变。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of agentic coding tools that autonomously generate and submit pull requests, this study investigates how these tools are adopted and managed at the project level across 2,361 GitHub repositories. Analyzing 25,264 agentic PRs, the research finds that most projects generate only one to two such PRs over three months, indicating limited widespread adoption. Small projects (1–5 contributors) show higher participation and activity levels than larger ones, yet overall productivity varies widely—with only a few projects exceeding 36 PRs per participant. Human oversight is predominantly single-developer, with rare instances of multi-human collaboration, suggesting that successful integration relies as much on organizational processes as on agent capabilities.
本研究旨在探讨代理编码工具在开源项目中的采用与管理方式，因这些工具已能生成并提交拉取请求。通过对2361个GitHub仓库的25264个代理PR进行分析，发现多数项目在三个月内仅产生一到两个代理PR，表明广泛采用仍集中在少数项目。规模较小的项目（1–5名贡献者）参与度和活动水平更高，而项目间代理PR生产力差异显著——仅有少数项目超过每名参与者36个PR的行业基准。人类与代理协作主要由单人审查主导，多人协作模式罕见。研究结果表明，代理代码的成功整合不仅依赖工具能力，更取决于人类组织流程。
Subjective Risk Decomposition: A New View for Uncertainty Quantification
Authors: Raghad Alamri, Michele Caprio, Gavin Brown
First: 2026-07-16T16:52:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:52:54+00:00
Comments: 27 pages (including bibliography/appendix)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present a novel viewpoint for uncertainty quantification. Uncertainty measures are not primitives, in need of axioms and argumentation, but instead consequences, of higher-level modelling decisions. We show how epistemic and aleatoric uncertainty measures can be derived via decomposition of a subjective risk, based on a strictly proper loss. Reverse cross-entropy provides a prominent example, where decomposition recovers the classic information-theoretic uncertainty terms. The same approach recovers numerous measures previously proposed across the UQ literature, providing them a common theoretical foundation. From a practical point of view, this suggests a new approach to UQ: given a modelling scenario and strictly proper loss, the corresponding epistemic and aleatoric terms are induced by the subjective-risk decomposition.
