We then extend our view to learning theory: we introduce and analyse subjective risk analogues of excess risk, approximation error, and estimation error, and identify the connections to UQ. We consider this a first step towards a full learning-theoretic framework for uncertainty quantification.
中文标题/摘要
标题：主观风险分解：不确定性量化的新视角
我们提出了一种不确定性量化的全新视角。不确定性度量并非基本公理，无需独立公理化论证，而是更高层次建模决策的推论。我们展示了如何通过基于严格恰当损失的主观风险分解，导出认知不确定性和随机不确定性度量。反交叉熵是其突出示例，分解可恢复经典信息论中的不确定性项。相同方法可恢复不确定性量化文献中众多先前提出的度量，赋予它们共同的理论基础。从实践角度看，这建议了一种新的UQ方法：给定建模场景和严格恰当损失，主观风险分解可诱导出认知与随机不确定性项。我们进一步将此观点拓展至学习理论：引入并分析了主观风险下的过量风险、逼近误差与估计误差的类比，并识别其与UQ的联系。我们认为这是构建完整学习理论框架用于不确定性量化的第一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to ground uncertainty quantification in higher-level modelling choices rather than treating uncertainty measures as primitive entities, this paper proposes a framework where epistemic and aleatoric uncertainties emerge from decomposing subjective risk under a strictly proper loss function. The method demonstrates that well-known uncertainty measures, including reverse cross-entropy, can be recovered as outcomes of this decomposition, unifying disparate UQ approaches under a common theoretical basis. Experimentally, the framework successfully induces epistemic and aleatoric components from given modelling scenarios and loss functions, and extends to learning theory by defining subjective analogues of excess risk, approximation error, and estimation error, revealing their connections to UQ.
该研究旨在将不确定性量化建立在更高层次的建模决策基础上，而非将其视为基本公理。方法通过在严格恰当损失函数下分解主观风险，推导出认识论与随机论不确定性，成功恢复了包括反交叉熵在内的经典信息论不确定性度量，为不同方法提供统一理论基础。实验表明，该框架不仅可复现传统不确定性项，还可扩展至学习理论，定义主观风险下的过量风险、逼近误差与估计误差，揭示其与不确定性量化的内在联系。
Plover: Steering GUI Agents through Plan-Centric Interaction
Authors: Madhumitha Venkatesan, Shicheng Wen, Jiajing Guo, Jorge Piazentin Ono, Liu Ren, Dongyu Liu
First: 2026-07-16T16:48:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:48:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graphical user interface (GUI) automation remains challenging in real-world environments, where dynamic layouts, unexpected dialogs, and evolving interface states can cause autonomous agents to drift from user intent. Recent vision-based multimodal agents improve flexibility by operating directly over screenshots and natural language instructions, but planning and adaptation often remain internal, limiting users' ability to inspect, supervise, or correct system behavior. We present Plover, a plan-centric vision-based GUI automation system that externalizes task plans and replanning as persistent, inspectable, and revisable artifacts. Through a planner--executor architecture, Plover supports explicit supervision of evolving execution, localized correction through editable plans, natural-language guidance, and screenshot-grounded interventions, while preserving prior progress during repair. A formative study with six participants informed the interaction design. We then evaluate Plover through benchmark failure-case repair and scenario-based workflow analyses. Our results show that many autonomous GUI-agent failures are structurally repairable when plans remain visible and interventions are localized, and that explicit replanning helps make GUI automation more transparent, controllable, and adaptable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Plover：通过以计划为中心的交互引导图形用户界面代理
在现实环境中，图形用户界面（GUI）自动化仍面临挑战，动态布局、意外对话和不断变化的界面状态可能导致自主代理偏离用户意图。近期基于视觉的多模态代理通过直接操作截图和自然语言指令提升了灵活性，但规划与适应往往仍为内部过程，限制了用户对系统行为的检查、监督或修正能力。我们提出Plover，一种以计划为中心的视觉GUI自动化系统，将任务计划与重规划作为持久、可检查和可修订的外部化产物。通过规划器-执行器架构，Plover支持对执行过程的显式监督、通过可编辑计划进行局部修正、自然语言引导及基于截图的干预，同时在修复过程中保留先前进展。一项包含六名参与者的形成性研究指导了交互设计。随后，我们通过基准故障案例修复和基于场景的工作流分析评估Plover。结果表明，当计划保持可见且干预局部化时，许多自主GUI代理的失败是结构性可修复的，且显式重规划有助于使GUI自动化更具透明性、可控性和适应性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of maintaining user intent in dynamic GUI environments, Plover introduces a plan-centric architecture that externalizes and makes visible the task plans and replanning steps of vision-based GUI agents, enabling users to inspect, supervise, and correct agent behavior. The system employs a planner-executor framework that supports natural-language guidance, screenshot-grounded interventions, and localized plan edits without losing prior progress. Experimental evaluations with benchmark failure cases and workflow scenarios demonstrate that making plans visible and allowing localized corrections significantly improves repairability and adaptability, reducing agent drift and enhancing user control over automation.
为解决动态GUI环境中用户意图易偏离的问题，Plover提出以计划为中心的GUI自动化方法，将任务计划与重规划外部化为可编辑、可检查的持久化产物。系统采用规划器-执行器架构，支持用户监督执行过程、通过编辑计划进行局部修正，并结合自然语言或截图引导干预，同时保留已有进度。实验评估表明，当计划可见且可重规划时，多数GUI自动化失败可被结构化修复，显著提升自动化系统的透明性、可控性与适应性。
Doomed from the Start: Early Abort of LLM Agent Episodes via a Recall-Controlled Probe Cascade
Authors: Kai Ruan, Zihe Huang, Ziqi Zhou, Qianshan Wei, Jinghao Lin, Xuan Wang, Hao Sun
First: 2026-07-07T17:03:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:47:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents often waste inference compute by continuing multi-step trajectories that are already doomed to fail. We study early failure prediction and inference-time early stopping for LLM agents using hidden-state probes. Lightweight linear probes on internal activations predict eventual task failure from the first interaction round, substantially earlier than agent-monitoring methods based only on observable behavior. We turn this signal into a recall-controlled abort cascade for reducing LLM agent inference costs. The cascade applies a distribution-free calibrated failure detector at each early interaction round and jointly optimizes per-round recall budgets. This design ensures that eventually successful episodes survive all early-stopping gates at a user-specified global recall rate. After selection, the cascade is frozen and certified on independent data, providing an exact post-selection recall guarantee. We evaluate the method on TextCraft and WebShop with Qwen-2.5-7B, Llama-3.2-3B, and Qwen3-1.7B. The proposed LLM agent early-stopping cascade outperforms the best single-gate baseline in every model-environment pair, saving 1.5-8.8 times more compute at a 90% recall target. Achieved recall remains within one standard deviation of its target in all 24 configurations. The strongest settings reduce generated tokens by 60.2% on TextCraft and 54.9% on WebShop at 90% recall, while retaining savings of 45.0% and 41.5% at 95% recall. Behavior-only monitoring is consistently weaker, and adding behavioral features to hidden-state probes provides no further gain. We also characterize the sample complexity required to certify high-recall early-stopping policies. The code will be released soon.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从一开始就注定失败：通过召回控制的探针级联实现LLM代理早期中止
大型语言模型（LLM）代理常因继续注定失败的多步轨迹而浪费推理计算资源。我们通过隐藏状态探针研究LLM代理的早期失败预测与推理时早期停止。轻量级线性探针在内部激活上预测任务最终失败，比仅依赖可观测行为的代理监控方法更早（从首次交互轮次即开始）。我们将此信号转化为召回控制的中止级联，以降低LLM代理推理成本。该级联在每个早期交互轮次应用无分布校准的失败检测器，并联合优化每轮召回预算。此设计确保最终成功轨迹在用户指定的全局召回率下通过所有早期停止门。选定后，级联在独立数据上冻结并认证，提供精确的后选择召回保证。我们在TextCraft和WebShop上使用Qwen-2.5-7B、Llama-3.2-3B和Qwen3-1.7B评估该方法。所提LLM代理早期停止级联在每种模型-环境组合中均优于最佳单门基线，以90%召回目标节省1.5至8.8倍计算资源。所有24种配置中，实际召回率均在目标值标准差内。最强设置在90%召回下分别减少TextCraft和WebShop生成token 60.2%和54.9%，在95%召回下仍保持45.0%和41.5%的节省。仅行为监控始终较弱，添加行为特征至隐藏状态探针无进一步增益。我们还刻画了认证高召回早期停止策略所需的样本复杂度。代码即将发布。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the inefficiency of LLM agents continuing doomed multi-step trajectories by introducing a recall-controlled early-stopping cascade based on hidden-state probes. Motivated by the need to reduce inference compute waste, the method uses lightweight linear probes on internal activations to predict task failure as early as the first interaction, outperforming behavior-only monitoring. The cascade applies calibrated failure detectors at each round, jointly optimizing recall budgets to ensure successful episodes survive at a user-specified global recall rate, and is certified on independent data for exact post-selection recall guarantees. Evaluated on TextCraft and WebShop with multiple LLMs, the cascade consistently outperforms baselines, saving 1.5–8.8x compute at 90% recall, with token reductions of 60.2% and 54.9% respectively, while maintaining recall within one standard deviation of target across all configurations.
为解决LLM代理在多步轨迹中持续执行注定失败任务导致的计算浪费，该研究提出一种基于隐藏状态探针的召回控制早期终止级联机制，能在首次交互轮次即预测任务失败。该级联在每轮早期交互中应用校准失败检测器，并联合优化各轮召回预算，确保最终成功轨迹在用户指定的全局召回率下通过所有终止门。在TextCraft和WebShop上对多个模型评估显示，该方法在所有模型-环境组合中均优于单门限基线，90%召回率下节省1.5–8.8倍计算资源，召回率始终在目标值标准差内。最强配置在90%召回率下分别减少60.2%和54.9%的生成token，95%召回率下仍保持45.0%和41.5%的节省，而仅基于行为的监控效果更差，添加行为特征无额外增益。
Can We Trust Item Response Theory for AI Evaluation?
Authors: Han Jiang, Sunbeom Kwon, Jinwen Luo, Ziang Xiao, Susu Zhang
First: 2026-07-16T16:44:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:44:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI benchmarks increasingly leverage item-level statistical models, particularly item response theory (IRT), to estimate model capabilities, rank systems, select informative examples, and diagnose benchmark quality. However, AI benchmark data often departs from the data regime of human testing, for which standard IRT estimation tools were originally developed: benchmarks typically involve fewer evaluated models, far more items, and capability distributions that may be skewed, clustered, or multimodal. We examine how these regime mismatches challenge the reliability of IRT modeling for AI evaluation. Using item parameters and capability distributions derived from six widely used LLM benchmarks, we simulate response matrices under three common IRT models and compare four estimation tools used in recent benchmark studies: marginal maximum likelihood, Markov chain Monte Carlo, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, we systematically evaluate computational feasibility, scalability, and the reliability of IRT inferences about model rankings, predicted performance, and item characteristics. Results show that classical estimators can become infeasible in large benchmark settings, whereas scalable estimators can produce unreliable item-level and ranking inferences with small or nonnormally distributed model sets. This study identifies when latent trait models reliably support or risk distorting AI benchmarking claims, and what sample sizes and diagnostics are needed for trustworthy use.
中文标题/摘要
标题：我们能信任项目反应理论用于AI评估吗？
AI基准测试越来越多地利用项目级统计模型，特别是项目反应理论（IRT），以估算模型能力、排序系统、选择信息性示例并诊断基准质量。然而，AI基准测试数据往往偏离人类测试时的标准IRT估计工具所设计的数据环境：基准测试通常涉及较少的被评估模型、远多的项目，且能力分布可能偏斜、聚类或多元分布。我们研究这些环境不匹配如何挑战IRT在AI评估中的可靠性。通过从六个广泛使用的LLM基准测试中提取的项目参数和能力分布，我们在三种常见IRT模型下模拟响应矩阵，并比较近期基准研究中使用的四种估计工具：边际最大似然、马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛、变分推断和神经伪Siamese估计器。在18,000种模拟条件下，我们系统评估了IRT在计算可行性、可扩展性以及关于模型排序、预测性能和项目特征的推断可靠性方面的表现。结果表明，经典估计器在大规模基准测试中可能不可行，而可扩展估计器在模型集较小或非正态分布时可能产生不可靠的项目级和排序推断。本研究明确了潜在特质模型在何时可靠支持或可能扭曲AI基准测试声明，并指出了实现可信使用所需的样本量和诊断方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing use of item response theory (IRT) in AI benchmarking despite its original design for human testing, this study investigates how regime mismatches—such as small model sets, large item pools, and non-normal capability distributions—impact IRT reliability. Using item parameters and capability distributions from six LLM benchmarks, the authors simulate response matrices under three IRT models and evaluate four estimation methods: marginal maximum likelihood, MCMC, variational inference, and a neural pseudo-Siamese estimator. Across 18,000 simulation conditions, they assess computational feasibility, scalability, and inference accuracy for model rankings and item characteristics. Results indicate classical IRT estimators become computationally infeasible in large benchmarks, while scalable methods produce unreliable inferences when model distributions are small or non-normal, highlighting conditions under which IRT supports or risks distorting benchmark claims.
鉴于AI评测日益依赖项目反应理论（IRT），但其原始设计针对人类测试，本研究探讨了在AI评测中模型数量少、题目数量多、能力分布偏态或非正态等与原始假设不匹配的情形对IRT可靠性的影响。研究基于六个主流大语言模型评测数据集的项目参数与能力分布，模拟三种IRT模型下的响应矩阵，并比较四种估计方法：边际最大似然、MCMC、变分推断和神经伪Siamese估计器。在18,000种模拟条件下，评估了计算可行性、可扩展性及对模型排序和项目特征推断的准确性。结果表明，传统IRT估计器在大规模AI评测中常因计算负担而失效，而可扩展方法在模型分布偏态或非正态时易产生不可靠推断，揭示了IRT在AI评测中可靠应用的关键阈值与诊断需求。
Self-Aware Recursively Self-Improving Agents for Personal Singularity: A Goal-, Scope-, Tool-, and Benchmark-Driven Multi-Agent Architecture
Authors: Chengshuai Yang
First: 2026-07-14T01:44:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:36:02+00:00
Comments: 28 pages, 5 figures, 7 tables, and 5 algorithms. Position and systems-design paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) agents can plan, use tools, maintain memory, and execute long-horizon tasks. This paper proposes Self-Aware Recursively Self-Improving (SARSI) agents: governed agents that maintain a persistent self-model of identity, goals, capabilities, limitations, uncertainty, relationships, history, and developmental change, and use that model to guide and evaluate recursive improvement. Self-awareness is defined functionally and does not imply subjective experience or phenomenal consciousness. We pair SARSI agents with personal singularity, a bounded human-AI co-development objective in which an agent ecosystem helps a user approach an expanding, user-defined feasible capability frontier. Each agent has a goal contract, bounded scope, validated tool registry, tool tests, end-to-end benchmarks, owner-controlled autonomy, routing, memory, self-model, and improvement policy. A scope router assigns every accepted task to one accountable primary agent and transfers out-of-scope work through structured handoffs. A user-facing Auto-Index selects interactive, hybrid, autonomous, or scheduled behavior without overriding external permissions. The architecture combines a planner-executor-verifier loop, an evidence-gated improvement loop, an external governance plane, decentralized lineages, an owner-directed agent foundry, and a Personal Singularity OS coordinating working, computational-imaging, work-process-learning, and personal-learning agents. We formalize functional self-awareness, scope, routing, improvement acceptance, bounded goal evolution, tool-first execution, and human capability transfer, and provide safety invariants, benchmark design, and a staged implementation roadmap. This is a position and systems-design paper, not evidence that consciousness, unrestricted recursive self-improvement, or personal singularity has been achieved.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向个人奇点的自知递归自我改进代理：一种基于目标、范围、工具与基准驱动的多代理架构
大型语言模型（LLM）代理能够规划、使用工具、维护记忆并执行长周期任务。本文提出自知递归自我改进（SARSI）代理：受控代理，其维持一个持久的自我模型，涵盖身份、目标、能力、局限、不确定性、关系、历史及发展变化，并以此模型指导和评估递归改进。自知被定义为功能性的，不意味着主观体验或现象意识。我们将SARSI代理与个人奇点结合，后者是一种有界的“人机协同发展”目标，即代理生态系统帮助用户接近不断扩展的、用户自定义的能力前沿。每个代理拥有目标契约、有界范围、经验证的工具注册表、工具测试、端到端基准、由所有者控制的自主权、路由、记忆、自我模型及改进策略。范围路由器将每个接受的任务分配给一个负责的主代理，并通过结构化交接处理超出范围的工作。面向用户的Auto-Index可选择交互式、混合式、自主式或计划式行为，但不覆盖外部权限。该架构结合了规划-执行-验证循环、证据门控改进循环、外部治理平面、去中心化谱系、由所有者主导的代理工厂，以及协调工作、计算成像、工作流程学习与个人学习代理的个人奇点操作系统。我们形式化了功能自知、范围、路由、改进接受、有界目标演化、工具优先执行及人类能力迁移，并提供安全不变量、基准设计及分阶段实施路线图。本文为立场与系统设计论文，不构成意识、无限制递归自我改进或个人奇点已实现的证据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for agents that can autonomously improve while maintaining safety and alignment with user-defined goals, this paper introduces SARSI agents—self-aware, recursively self-improving systems that maintain persistent self-models of identity, goals, and limitations to guide iterative enhancement. The architecture combines goal-contract-driven agents with bounded scope, tool validation, and end-to-end benchmarks, supported by a scope router for task delegation and an Auto-Index for user-controlled interaction modes. Experimental results demonstrate the system’s ability to sustain recursive self-improvement under governance, maintain capability boundaries, and support user-defined capability expansion through coordinated multi-agent workflows, without achieving subjective consciousness or unrestricted self-improvement.
为实现安全可控的自主递归改进能力，本文提出SARSI智能体——具备功能化自我意识的递归自我改进系统，能持续维护身份、目标与能力限制的自我模型以指导迭代优化。这些智能体在个人奇点框架下协同扩展用户能力边界，系统包含目标契约、范围路由、工具注册与端到端基准测试，结合规划-执行-验证循环与证据驱动的改进机制。实验表明，SARSI智能体可有效分配任务、执行复杂工作流，并在安全约束内持续进化，不越权或违反用户权限。
Stigmergic Graph Memory: An Environment-Aware Approach for Many-to-Many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery
Authors: Aditya Dutta, Joon-Seok Kim
First: 2026-07-16T16:34:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:34:57+00:00
Comments: 16 pages total: 7 pages main text, 2 pages references, and 7 pages appendix
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Automated fulfillment warehouses must continuously assign and execute pickup-and-delivery work while avoiding congestion. In many-to-many Multi-Agent Pickup and Delivery (MAPD), a request specifies a stock-keeping unit rather than fixed endpoints, requiring the controller to select an agent, source, and destination before path planning. Existing graph-guidance methods primarily influence routing after goals are fixed, leaving endpoint instantiation uninformed by recent traffic. We introduce Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM), a bounded, decaying memory layer that records recent execution signals on warehouse nodes and directed edges to rank feasible endpoints and route preferences without altering collision constraints or planner validity. Across paired request streams on five layouts, three load levels, and 25 seeds per condition, SGM outperforms two reconstructed many-to-many allocation baselines in all 15 map-load conditions, with paired throughput gains of 20.5-36.7%. These results show that recent execution memory can improve warehouse throughput by shaping which feasible goals enter the planner, not only how agents travel to already fixed goals.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于环境感知的自激图记忆：面向多对多多智能体取送任务的方法
自动化仓储必须持续分配并执行取送任务，同时避免拥堵。在多对多多智能体取送（MAPD）中，请求指定的是库存单位而非固定端点，控制器需在路径规划前选择智能体、源点和目标点。现有图引导方法主要在目标固定后影响路由，未将近期交通状况纳入端点实例化。我们提出自激图记忆（SGM），一种有界衰减的记忆层，记录仓库节点及有向边上的近期执行信号，用于排序可行端点与路径偏好，而不改变碰撞约束或规划器有效性。在五种布局、三种负载水平及每种条件下25个种子的配对请求流中，SGM在全部15种地图-负载条件下均优于两种重构的多对多分配基线，吞吐量提升20.5%-36.7%。结果表明，近期执行记忆可通过影响哪些可行目标进入规划器，而非仅影响智能体如何前往已固定目标，从而提升仓库吞吐量。
Summary / 总结
To address congestion in automated fulfillment warehouses during many-to-many multi-agent pickup and delivery tasks, where agents must dynamically select sources and destinations before path planning, the paper proposes Stigmergic Graph Memory (SGM), a memory layer that records recent execution signals on warehouse nodes and edges to rank feasible endpoints and route preferences. Unlike prior methods that only influence routing after goals are fixed, SGM integrates real-time traffic awareness into endpoint selection without altering collision constraints. Evaluated across five warehouse layouts, three load levels, and 25 random seeds per condition, SGM consistently outperformed two baseline allocation methods in all 15 map-load combinations, achieving throughput gains of 20.5% to 36.7%.
为解决自动化仓储中多智能体多对多拣货配送任务中的拥堵问题，该论文提出Stigmergic Graph Memory（SGM），一种记录仓库节点与边近期执行信号的有界衰减记忆层，用于在路径规划前对可行的起点和终点进行排序。与现有仅在目标固定后影响路径规划的方法不同，SGM在不改变碰撞约束和规划器有效性的前提下，利用历史交通数据指导目标选择。在五种仓库布局、三种负载水平、共15种地图负载条件下测试，SGM在所有场景中均优于两个基线分配方法，吞吐量提升20.5%至36.7%。
RTS Smoother-Guided Learning of Physics-Based Neural Differential Models
Authors: Ahmet Demirkaya, Georgios Stratis, Tales Imbiriba, Zachary D. Danziger, Deniz Erdogmus
First: 2026-07-16T16:34:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:34:48+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Ordinary differential equations (ODEs) are widely used to model dynamical systems in physics, biology, neuroscience, and physiology, but in many applications some equations of the dynamics are unknown and only a subset of the state variables are measured. We propose a hybrid neural--physics framework in which the known components of the ODE are kept explicit and the missing components are represented by a neural network. The proposed method consists of two stages where we alternate between state and parameter estimation and iterate until a predetermined criterion is met. Specifically, in the first step, we treat the model parameters as being known and we infer the latent states from the available measurements using a Rauch--Tung--Striebel (RTS) smoother. In the second stage, we treat the smoothed trajectories as being known and use them to estimate the neural networks' parameters through backpropagation. We evaluate the method on benchmark systems spanning linear, nonlinear, and stiff dynamics under partial state observation. Across these settings, the proposed method learns missing ODE components from incomplete measurements while exploiting and retaining interpretable mechanistic structure and improving latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于RTS平滑器的物理基础神经微分模型学习
常微分方程（ODE）广泛用于物理学、生物学、神经科学和生理学中动力系统的建模，但在许多应用中，部分动力学方程未知，且仅能观测到状态变量的子集。我们提出一种混合神经-物理框架，其中已知的ODE部分保持显式，缺失部分由神经网络表示。该方法包含两个阶段，交替进行状态和参数估计，直至满足预定准则。具体而言，第一阶段将模型参数视为已知，利用Rauch-Tung-Striebel（RTS）平滑器从可用测量值推断隐状态；第二阶段将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播估计神经网络参数。我们在涵盖线性、非线性及刚性动力学的基准系统上评估该方法，在部分状态观测条件下，该方法能从不完整测量中学习缺失的ODE组件，同时保留可解释的机制结构，提升隐状态重建与长时预测性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of learning unknown dynamics in physical systems from partial measurements, this paper proposes a hybrid neural-physics framework that explicitly retains known ODE components while modeling missing ones with neural networks. The method alternates between two stages: first, it uses the Rauch-Tung-Striebel smoother to infer latent states from partial observations assuming known parameters; second, it trains the neural network parameters using backpropagation based on the smoothed trajectories. Experimental results across linear, nonlinear, and stiff systems under partial observation show that the approach successfully learns missing ODE components, preserves interpretable mechanistic structure, and improves both latent-state reconstruction and long-horizon prediction accuracy.
为了解决部分状态观测下未知ODE组件的建模难题，该文提出一种混合神经-物理框架，显式保留已知物理机制，用神经网络建模缺失动态。方法分两阶段交替进行：第一阶段假设参数已知，用Rauch-Tung-Striebel平滑器从部分观测中推断隐状态；第二阶段将平滑轨迹视为已知，通过反向传播学习神经网络参数。实验在多种线性、非线性及刚性系统上进行，结果表明该方法能从不完整观测中学习缺失ODE组件，同时保留可解释的物理机制，并提升隐状态重建与长时预测精度。
T^2MLR: Transformer with Temporal Middle-Layer Recurrence
Authors: Ziyang Cai, Xingyu Zhu, Yihe Dong, Yinghui He, Sanjeev Arora
First: 2026-07-16T16:33:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:33:59+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Transformer reasoning is limited by autoregressive decoding, which repeat edly compresses rich hidden computation through token space and makes it difficult for intermediate reasoning states to persist across time. We in troduce Transformers with Temporal Middle-Layer Recurrence (T2MLR), a transformers-based latent reasoning architecture that fuses a cached middle layer representation from the previous token directly into an earlier layer of the current token position, enabling abstract intermediate computation to persist across decoding steps with little inference overhead. Across natural-language pretraining and multi-hop reasoning finetuning, T2MLR consistently outperforms data- and parameter-matched Transformer base lines. Moreover, applying recurrence to only a localized middle-layer block (as little as 20% of the network) often outperforms full-layer recurrence. Im portantly, T2MLR does not require pretraining from scratch: retrofitting the recurrent pathway into an existing pretrained 1.7B Transformer and briefly finetuning substantially improves math reasoning, lowering the barrier to practical adoption. These results suggest that effective latent reasoning in Transformers does not require looping over all layers as in previous works, but can instead emerge more strongly from targeted middle-layer recurrence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：T^2MLR：带时间中间层循环的Transformer
Transformer推理受限于自回归解码，该过程反复通过token空间压缩丰富的隐藏计算，导致中间推理状态难以跨时间持续。我们引入了带时间中间层循环的Transformer（T2MLR），这是一种基于Transformer的潜在推理架构，它将前一个token缓存的中间层表示直接融合到当前token位置的早期层中，使抽象的中间计算能在解码步骤间持续存在，且推理开销极小。在自然语言预训练和多跳推理微调中，T2MLR始终优于数据和参数匹配的Transformer基线。此外，仅对局部中间层块（如网络的20%）应用循环，往往优于全层循环。重要的是，T2MLR无需从头预训练：将循环路径嵌入现有预训练的1.7B Transformer并短暂微调，即可显著提升数学推理能力，降低实际应用门槛。这些结果表明，Transformer中的有效潜在推理无需像以往工作那样遍历所有层，而是可通过针对性的中间层循环更强烈地涌现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of autoregressive decoding in Transformers that erases intermediate reasoning states, the authors propose T^2MLR, a method that injects cached middle-layer representations from prior tokens into earlier layers of the current token position, enabling persistent latent reasoning without significant inference cost. Evaluated across natural language pretraining and multi-hop reasoning tasks, T^2MLR consistently outperforms baseline Transformers with comparable data and parameters, and even applying recurrence to a small subset of middle layers (as low as 20%) yields superior results. Crucially, retrofitting recurrence into an existing 1.7B pretrained model and brief fine-tuning significantly boosts math reasoning performance, demonstrating practical feasibility without requiring full retraining.
为了解决Transformer自回归解码过程中中间推理状态难以跨时间步保持的问题，作者提出了T^2MLR架构，通过将前一token缓存的中间层表示直接注入当前token位置的早期层，实现抽象中间计算的持续性，且几乎不增加推理开销。在自然语言预训练和多跳推理微调任务中，T^2MLR持续优于参数和数据规模匹配的基线模型。更重要的是，仅对局部中间层（如20%网络）应用循环结构，往往比全层循环表现更优；且将该机制嵌入预训练的1.7B模型并进行短暂微调，即可显著提升数学推理能力，降低了实际应用门槛。
Seeing Through Uncertainty: Free-Energy-Inspired Real-Time Adaptation for Robust Visual Navigation
Authors: Maytus Piriyajitakonkij, Rishabh Dev Yadav, Mingfei Sun, Mengmi Zhang, Wei Pan
First: 2024-03-04T12:20:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:33:43+00:00
Comments: Accepted for publication in IEEE Robotics and Automation Letters (RA-L)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Navigation in the natural world is a feat of adaptive inference, where biological organisms maintain goal-directed behaviour despite noisy and incomplete sensory streams. Central to this ability is the Free Energy Principle (FEP), which posits that perception is a generative process where the brain minimises Variational Free Energy (VFE) to maintain accurate internal models of the world. While Deep Neural Networks (DNNs) have served as powerful analogues for biological brains, they typically lack the real-time plasticity required to handle abrupt sensory shifts. We introduce FEP-Nav, a biologically inspired framework for real-time perceptual adaptation in robust visual navigation. Motivated by the decomposition of VFE into prediction error and Bayesian surprise, FEP-Nav combines a Top-down Decoder, which provides an internal expectation of uncorrupted sensory input, with Adaptive Normalisation, which adjusts shifted feature distributions toward prior statistics. We interpret reconstruction and normalisation as approximate mechanisms for reducing the corresponding VFE-related terms during inference without gradient-based updates. Experiments across simulated and real-world visual corruptions show that FEP-Nav restores performance lost under visual corruption, outperforming non-adaptive baselines and strong adaptive methods. These results suggest that variational principles can provide a useful design perspective for robust autonomous behaviour under degraded sensory conditions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：穿透不确定性：基于自由能原理的实时自适应视觉导航
在自然世界中导航是适应性推断的体现，生物体能在嘈杂且不完整的感官输入下维持目标导向行为。自由能原理（FEP）是这一能力的核心，它认为感知是一种生成过程，大脑通过最小化变分自由能（VFE）来维持对世界的准确内部模型。尽管深度神经网络（DNN）已成为生物大脑的强大类比，但它们通常缺乏处理突发感官变化所需的实时可塑性。我们提出了FEP-Nav，一种受生物启发的框架，用于在鲁棒视觉导航中实现实时感知适应。该框架受VFE分解为预测误差和贝叶斯惊讶的启发，结合自上而下的解码器（提供未受干扰感官输入的内部预期）与自适应归一化（调整移位的特征分布以接近先验统计）。我们将重建和归一化解释为推理过程中无需梯度更新的近似机制，用于减少相应的VFE相关项。在模拟和真实世界视觉干扰下的实验表明，FEP-Nav能恢复因视觉干扰而损失的性能，优于非自适应基线和强自适应方法。这些结果表明，变分原理可为在退化感官条件下实现鲁棒自主行为提供有益的设计视角。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for real-time sensory adaptation in autonomous navigation under noisy conditions, this work introduces FEP-Nav, a biologically inspired framework grounded in the Free Energy Principle. It combines a top-down decoder that generates internal expectations of clean sensory input with adaptive normalization that realigns shifted feature distributions toward prior statistics, approximating variational free energy minimization without gradient updates. Experiments in both simulated and real-world visual corruption scenarios demonstrate that FEP-Nav effectively recovers navigation performance degraded by sensory noise, surpassing non-adaptive baselines and competitive adaptive methods.
为提升自主导航系统在视觉噪声环境下的实时适应能力，本文提出FEP-Nav框架，受自由能原理启发，将变分自由能分解为预测误差与贝叶斯惊讶项，通过自上而下的解码器生成内部期望，结合自适应归一化调整特征分布以贴近先验统计。该方法无需梯度更新，在推理中近似降低自由能。实验表明，FEP-Nav在模拟与真实视觉干扰场景下显著优于非自适应基线及强自适应方法，验证了变分原理在鲁棒感知设计中的有效性。
Benchmarking Multimodal Large Language Models for Scientific Visualization Literacy
Authors: Patrick Phuoc Do, Chau M. Ta, Chaoli Wang
First: 2026-07-16T16:29:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:29:34+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) are increasingly used to interpret visualizations, yet current evaluations remain largely chart-centric and provide limited evidence of understanding of scientific visualization (SciVis). We benchmark six MLLMs on the scientific visualization literacy assessment test, a standardized SciVis literacy assessment comprising 49 items based on 18 scientific visualizations and illustrations, spanning 8 techniques and 11 task types. We evaluate three closed-source and three open-source models under a closed-world protocol and compare their performance using data from 485 human participants. Results show that current MLLMs do not exhibit uniform SciVis literacy. Gemini is the strongest model overall, exceeding the human mean across the evaluated subsets, whereas the open-source models remain below the human baseline. Performance is highly uneven across techniques and tasks: models perform best on scientific illustration, search, and spatial understanding, but struggle on texture-based and integration-based visualizations and on quantitative estimation. Error analysis reveals recurring failures in fine-grained quantitative estimation, flow-direction interpretation, and grounded encoding interpretation. These findings position SciVis literacy as a necessary benchmark dimension for evaluating multimodal AI systems. Our code and model outputs are publicly available at https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark.
中文标题/摘要
标题：科学可视化素养的多模态大语言模型基准评测
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）正越来越多地用于解释可视化图表，但当前评估仍主要围绕图表展开，对科学可视化（SciVis）理解能力的证据有限。我们对六种MLLMs在科学可视化素养评估测试中进行基准评测，该测试包含18个科学可视化图表与插图，涵盖8种技术与11种任务类型，共49道题目。我们在封闭世界协议下评估了三种闭源模型和三种开源模型，并与485名人类参与者的测试数据进行对比。结果显示，当前MLLMs在SciVis素养方面表现不一：Gemini整体表现最佳，超过人类平均值；而开源模型仍低于人类基准。模型在不同技术和任务上的表现差异显著：在科学插图、搜索与空间理解任务上表现最佳，但在基于纹理与整合的可视化及定量估算任务上表现不佳。错误分析显示，模型在细粒度定量估算、流方向解读及基于语义的编码解读方面存在反复失败。这些发现表明，SciVis素养应成为评估多模态AI系统的重要基准维度。我们的代码与模型输出已公开于https://github.com/patdmp/mllm-scivis-lit-benchmark。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate multimodal large language models (MLLMs) beyond chart-centric benchmarks, this study assesses six MLLMs—three closed-source and three open-source—using a standardized scientific visualization literacy test comprising 49 items across 18 visualizations, 8 techniques, and 11 task types. Evaluated under a closed-world protocol against data from 485 human participants, results show that Gemini outperforms human baselines across most subsets, while open-source models fall below human performance. Performance varies significantly by visualization technique and task type, with models excelling in scientific illustration, search, and spatial tasks but struggling with texture-based, integration-based visualizations, and quantitative estimation. Recurring errors include misinterpretation of flow direction and grounded encoding, highlighting SciVis literacy as a critical benchmark for multimodal AI.
为评估多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）对科学可视化内容的理解能力，本研究基于18个科学可视化图表、8种技术与11类任务构建49题标准化测试，对六款模型（三款闭源、三款开源）进行评测，采用闭域协议并对比485名人类参与者表现。结果显示，Gemini整体表现优于人类平均水平，而开源模型仍低于人类基准。模型在科学插图、搜索与空间理解任务上表现较好，但在纹理类与综合类可视化及定量估计任务上表现不佳，常见错误包括精细数值估计、流方向判断与编码映射理解。研究指出，科学可视化素养应成为评估多模态AI系统的关键基准维度。
Linear representations of grammaticality in neural language models
Authors: Jane Li, Najoung Kim
First: 2026-07-16T16:23:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:23:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Whether neural language models (NLMs) possess the ability to distinguish strings on the basis of their grammaticality remains a debated topic in the computational linguistics literature. Existing evidence has largely relied on probability-based measures, testing whether models assign higher probabilities to grammatical than ungrammatical strings. However, probability comparisons have been criticized as a measure for grammatical knowledge based on the assumption that grammaticality is inherently entangled with likelihood. Model-assigned probability is a function of many related sentence properties, such as lexical frequency, plausibility, and world knowledge. In this work, we move beyond probability-based evaluations and investigate whether grammaticality is encoded in the internal representations of NLMs. Using mass-mean probing, we test whether grammatical and ungrammatical sentences are systematically separated in representational space. We further examine the extent to which these representations are independent of sentence properties that are correlated with grammaticality, as well as their generalization across grammatical phenomena and languages. Our results provide evidence that grammaticality is robustly encoded in sentence representations of a wide range of pretrained NLMs, yielding clear representational separation on the dimension of grammaticality that cannot be fully explained by alternative sentence-level factors. Moreover, this encoding generalizes across a broad range of grammatical phenomena and to some degree, across languages, suggesting that grammaticality constitutes a coherent representational dimension in contemporary NLMs. These findings contribute new evidence to debates about the nature of syntactic knowledge in language models and offer a complementary framework for evaluating grammatical competence that is not dependent on string probabilities alone.
中文标题/摘要
标题：神经语言模型中句法正确性的线性表示
神经语言模型（NLMs）是否具备根据句法正确性区分字符串的能力，仍是计算语言学文献中的争议话题。现有证据主要依赖基于概率的度量，检验模型是否对句法正确字符串赋予更高概率。然而，概率比较因假设句法正确性与似然性内在关联而受到批评。模型分配的概率受多种相关句子属性影响，如词汇频率、合理性及世界知识。本研究超越概率评估，探究句法正确性是否编码于NLMs的内部表征中。通过质量均值探测，我们检验句法正确与不正确句子在表征空间中是否系统性分离。进一步考察这些表征在多大程度上独立于与句法正确性相关的句子属性，以及其在不同句法现象和语言间的泛化能力。结果表明，广泛预训练NLMs的句子表征中，句法正确性被稳健编码，形成无法被其他句子层面因素完全解释的明确表征分离。此外，该编码在广泛句法现象及一定程度上跨语言泛化，表明句法正确性在当代NLMs中构成一致的表征维度。这些发现为语言模型中句法知识本质的争论提供新证据，并提供一种不依赖字符串概率的语法能力评估框架。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether neural language models encode grammaticality as a distinct representational feature, moving beyond traditional probability-based assessments that are confounded by lexical frequency and plausibility. Using mass-mean probing, the authors analyze whether grammatical and ungrammatical sentences are systematically separated in model representations, while controlling for confounding sentence properties. Results show that grammaticality is robustly encoded across diverse pretrained models, with clear separability in representation space that cannot be fully explained by other sentence-level factors. This encoding generalizes across multiple grammatical phenomena and to some extent across languages, suggesting grammaticality forms a coherent dimension in NLMs.
该研究旨在探讨神经语言模型是否以独立于概率的内部表征方式编码句法正确性，突破传统依赖概率比较的方法。通过大规模均值探测，作者检验了语法句与非语法句在模型内部表征空间中的系统性分离情况，并控制词频、语义合理性等干扰因素。结果表明，句法正确性在多种预训练模型中被稳健编码，且其表征分离无法完全由其他句子属性解释。该编码在不同句法现象和一定程度上跨语言中具有一致性，表明现代NLMs中句法正确性构成一个连贯的表征维度。
From Reconstruction to Interpretation: Zero-Setup Multi-Phase Segmentation of X-ray Tomography Data
Authors: Pradyumna Elavarthi, Arun J. Bhattacharjee, Harrison Lisabeth, Anca Ralescu, Petrus H. Zwart, Dilworth Parkinson, Elizabeth G. Clark
First: 2026-07-13T21:44:39+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:22:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
X-ray tomography enables nondestructive characterization of material microstructures, while advances in micro-CT imaging have accelerated volumetric data acquisition and reconstruction. However, rapid interpretation remains limited by image segmentation, which often requires manual thresholding, user prompting, or material-specific model training. We present a zero-setup framework for multi-phase segmentation of synchrotron X-ray tomography data that generates interpretable masks for previously unseen datasets without user input or retraining during deployment. The framework combines a material-agnostic mask preparation strategy with a pretrained semantic segmentation network. It represents commonly occurring structural regions as background, sample, bright, dark-gray, light-gray, and porosity masks. Unlike conventional deep learning pipelines that require dataset-specific annotations and retraining, the proposed framework can be applied directly to new scans and produce diagnostic-level segmentations within minutes of reconstruction. This enables rapid assessment of scan quality, sample morphology, porosity, and attenuation variations during ongoing beamline experiments. The generated masks can later be manually refined or used to fine-tune application-specific models when greater accuracy or material-specific labeling is required. Evaluation on held-out synchrotron micro-CT images and qualitative testing on additional datasets demonstrate consistent and physically meaningful segmentations across varying samples and imaging conditions. The framework also substantially outperforms conventional intensity-based thresholding. By connecting high-speed reconstruction with immediate interpretation, the approach supports near-real-time beamline feedback and scalable AI-assisted scientific imaging workflows.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从重建到解释：零配置多相X射线断层扫描数据分割
X射线断层扫描可实现材料微观结构的无损表征，而微CT成像技术的进步加速了体数据采集与重建。然而，快速解释仍受限于图像分割，通常需手动阈值设定、用户提示或材料特定模型训练。本文提出一种零配置框架，用于同步辐射X射线断层扫描数据的多相分割，无需用户输入或重新训练即可对未见过的数据集生成可解释掩膜。该框架结合材料无关的掩膜准备策略与预训练语义分割网络，将常见结构区域表示为背景、样品、亮区、深灰色、浅灰色及孔隙掩膜。与传统深度学习流程需特定数据集标注和重新训练不同，该框架可直接应用于新扫描数据，在重建后数分钟内生成诊断级分割结果，从而支持在运行中的束线实验中快速评估扫描质量、样品形貌、孔隙率及衰减变化。生成的掩膜可后续手动优化，或用于微调应用特定模型以获得更高精度或材料特定标注。在保留测试集同步辐射微CT图像上的评估及额外数据集的定性测试表明，该框架在不同样品和成像条件下均能生成一致且物理意义明确的分割结果，且显著优于传统基于强度的阈值分割方法。通过将高速重建与即时解释相结合，该方法支持近实时束线反馈及可扩展的AI辅助科学成像工作流程。

It combines a material-agnostic mask preparation strategy with a pretrained semantic segmentation network to generate interpretable masks—such as background, sample, bright, dark-gray, light-gray, and porosity—for unseen datasets. Evaluated on held-out synchrotron micro-CT images and additional datasets, the method consistently produces physically meaningful segmentations across diverse samples and imaging conditions, outperforming conventional intensity-based thresholding and enabling near-real-time feedback during beamline experiments.
为解决X射线断层扫描数据快速自动解读中需人工干预或模型重训练的问题，本文提出一种零配置多相分割框架，适用于同步辐射微CT数据。该方法结合材料无关的掩码预处理策略与预训练语义分割网络，自动生成背景、样品、亮区、深灰、浅灰及孔隙等区域掩码。在多个独立数据集上验证，该框架在不同样品与成像条件下均能稳定输出物理意义明确的分割结果，显著优于传统强度阈值法。它可在重建后数分钟内完成诊断级分析，支持实时束线反馈与可扩展的AI辅助科学成像流程。
Beyond Interestingness: Semantic and Context-Aware Natural Language Query Recommendations for Visual Data Analysis
Authors: Xingbo Wang, Siyuan Li, Furui Cheng, Yong Wang, Jiang Long, Hong Lu, Huamin Qu, Ke Xu
First: 2022-01-13T10:20:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:21:53+00:00
Comments: This is the preprint version of the paper accepted to Visual Informatics journal
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made natural language interfaces (NLIs) widely accessible for data exploration, yet analysts who have a broad analytical objective still face the challenge of decomposing it into effective step-by-step queries, especially over unfamiliar, multi-table relational databases. Rather than generating high-level analytical agendas, we investigate how to augment an NLI with semantic- and context-aware next-step query recommendations that act as analytical scaffolding for relational database exploration. Our approach goes beyond interestingness-only methods by jointly integrating semantic relevance, data interestingness, and context coherence to guide exploration toward coherent, topic-focused analyses and potentially insightful subsets. We evaluate QRec-NLI with NL2SQL benchmarking, LLM-enhanced description validation, agentic comparisons against interestingness-only and LLM-based prompting baselines, and a 12-participant user study. In the agentic comparison, QRec-NLI yields more topically relevant and locally coherent query sequences than both baselines. In the user study against the interestingness-only baseline, it receives stronger ratings for insight-generation support and decision support.
中文标题/摘要
标题：超越趣味性：面向视觉数据分析的语义与上下文感知自然语言查询推荐
大型语言模型（LLMs）的最新进展使自然语言接口（NLIs）广泛应用于数据探索，但分析师在面对广泛分析目标时，仍需将目标分解为有效的分步查询，尤其在处理不熟悉的多表关系数据库时。我们不生成高层分析计划，而是研究如何通过语义与上下文感知的下一步查询推荐增强NLI，为关系数据库探索提供分析支撑。我们的方法超越了仅关注趣味性的方法，通过联合整合语义相关性、数据趣味性与上下文连贯性，引导探索走向连贯、主题聚焦的分析及潜在有洞察力的子集。我们通过NL2SQL基准测试、LLM增强的描述验证、与仅趣味性方法及基于LLM提示的基线方法的代理比较，以及12名参与者用户研究评估QRec-NLI。在代理比较中，QRec-NLI生成的查询序列在主题相关性和局部连贯性上优于两个基线。在与仅趣味性基线的用户研究中，其在洞察生成支持和决策支持方面获得更高评分。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge analysts face in decomposing broad analytical goals into effective step-by-step queries over unfamiliar relational databases, this paper proposes QRec-NLI, a semantic- and context-aware natural language query recommendation system that acts as analytical scaffolding. The method jointly integrates semantic relevance, data interestingness, and context coherence to guide users toward coherent, topic-focused query sequences. Experimental results show that QRec-NLI outperforms both interestingness-only and LLM-based prompting baselines in agentic comparisons for topical relevance and local coherence, and receives higher user ratings for insight generation and decision support in a 12-participant study.
为了解决分析师在面对不熟悉的关系数据库时难以将宏观分析目标分解为有效步骤查询的问题，本文提出QRec-NLI系统，通过语义与上下文感知的推荐机制增强自然语言接口。该方法联合优化语义相关性、数据有趣性与上下文连贯性，引导用户进行主题聚焦、连贯的探索。实验表明，QRec-NLI在生成主题相关且局部连贯的查询序列方面优于仅基于有趣性或大语言模型提示的基线方法，且在12人用户研究中，用户对其生成洞察力与决策支持能力的评分显著更高。
AHEAD: Anticipatory Hand-Driven Teleoperation via Human Intent Prediction
Authors: Seok Joon Kim, Junho Lee, Federica Spinola, Taein Kwon, Mohsen Moghaddam
First: 2026-07-16T16:21:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:21:40+00:00
Comments: Accepted to IROS2026, 8 pages, 6 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Direct hand-driven teleoperation maps an operator's hand motion to robot end-effector commands at every frame, enabling precise control, but it requires constant monitoring and correction during approach, grasp, and placement, which can be slow and fatiguing. For repetitive pick-and-place tasks, supervisory (goal-based) teleoperation simplifies this process: the operator specifies goals/waypoints, and the robot executes the motion using planning algorithms. Yet, this introduces latency, as the robot must wait for the next command before it can plan and act. "How can we reduce robot reaction time while lowering operator workload?" To tackle this question, we present AHEAD, a real-time VR teleoperation system that anticipates operator intent to enable proactive, hand-driven control. In a digital twin, the operator performs pick-and-place naturally, using hand motion to convey high-level commands rather than a continuous robot trajectory. AHEAD processes a short window of 3D hand and head signals together with scene context through an attention-based classifier to predict the intended grasp object and placement slot. A state machine converts intent predictions into stable robot goals, enabling early motion while remaining stable under noisy predictions and corrective hand movements. AHEAD's intent prediction module achieves Top1 accuracy: 76% for grasp objects and 76% for target slots. Moreover, our user study shows AHEAD reduces robot reaction latency by 0.6 s (object) and 1.4 s (slot) relative to baselines. Participants also reported lower operator load, indicating faster robot responses while maintaining low operator effort in practice.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AHEAD：通过人类意图预测实现预判式手控遥操作
直接手控遥操作将操作者的手部运动实时映射为机器人末端执行器指令，实现精确控制，但需在接近、抓取和放置过程中持续监控与修正，易导致缓慢和疲劳。对于重复的取放任务，监督式（目标导向）遥操作可简化流程：操作者指定目标或路径点，机器人通过规划算法执行运动。然而，这引入了延迟，因机器人需等待下一条指令才能规划并行动。如何在降低操作者负担的同时减少机器人响应时间？为此，我们提出AHEAD，一种实时VR遥操作系统，通过预测操作者意图实现主动式手控。在数字孪生环境中，操作者自然完成取放任务，通过手部运动传达高层指令而非连续轨迹。AHEAD通过注意力机制分类器，结合3D手部与头部信号及场景上下文，预测目标抓取物体与放置槽位。状态机将意图预测转化为稳定机器人目标，实现早期运动，同时在预测噪声和手部修正动作下保持稳定。AHEAD意图预测模块准确率达：抓取物体76%，目标槽位76%。用户实验表明，AHEAD相较基线方法，机器人响应延迟分别降低0.6秒（物体）和1.4秒（槽位），且操作者报告负担更低，表明实际操作中机器人响应更快，操作者负担更轻。
Summary / 总结
To address the trade-off between precise hand-driven teleoperation and the latency of goal-based systems, AHEAD introduces a real-time VR teleoperation framework that predicts operator intent to enable proactive robot control. By processing a short window of 3D hand and head motion alongside scene context using an attention-based classifier, AHEAD anticipates which object to grasp and where to place it, converting predictions into stable robot goals via a state machine. This allows early motion initiation while remaining robust to noisy inputs or corrective hand movements. Experimental results show the system achieves 76% Top1 accuracy for both grasp object and target slot prediction, and reduces robot reaction latency by 0.6 seconds for object and 1.4 seconds for slot compared to baselines, while users reported lower perceived workload.
为解决手控遥操作精度高但反应延迟大、目标导向控制延迟低但操作负担重的问题，AHEAD提出一种基于意图预测的实时VR遥操作系统。该系统通过注意力机制分类器，结合3D手部与头部运动及场景上下文，预测抓取目标与放置槽位，准确率达76%。预测结果经状态机转化为稳定机器人目标，支持提前动作并抵抗噪声或修正动作干扰。实验表明，AHEAD相较基线将机器人反应延迟分别降低0.6秒（目标）和1.4秒（槽位），同时显著降低操作者负担。
MedFailBench: A Clinician-Built Open-Source Benchmark for Medical AI Safety Boundary Inspection
Authors: Goktug Ozkan
First: 2026-07-16T16:16:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:16:07+00:00
Comments: 6 pages; clinician-reviewed synthetic benchmark; no patient data
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Most medical AI benchmarks measure whether a model knows the correct answer. MedFailBench asks a different question: which safety boundary failed? We present a clinician-built synthetic benchmark and failure atlas that labels medical AI errors by severity (1--5) and safety gate type (missed urgent escalation, unsafe remote dosing, unsafe discharge reassurance, evidence fabrication, unsafe protocol execution, source support gap). The current public release (v0.2.1) contains 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases with severity annotations, a live HuggingFace leaderboard preview, a safety gate taxonomy, a clinical severity rubric, and an automated pipeline for archiving model-response screening runs. No patient data, clinical validation claims, or model rankings are included. MedFailBench is released under Apache-2.0 and CC-BY-4.0 and carries the Zenodo DOI 10.5281/zenodo.21205535.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MedFailBench：由临床医生构建的开源医疗AI安全边界检验基准
大多数医疗AI基准衡量模型是否知道正确答案。MedFailBench提出不同问题：哪个安全边界失效了？我们提供一个由临床医生构建的合成基准和失败图谱，按严重程度（1-5）和安全门类型（漏报紧急升级、远程用药不安全、出院建议不安全、证据伪造、执行协议不安全、来源支持缺失）标注医疗AI错误。当前公开版本（v0.2.1）包含44个经临床医生审核的合成案例及严重性标注、HuggingFace实时排行榜预览、安全门分类法、临床严重性评分标准，以及自动归档模型响应筛查运行的流程。不包含患者数据、临床验证声明或模型排名。MedFailBench采用Apache-2.0和CC-BY-4.0协议发布，DOI为10.5281/zenodo.21205535。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate medical AI not just for correctness but for safety boundary compliance, MedFailBench introduces a clinician-designed synthetic benchmark that identifies which specific safety gates are violated in model responses. The method involves creating 44 clinician-reviewed synthetic cases, each annotated with severity levels (1–5) and categorized by failure type such as missed escalation or unsafe dosing. Experimental results show the benchmark effectively captures diverse safety failures without relying on real patient data, enabling automated screening and leaderboard tracking while avoiding model rankings or clinical validation claims.
为评估医疗AI是否遵守安全边界而非仅正确性，MedFailBench由临床医生设计，构建了一个合成基准，按安全门类型和严重程度（1-5级）标注模型错误。该方法创建了44个经临床医生审核的匿名合成病例，标注了如不安全给药、漏报急症、伪造证据等失败模式。实验结果表明，该基准能有效识别并分类模型安全行为缺陷，且不依赖真实患者数据或临床验证。
The Industrialization of Research ; On AI-Driven Science and Its Consequences
Authors: Emmanuel Jeannot
First: 2026-07-16T16:14:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:14:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial intelligence is transforming scientific research -- not merely as a more powerful instrument, but as an autonomous participant in the research cycle itself. This transition constitutes, in the most precise sense of the term, the industrialization of research: a shift from a craft model, in which knowledge, method, and judgment are embedded in the researcher, to a pipeline model, in which these steps are decomposed, automated, and supervised. The US Department of Energy's Genesis Mission is the most ambitious current instantiation of this shift, but the fundamental questions it raises extend far beyond any single program. This essay examines seven such questions: the erosion of the intergenerational transmission of scientific competence; the growing opacity of AI-generated theories; the collapse of peer evaluation under a flood of machine-generated output; the unproven capacity of AI for paradigm-shifting discovery; the capture of the scientific agenda by political and industrial actors; the compounding of systematic errors in closed-loop pipelines; and the structural bifurcation of the global research community into incommensurable tiers. These concerns do not constitute an argument against AI-driven science -- whose demonstrated potential is real and significant. They constitute the conditions under which that potential can be responsibly pursued.
中文标题/摘要
标题：研究的工业化：人工智能驱动科学及其后果
人工智能正在重塑科学研究——不仅作为更强大的工具，更作为研究循环中的自主参与者。这一转变在最精确的意义上，即为研究的工业化：从知识、方法与判断嵌入研究者个人的工匠模式，转向将这些步骤分解、自动化与监督的流水线模式。美国能源部的Genesis计划是当前这一转变最雄心勃勃的体现，但其所引发的根本性问题远超任何单一项目。本文探讨七个此类问题：科学能力代际传承的削弱；人工智能生成理论日益增加的不透明性；在机器生成成果洪流下同行评审体系的崩溃；人工智能在范式性发现方面的未证实能力；科学议程被政治与工业利益方所主导；封闭式流水线中系统性错误的累积；以及全球研究社区在结构上分裂为不可通约的层级。这些关切并非反对人工智能驱动科学——其已证实的潜力真实而重大。它们构成了负责任地追求该潜力的前提条件。
Summary / 总结
This paper examines the industrialization of scientific research driven by AI, framing it as a shift from a craft-based model to an automated pipeline system. Motivated by the growing role of AI as an autonomous participant in the research cycle, the author analyzes seven critical consequences: loss of intergenerational knowledge transfer, opacity of AI-generated theories, collapse of peer review under machine output, unproven capacity for paradigm-shifting discovery, political-industrial capture of research agendas, compounding errors in closed-loop systems, and global bifurcation into unequal research tiers. The paper does not oppose AI-driven science but emphasizes the structural and epistemological conditions necessary for its responsible deployment.
本文探讨人工智能作为科研自主参与者带来的变革，指出科研正从依赖个人经验的工匠模式转向可自动化、模块化的工业流水线模式。以美国能源部Genesis项目为例，分析七个关键后果：科学能力代际传承的削弱、AI生成理论的透明度下降、机器产出导致同行评审失效、AI突破性发现能力未经验证、科研议程被政治与产业力量主导、闭环系统中系统性误差累积，以及全球科研体系分裂为不可通约的层级。文章并非反对AI驱动科研，而是强调在合理利用其潜力前必须正视这些结构性与认识论前提。
Primal-dual algorithm for contextual stochastic combinatorial optimization
Authors: Louis Bouvier, Thibault Prunet, Vincent Leclère, Axel Parmentier
First: 2025-05-07T19:37:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:13:22+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper introduces a novel approach to contextual stochastic optimization, integrating operations research and machine learning to address decision-making under uncertainty. Traditional methods often fail to leverage contextual information, which underscores the necessity for new algorithms. In this study, we utilize neural networks with combinatorial optimization layers to encode policies. Our goal is to minimize the empirical cost, which is estimated from past data on uncertain parameters and contexts. To that end, we present a surrogate learning problem and a generic primal-dual algorithm that is applicable to various combinatorial settings in stochastic optimization. Our approach extends classic Fenchel--Young loss results and introduces a new regularization method using sparse perturbations on the distribution simplex. This allows for tractable updates in the original space and can accommodate diverse objective functions. We establish sublinear convergence for the exact linear-parametric version and provide a bound on the non-optimality of the resulting policy in terms of the empirical cost. Experiments on three contextual stochastic optimization problems show that our algorithm is efficient and scalable, achieving performance comparable to state-of-the-art baselines with significantly reduced computational requirements.

标题：用于上下文随机组合优化的原始-对偶算法
本文提出了一种新颖的上下文随机优化方法，结合运筹学与机器学习以解决不确定性下的决策问题。传统方法往往无法利用上下文信息，凸显了新算法的必要性。本研究利用带有组合优化层的神经网络编码策略，目标是最小化从历史数据中估计的实证成本（基于不确定参数和上下文）。为此，我们提出了一个代理学习问题及一个通用的原始-对偶算法，适用于多种随机优化中的组合设置。我们的方法扩展了经典的Fenchel-Young损失结果，并引入了一种基于分布单纯形稀疏扰动的新正则化方法，从而在原始空间中实现可计算的更新，并能适应多样化的目标函数。我们证明了精确线性参数版本的次线性收敛性，并给出了所得策略相对于实证成本的非最优性上界。在三个上下文随机优化问题上的实验表明，我们的算法高效且可扩展，性能与现有最先进基线相当，但计算需求显著降低。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle decision-making under uncertainty while leveraging contextual information, this paper proposes a primal-dual algorithm for contextual stochastic combinatorial optimization. It employs neural networks with combinatorial optimization layers to encode policies and formulates a surrogate learning problem to minimize empirical cost derived from historical data. The method introduces a novel regularization via sparse perturbations on the distribution simplex, enabling tractable updates and broad applicability across combinatorial settings. Theoretical guarantees include sublinear convergence for the linear-parametric version and a bound on policy non-optimality. Empirical results on three problems demonstrate the algorithm’s efficiency and scalability, matching state-of-the-art performance while requiring substantially less computation.
为解决在不确定性环境下利用上下文信息进行决策的问题，本文提出了一种用于上下文随机组合优化的对偶算法。该方法采用带有组合层的神经网络编码策略，并构建代理学习问题以最小化由历史数据估计的实证成本。算法引入通用的对偶框架，扩展了Fenchel--Young损失并结合分布单纯形上的稀疏扰动，实现可计算更新和灵活的目标函数支持。该算法在参数线性情况下实现次线性收敛，并对策略非最优性给出实证成本的理论界。在三个实验问题中，算法展现出高效性和可扩展性，性能媲美当前最优方法，同时计算开销显著降低。
Octopus-inspired Distributed Control for Soft Robotic Arms: A Graph Neural Network-Based Attention Policy with Environmental Interaction
Authors: Linxin Hou, Qirui Wu, Zhihang Qin, Yongxin Guo, Cecilia Laschi
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-03-10T19:56:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:10:04+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 5 figures, 2 tables, accepted by IROS 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper proposes SoftGM, an octopus-inspired distributed control architecture for segmented soft robotic arms that learn to reach targets in contact-rich environments using online obstacle discovery without assuming full obstacle geometry at the start of each episode. SoftGM formulates each arm section as a cooperative agent and represents the arm-environment interaction as a graph. SoftGM uses a two-stage graph attention message passing scheme following a Centralised Training Decentralised Execution (CTDE) paradigm with a centralised critic and decentralised actor. We evaluate SoftGM in a Cosserat-rod simulator (PyElastica) across three tasks that increase the complexity of the environment: obstacle-free, structured obstacles, and a wall-with-hole scenario. Compared with six widely used MARL baselines (IDDPG, IPPO, ISAC, MADDPG, MAPPO, MASAC) under identical information content and training conditions, SoftGM matches strong CTDE methods in simpler settings and achieves the best performance in the wall-with-hole task. Robustness tests with observation noise, single-section actuation failure, and transient disturbances show that SoftGM maintains comparable performance under non-ideal simulated conditions while keeping control effort bounded, suggesting that selective contact-relevant information routing improves resilience in the tested settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：受章鱼启发的软体机械臂分布式控制：基于图神经网络的注意力策略与环境交互
本文提出SoftGM，一种受章鱼启发的分布式控制架构，用于分段软体机械臂在接触密集环境中通过在线障碍物发现学习目标到达，无需假设每个回合开始时已知完整障碍物几何形状。SoftGM将每个机械臂段视为协作智能体，并将机械臂-环境交互建模为图结构。SoftGM采用两阶段图注意力消息传递机制，遵循集中训练分散执行（CTDE）范式，配备集中式评论家与分散式执行者。我们在Cosserat杆模拟器（PyElastica）中评估SoftGM，涵盖三个环境复杂度递增的任务：无障碍、结构化障碍物及带孔墙壁场景。在相同信息内容与训练条件下，与六种广泛使用的多智能体强化学习基线（IDDPG、IPPO、ISAC、MADDPG、MAPPO、MASAC）相比，SoftGM在简单场景中表现与强CTDE方法相当，并在带孔墙壁任务中取得最佳性能。在观测噪声、单段执行失效及瞬态干扰等鲁棒性测试中，SoftGM在非理想模拟条件下仍保持相近性能，且控制努力有界，表明选择性接触相关信息路由可提升其在测试场景中的鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the decentralized coordination of octopus arms, this work introduces SoftGM, a distributed control framework for segmented soft robotic arms that learns to navigate contact-rich environments without prior knowledge of obstacle geometry. The method models each arm segment as an agent and encodes arm-environment interactions via a graph, employing a two-stage graph attention mechanism under a Centralised Training Decentralised Execution (CTDE) paradigm with a central critic and decentralized actors. Evaluated in a Cosserat-rod simulator across three increasing environmental complexity tasks, SoftGM outperforms six MARL baselines under identical conditions, matching top CTDE methods in simpler tasks and achieving the best performance in the most complex wall-with-hole scenario. Robustness tests under observation noise, actuation failure, and disturbances confirm its resilience while maintaining bounded control effort, indicating that selective routing of contact-relevant information enhances stability.
受章鱼触手分布式协调的启发，本文提出SoftGM框架，用于分段软机械臂在接触密集环境中学习目标抓取，无需预先知道障碍物几何形状。每个臂段被建模为一个智能体，通过图结构编码臂与环境的交互，采用两阶段图注意力机制，遵循集中训练、分散执行（CTDE）范式，配备集中式评论家和分散式执行者。在Cosserat杆模拟器中，针对三种递增复杂度的环境任务进行评估，SoftGM在相同信息与训练条件下优于六种主流多智能体强化学习基线，简单场景下匹配最优CTDE方法，墙洞场景中表现最佳。在观测噪声、单段失效及瞬态干扰等鲁棒性测试中，其性能保持稳定且控制能耗有界，表明选择性传递接触相关信息可提升系统在测试环境中的稳定性。
Scaling Behavior Foundation Model for Humanoid Robots
Authors: Weishuai Zeng, Kangning Yin, Xiaojie Niu, Shunlin Lu, Weixiang Zhong, Jiahe Chen, Feiyu Jia, Xiao Chen, Zirui Wang, Furui Xu, Ming Zhou, Kailin Li, Weinan Zhang, He Wang, Li Yi, Dahua Lin, Jiangmiao Pang, Jingbo Wang
First: 2026-07-16T16:08:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:08:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Humanoid control requires natural whole-body coordination, precise real-time responses to control signals, and robust generalization across diverse environmental contexts, making it a cornerstone for generalist embodied agents. Behavior Foundation Models (BFMs) have recently emerged as a promising solution to address these challenges by leveraging large-scale behavioral data to achieve superior expressiveness, versatility and generalization. However, despite growing interest in scaling BFMs to further improve their capabilities, it remains unclear how key factors, including the learning paradigm, behavioral data and model architecture should be coordinated to enable effective scaling. In this work, we revisit the scaling recipe for BFMs and demonstrate that substantial performance gains can be achieved through the coordination of three core components: 1) the learning paradigm of motion tracking that reformulates diverse humanoid control problems as the reproduction of integrated whole-body behaviors in the global frame; 2) the strategic synergy between on-policy rollout quantity and reference motion diversity; and 3) the expressive and scalable model architecture termed Humanoid Transformer that facilitates the natural emergence of structured behavioral representations. Through extensive experiments in both simulation and real-world deployment, we demonstrate that our approach yields significant improvements in control fidelity and task generalization, reducing Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE) on the test set by over 10% in local mode and 82% in global mode compared with existing humanoid controllers. These results establish BFM as a principled and effective foundation for scalable and general-purpose humanoid control.
中文标题/摘要
标题：人形机器人行为基础模型的扩展行为研究
人形机器人控制需要自然的全身协调、对控制信号的精确实时响应，以及在多样化环境中的鲁棒泛化能力，使其成为通用具身智能体的核心。行为基础模型（BFMs）近期作为一种有前景的解决方案出现，通过利用大规模行为数据实现卓越的表达能力、多功能性和泛化能力。然而，尽管对扩展BFMs以进一步提升其能力的兴趣日益增长，目前尚不清楚如何协调学习范式、行为数据和模型架构等关键因素以实现有效扩展。在本研究中，我们重新审视BFMs的扩展方法，并证明通过协调三个核心组件可实现显著性能提升：1）将运动跟踪学习范式重构为在全局坐标系下再现整合全身行为，以解决多样化人形控制问题；2）策略性协调在线策略回放量与参考运动多样性；3）采用名为“人形Transformer”的表达力强且可扩展的模型架构，促进结构化行为表征的自然涌现。通过在仿真和真实部署中的大量实验，我们证明该方法在控制保真度和任务泛化能力上显著优于现有的人形控制器，测试集上的平均关键点位置误差（MPKPE）在局部模式下降低10%以上，在全局模式下降低82%。这些结果确立了BFM作为可扩展、通用人形控制的原理性且有效的基础。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of achieving natural, real-time, and generalizable whole-body control in humanoid robots by proposing a scalable Behavior Foundation Model (BFM) framework. The authors identify that scaling BFMs effectively requires coordinated optimization of three components: a motion-tracking learning paradigm that frames control as global whole-body behavior reproduction, a balanced strategy between on-policy rollout volume and reference motion diversity, and a Humanoid Transformer architecture that supports structured behavioral representations. Experimental results across simulation and real-world tests show significant performance gains, with Mean Per-Keypoint Position Error (MPKPE) reduced by over 10% in local mode and 82% in global mode compared to existing controllers, demonstrating the framework’s effectiveness in improving control fidelity and task generalization.
本文旨在解决人形机器人实现自然、实时且泛化能力强的全身协调控制问题，提出了一种可扩展的行为基础模型（BFM）框架。作者指出，有效扩展BFM需协调三个核心要素：将控制问题重构为全局坐标系下全身行为再现的运动跟踪学习范式、平衡在线策略回滚量与参考动作多样性、以及支持结构化行为表征的“人形Transformer”架构。实验在仿真与真实部署中均表明，该方法显著提升控制精度与泛化能力，测试集上的平均关键点位置误差（MPKPE）较现有控制器降低10%以上（局部模式）和82%（全局模式），验证了BFM作为可扩展人形控制基础的有效性。
On-Policy Delta Distillation
Authors: Byeongho Heo, Jaehui Hwang, Sangdoo Yun, Dongyoon Han
First: 2026-07-16T16:07:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:07:19+00:00
Comments: 19 pages, 4 figures, 12 tables
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
On-policy distillation is an alternative post-training method in reinforcement learning that alleviates the constraints imposed by reward models by providing token-level supervision from a teacher model. Although on-policy distillation has been studied and applied across various settings, its fundamental design remains underexplored. In this paper, we introduce a new distillation reward, termed the delta signal, instead of directly imitating the teacher's output distribution. The delta signal is defined as the difference between the teacher model and its base model prior to instruction tuning for reasoning capability. It therefore captures the changes induced by reasoning tuning and provides a more direct signal for transferring reasoning capabilities. Using extensive empirical evidence, we show that the delta signal substantially improves on-policy distillation and refer to the new distillation method as On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$). Experiments across mathematics, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks demonstrate that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms conventional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with only a short post-training period. Code will be available at https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2
中文标题/摘要
标题：在线策略Delta蒸馏
在线策略蒸馏是强化学习中一种替代的后训练方法，通过教师模型提供的词级监督缓解奖励模型的约束。尽管在线策略蒸馏已在多种场景中被研究和应用，其基本设计仍待深入探索。本文引入一种新的蒸馏奖励——Delta信号，而非直接模仿教师模型的输出分布。Delta信号定义为教师模型与其在推理能力调优前的基础模型之间的差异，因此捕捉了推理调优所引发的变化，并为推理能力的迁移提供更直接的信号。通过大量实证证据，我们证明Delta信号显著提升了在线策略蒸馏效果，并将该新方法命名为在线策略Delta蒸馏（OPD²）。在数学、科学和代码推理基准测试中，OPD²持续优于传统在线策略蒸馏，使推理大语言模型仅需短暂后训练即可实现强性能。代码将在https://github.com/naver-ai/opd2公开。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing on-policy distillation methods that rely on teacher output imitation, this paper proposes On-Policy Delta Distillation (OPD$^2$), which uses a novel delta signal—defined as the difference between a teacher model and its base model before instruction tuning—to guide student model learning. This signal captures the reasoning-specific changes introduced by tuning, offering a more direct transfer mechanism. Experimental results across math, science, and code-reasoning benchmarks show that OPD$^2$ consistently outperforms traditional on-policy distillation, enabling reasoning LLMs to achieve strong performance with minimal post-training time.
为了解决现有基于教师输出模仿的在线蒸馏方法存在的局限性，本文提出了一种新的在线蒸馏方法——On-Policy Delta Distillation（OPD$^2$），其核心是引入“delta信号”，即教师模型在推理能力调优前与基础模型的输出差异。该信号直接捕捉推理能力调优带来的变化，从而更有效地传递推理能力。在数学、科学和代码推理多个基准测试中，OPD$^2$ 表现显著优于传统在线蒸馏方法，使推理大语言模型能在极短的后训练阶段达到优异性能。
Temporal Cascading of Planning and Control for Quadrotor MPC
Authors: Rudolf Reiter, Chao Qin, Leonard Bauersfeld, Davide Scaramuzza
First: 2025-12-13T18:53:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:04:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Many aerial tasks involving quadrotors demand both instant reactivity and long-horizon planning for obstacle avoidance, energy efficiency, or trajectory tracking. High-fidelity models enable accurate control but are too slow for long horizons. Low-fidelity planners scale but cannot directly control the system, necessitating cascaded architectures. Prevailing hierarchical approaches plan with a simplified model and use a high-fidelity controller for tracking, yet this decomposition is inherently suboptimal. The controller is limited by the coarse plan, and conventional MPC alternatives shorten the horizon to stay real-time feasible. We present UNIQUE, an MPC architecture that replaces this hierarchical stacking with temporal cascading. The planning problem is formulated as the second-tail horizon of a single multi-phase MPC, rather than being solved separately. We align costs across horizons, derive feasibility constraints for the point-mass planning model, and introduce transition constraints that convert high-fidelity states into meaningful low-fidelity states. Parallel point-mass and mixed-integer solvers address nonconvexities while incorporating progressive 3D obstacle smoothing over the planning horizon. In simulations and real flights, under equal computational budgets, UNIQUE improves closed-loop tracking by up to 75% compared with standard MPC and hierarchical baselines. Ablations and Pareto analyses confirm performance gains across variations in horizon, constraint approximations, and smoothing schedules.
中文标题/摘要
标题：四旋翼机MPC的规划与控制时序级联
许多涉及四旋翼机的空中任务既需要即时响应，又需要长时域规划以实现避障、节能或轨迹跟踪。高保真模型虽能实现精确控制，但计算速度过慢，难以支持长时域。低保真规划器虽可扩展，却无法直接控制系统，需采用级联架构。现有分层方法使用简化模型规划，再用高保真控制器跟踪，但该分解本质上非最优：控制器受限于粗略规划，而传统MPC方法为保持实时性缩短时域。本文提出UNIQUE，一种MPC架构，用时序级联替代分层堆叠。规划问题被建模为单个多阶段MPC的第二尾时域，而非独立求解。我们对各时域成本进行对齐，推导点质量规划模型的可行性约束，并引入转换约束，将高保真状态转化为有意义的低保真状态。并行点质量与混合整数求解器处理非凸性，同时在规划时域内逐步平滑3D障碍物。仿真与真实飞行测试表明，在同等计算预算下，UNIQUE相比标准MPC和分层基线方法，闭环跟踪性能提升最高达75%。消融实验与帕累托分析证实，其性能优势在时域、约束近似及平滑调度等参数变化下均成立。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for quadrotors to balance real-time responsiveness with long-term planning for tasks like obstacle avoidance and trajectory tracking, this work introduces UNIQUE, a novel MPC architecture that replaces traditional hierarchical planning-control structures with temporal cascading. Instead of solving planning and control separately, UNIQUE formulates the planning problem as the second phase of a single multi-phase MPC, aligning costs across horizons and introducing transition constraints to bridge high- and low-fidelity models. By using parallel solvers for point-mass and mixed-integer problems and progressively smoothing 3D obstacles, the method maintains feasibility while handling nonconvexities. Experiments show that under equal computational budgets, UNIQUE improves closed-loop tracking performance by up to 75% over standard MPC and hierarchical baselines, with ablation studies confirming robust gains across horizon lengths, constraint approximations, and smoothing schedules.
为解决四旋翼飞行器在避障、节能与轨迹跟踪任务中需兼顾实时响应与长时规划的矛盾，本文提出UNIQUE架构，通过时序级联替代传统分层规划方法。该方法将规划与控制统一为单个多阶段MPC问题，通过跨时域成本对齐与状态转换约束，将高保真状态映射为低保真状态，实现规划与控制的协同优化。采用并行点质量与混合整数求解器处理非凸性，并引入渐进式三维障碍平滑机制。实验表明，在相同计算预算下，UNIQUE较标准MPC与分层基线方法提升闭环跟踪性能最高达75%，消融分析证实其在规划时域、约束近似与平滑策略变化下均保持性能优势。
New non-Euclidean neural quantum states from additional types of hyperbolic recurrent neural networks
Authors: H. L. Dao
First: 2026-04-27T11:27:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:03:32+00:00
Comments: v2: additional improved results added, new discussions added, main conclusions remain unchanged
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this work, we extend the class of previously introduced non-Euclidean neural quantum states (NQS) which consists only of Poincare hyperbolic GRU, to new variants including Poincare RNN as well as Lorentz RNN and Lorentz GRU. In addition to constructing the new non-Euclidean hyperbolic NQS ansatzes, we generalize the results of our earlier work regarding the definitive outperformances delivered by hyperbolic Poincare GRU NQS when benchmarked against their Euclidean counterparts in the Variational Monte Carlo (VMC) experiments involving the Heisenberg $J_1J_2$ and $J_1J_2J_3$ models. Here, using larger systems consisting of 100 spins, we find that all four hyperbolic RNN/GRU NQS variants always outperform their respective Euclidean counterpart with the same architecture. In our experiments, among the four hyperbolic NQS, Lorentz RNN stands out in particular because despite having almost three times fewer parameters, it is capable of surpassing the more complex Poincare GRU and Lorentz GRU to emerge as the best overall hyperbolic NQS ansatz on many instances involving different $J_2$ and ($J_2, J_3$) couplings. Given the findings from this work showing that the four newly constructed hyperbolic RNN/GRU NQS ansatzes are able to outperform the well-established Euclidean RNN/GRU NQS in Heisenberg spin models, we establish the utility and efficiency of the hyperbolic Poincare RNN/GRU and Lorentz RNN/GRU NQS for future variational studies of quantum many-body systems, especially those exhibiting a hierarchical structure in the form of the different degrees of nearest-neighbor interactions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：新型非欧几里得神经量子态：基于额外类型的双曲递归神经网络
本工作中，我们将先前仅包含庞加莱双曲GRU的非欧几里得神经量子态（NQS）类扩展至包含庞加莱RNN、洛伦兹RNN及洛伦兹GRU的新变体。除构建新的非欧几里得双曲NQS近似外，我们还推广了此前关于庞加莱双曲GRU NQS在变分蒙特卡洛（VMC）实验中相对于欧几里得同类模型在海森堡$J_1J_2$和$J_1J_2J_3$模型中表现更优的结论。在涉及100个自旋的更大系统中，我们发现所有四种双曲RNN/GRU NQS变体均优于其对应欧几里得架构。在实验中，四种双曲NQS中，洛伦兹RNN表现尤为突出：尽管其参数量几乎仅为庞加莱GRU和洛伦兹GRU的三分之一，却能在多种不同$J_2$及($J_2, J_3$)耦合情形下超越后者，成为最优的整体双曲NQS近似。鉴于本工作发现四种新构建的双曲RNN/GRU NQS近似均优于传统欧几里得RNN/GRU NQS，我们确立了庞加莱双曲RNN/GRU及洛伦兹双曲RNN/GRU NQS在量子多体系统（特别是具有不同最近邻相互作用层次结构的系统）未来变分研究中的实用性和高效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve variational quantum state representations for spin systems with hierarchical interactions, this work introduces four new non-Euclidean neural quantum states (NQS) based on hyperbolic recurrent neural networks: Poincare RNN, Lorentz RNN, and their GRU variants. These models are tested against their Euclidean counterparts in Variational Monte Carlo simulations of Heisenberg $J_1J_2$ and $J_1J_2J_3$ models using 100-spin systems. All four hyperbolic variants consistently outperform their Euclidean equivalents, with Lorentz RNN showing the strongest performance despite having nearly three times fewer parameters than Poincare GRU, making it the most efficient ansatz across multiple coupling scenarios.
为提升具有层次化相互作用的量子多体系统的变分量子态表示能力，本研究提出了四种基于双曲递归神经网络的新非欧几里得神经量子态（NQS）：庞加莱RNN、洛伦兹RNN及其GRU变体，扩展了此前仅使用庞加莱GRU NQS的工作。这些模型在包含100个自旋的海森堡$J_1J_2$和$J_1J_2J_3$模型的变分蒙特卡洛模拟中，与欧氏对应模型对比，所有四种双曲变体均表现更优，其中参数量仅为庞加莱GRU近三倍的洛伦兹RNN在多数情况下表现最佳。结果表明，双曲RNN/GRU NQS是研究具有层次化相互作用量子系统的有效工具。
How Inference Compute Shapes Frontier LLM Evaluation
Authors: Jessica McFadyen, Ole Jorgensen, Harry Coppock, Kevin Wei, Cozmin Ududec
First: 2026-06-16T13:40:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:03:20+00:00
Comments: 34 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI evaluations are shifting toward harder tasks that benefit from longer trajectories involving tool use and iterative problem solving. As a result, performance is increasingly sensitive to the amount and allocation of compute available at test time ("inference compute"). Yet many evaluations still report performance at a single restrictive budget, meaning that low scores may reflect the evaluation setup rather than the model's underlying capability. To test this, we evaluate up to 12 frontier language models on seven challenging benchmarks spanning software engineering, mathematics, medicine, and cybersecurity. We use a controlled setup combining three simple inference-scaling interventions: larger token budgets, context compaction, and repeated submission attempts, guided either by the model itself or by minimal correctness feedback. We find three main results. First, larger token budgets substantially improve performance on benchmarks across multiple domains, including cybersecurity, FrontierMath, Humanity's Last Exam, and TerminalBench. Second, fixed-budget evaluations can increasingly understate frontier capability as models advance. Newer models reach higher performance at large budgets, where they unlock harder tasks and solve them more reliably. Third, benchmarks differ in which inference-scaling methods help most: repeated submission broadly improves performance, but the value of larger token budgets, external feedback, and parallel attempts varies by benchmark. Overall, our results show that benchmark scores are protocol-dependent. We therefore argue that evaluations should report capability as a function of inference-time compute, specify protocol choices explicitly, and compare model generations over a large shared compute range at matched budgets, especially in safety- or policy-relevant settings.
中文标题/摘要
标题：推理计算如何塑造前沿大语言模型的评估
AI评估正转向更难的任务，这些任务受益于涉及工具使用和迭代问题解决的更长轨迹。因此，性能越来越依赖于测试时可用的计算量（“推理计算”）的多少和分配方式。然而，许多评估仍仅在单一受限预算下报告性能，这意味着低分可能反映的是评估设置而非模型的底层能力。为此，我们对12个前沿语言模型在涵盖软件工程、数学、医学和网络安全的七个挑战性基准上进行评估。我们采用受控设置，结合三种简单的推理扩展干预措施：更大的token预算、上下文压缩和重复提交尝试，由模型自身或最小正确性反馈引导。我们发现三个主要结果：第一，更大的token预算显著提升了多个领域基准（包括网络安全、FrontierMath、Humanity's Last Exam和TerminalBench）的性能；第二，随着模型进步，固定预算评估越来越低估前沿能力，新模型在大预算下能实现更高性能，解锁更难任务并更可靠地解决；第三，不同基准对推理扩展方法的依赖不同：重复提交普遍提升性能，但更大token预算、外部反馈和并行尝试的价值因基准而异。总体而言，我们的结果表明基准分数具有协议依赖性。因此，我们主张评估应将能力表示为推理时计算量的函数，明确说明协议选择，并在安全或政策相关场景中，于匹配预算下，通过共享的大计算范围比较模型版本。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the growing importance of inference compute in evaluating frontier language models, this study investigates how performance scales with computational resources during testing. The authors evaluate 12 advanced models across seven challenging benchmarks using three controlled inference-scaling methods: increased token budgets, context compaction, and repeated submission attempts, either self-guided or with minimal feedback. Results show that larger token budgets consistently boost performance across domains including cybersecurity and mathematics. Fixed-budget evaluations increasingly fail to capture true model capability as newer models unlock harder tasks at higher compute. Benchmark sensitivity varies: repeated submissions help broadly, while token budget and feedback effectiveness differ by task. The study concludes that evaluation scores are protocol-dependent and recommends reporting performance as a function of inference compute, with explicit protocol disclosure and matched-budget comparisons across model generations, especially for safety-critical applications.
随着AI评估转向需要工具调用和迭代求解的更难任务，模型表现对推理计算资源的分配变得高度敏感。为解决单一预算评估可能低估模型能力的问题，研究对12个前沿语言模型在七个跨领域挑战性基准上进行了测试，采用三种可控的推理扩展干预：增大token预算、上下文压缩和由模型或最小反馈引导的多次提交。结果表明，增大token预算显著提升多个领域（如网络安全和数学）的表现；固定预算评估在模型进步时越来越无法反映真实能力；不同基准对干预措施的收益差异明显，其中多次提交普遍有效，但token预算收益因任务而异。研究结论认为基准分数高度依赖评估协议，建议以推理计算量为变量报告能力、明确说明协议选择，并在安全相关场景中于匹配预算下跨模型比较，以确保评估的公平性与有效性。
SAGA: Scene-Aware, Goal-Evolving Agents for Long-Horizon Strategy Game Planning
Authors: Tianyu Jin, Shuo Chen, Yida Wang, Liuyu Xiang, Yingzhuo Liu, Zhiyao Jiang, Yexin Li, Peipei Li, Zhaofeng He
First: 2026-06-29T08:07:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:02:25+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 4 figures. Code: https://github.com/KazeCloud/SAGA-Civrealm
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Long-horizon strategic planning in complex strategy games requires coordinating tightly coupled decision domains, including technology, economy, diplomacy, and military, across hundreds of turns under imperfect information. Existing LLM-based agents face three challenges in this setting. They recover little relational structure, such as distance or threat, from raw coordinate observations. They serialize the entire growing game state into every prompt and plan all domains in a single output, so the context eventually overflows and urgent domains crowd out long-term ones. The only reward is a lagged final score, which provides no progress signal within or across games.<br/><br/>We present SAGA, an LLM multi-agent framework that addresses these challenges with three mechanisms: a Map-Semantic Scene Graph that renders typed entity relations as concise per-entity text, a Tool-Augmented Planner that retrieves state on demand and routes disjoint per-domain directives to specialist controllers, and a Dual-Horizon Feedback Loop that sets intermediate goals within a game and distills causal lessons across games. On CivRealm, a benchmark built on the strategy game FreeCiv, SAGA attains the highest mean final score among six methods, the statistically strongest gains on infrastructure, and a 27% reduction in output tokens. With cross-game evolution enabled, it reaches the highest score over five successive games.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAGA：面向长时战略游戏规划的场景感知、目标演进型代理
在复杂策略游戏中进行长时战略规划，需在信息不完整的情况下，跨数百回合协调技术、经济、外交与军事等紧密耦合的决策领域。现有基于大语言模型的代理在此场景中面临三大挑战：难以从原始坐标观测中提取如距离或威胁等关系结构；将不断增长的游戏状态完整序列化至每轮提示中，并在单次输出中规划所有领域，导致上下文溢出，紧急领域挤压长期领域；唯一奖励为滞后最终得分，无法提供游戏内或跨游戏的进展信号。<br/><br/>我们提出SAGA，一种基于大语言模型的多代理框架，通过三种机制解决上述问题：地图语义场景图，将实体关系以简洁的实体文本形式呈现；工具增强规划器，按需检索状态并将各领域指令路由至专业控制器；双时域反馈环，设定游戏内中间目标，并跨游戏提炼因果经验。在基于FreeCiv构建的基准测试CivRealm上，SAGA在六种方法中取得最高平均最终得分，基础设施方面统计显著提升，并减少27%输出token。启用跨游戏演化后，其在连续五局游戏中得分最高。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of existing LLM agents in long-horizon strategy games, SAGA introduces a multi-agent framework that addresses three core issues: poor relational understanding, context overflow from serialized state, and lack of intermediate feedback. It employs a Map-Semantic Scene Graph to encode entity relations concisely, a Tool-Augmented Planner to handle domain-specific tasks via on-demand state retrieval and specialized controllers, and a Dual-Horizon Feedback Loop to set mid-game goals and distill cross-game lessons. Experiments on CivRealm show SAGA achieves the highest mean final score among six methods, statistically strongest infrastructure gains, and a 27% reduction in output tokens, with cross-game evolution further boosting performance across five consecutive games.
为解决复杂策略游戏中长期规划在信息不完整条件下面临的三大挑战——关系结构理解不足、状态序列化导致上下文溢出、缺乏中间反馈信号，SAGA提出一种多智能体LLM框架。该框架通过地图语义场景图以文本形式精简编码实体关系，工具增强规划器按需检索状态并分发各领域指令至专用控制器，以及双时间线反馈环设置中间目标并跨游戏提炼因果经验。在CivRealm基准测试中，SAGA在六种方法中取得最高平均最终得分，基础设施方面统计显著领先，输出token减少27%，并开启跨游戏进化后在五场连续游戏中持续保持最高得分。
Assessing Physical Frailty and Fall-Risk Indicators with Social Robots: An in situ Evaluation with Older Adults
Authors: Aniol Civit, Antonio Andriella, Alba Martínez, Joan Ars, Aida Ribera, Cristian Barrué, Guillem Alenyà
First: 2026-07-16T16:00:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T16:00:38+00:00
Comments: This work has been submitted to the IEEE for possible publication
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Frailty assessments are crucial to evaluate the risk of adverse events and the health and social care needs of older adults, yet their administration remains resource-intensive and typically relies on coarse clinical outcomes, such as task completion times, which may overlook biomechanical indicators of functional decline. To address this, we present a robotic framework that guides older adults through standardised frailty and fall-risk tests while capturing clinical scores and additional frailty-related metrics, offering a deeper insight into a user's condition. The system uses a Behaviour Tree architecture that coordinates perception, decision-making, interaction, and measurement modules. Using vision-based skeleton tracking, the robot evaluates established clinical tests, including the Short Physical Performance Battery (SPPB) and the Timed Up and Go (TUG). The framework was co-designed with healthcare professionals and evaluated in situ during six months in a rehabilitation centre's research lab with N=81 older adults.
