Robot-derived measurements were compared against therapist assessments and clinical reference instruments, including a gait analysis walkway and an inertial measurement unit (IMU). Results showed excellent agreement for most test completion times and gait-related parameters ($ICC > 0.9$). And, substantial agreement for the overall SPPB score comparing the robot and the therapist ($k = 0.67$) and moderate agreement comparing the robot and the IMU ($k=0.55$). The findings highlight that social robots can provide reliable and objective frailty assessments in healthcare settings while enabling the collection of relevant mobility indicators beyond conventional outcomes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用社交机器人评估老年人身体虚弱与跌倒风险指标：一项在真实环境中的评估
虚弱评估对评估老年人不良事件风险及其健康和社会护理需求至关重要，但其实施仍需大量资源，且通常依赖粗略的临床结果，如任务完成时间，可能忽略功能衰退的生物力学指标。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种机器人框架，引导老年人完成标准化的虚弱与跌倒风险测试，同时记录临床评分及额外的虚弱相关指标，从而更深入地了解用户状况。该系统采用行为树架构，协调感知、决策、交互与测量模块。通过基于视觉的骨架追踪，机器人评估了标准临床测试，包括短体力性能电池（SPPB）和起立行走测试（TUG）。该框架与医疗专业人员共同设计，并在康复中心研究实验室中对81名老年人进行了为期六个月的实地评估。机器人测量结果与治疗师评估及临床参考工具（包括步态分析通道和惯性测量单元IMU）进行了比较。结果显示，大多数任务完成时间和步态相关参数的测量结果高度一致（ICC > 0.9），SPPB总分机器人与治疗师之间具有中度至高度一致性（k = 0.67），机器人与IMU之间具有中度一致性（k = 0.55）。研究结果表明，社交机器人可在医疗环境中提供可靠且客观的虚弱评估，同时收集传统结果之外的相关移动性指标。
Summary / 总结
To address the resource-intensive and clinically limited nature of frailty assessments in older adults, this study introduces a social robot system that guides participants through standardized tests like the SPPB and TUG while capturing biomechanical data via vision-based skeleton tracking. Co-designed with healthcare professionals, the robot uses a Behaviour Tree architecture to coordinate perception, interaction, and measurement modules, enabling real-time evaluation in a rehabilitation center setting with 81 older adults. Experimental comparisons against therapist assessments and clinical tools (gait walkway, IMU) revealed high agreement for task completion times and gait metrics (ICC > 0.9), moderate agreement for SPPB scores between robot and therapist (k=0.67), and moderate agreement between robot and IMU (k=0.55), demonstrating the robot’s potential to deliver reliable, objective frailty assessments with enriched mobility data.
为解决老年人衰弱评估资源密集且临床指标粗略的问题，本研究提出一种社交机器人系统，采用行为树架构引导老年人完成标准化测试（如SPPB和TUG），并利用基于视觉的骨骼追踪采集生物力学数据。在康复中心研究实验室对81名老年人进行为期六个月的实地评估，机器人测量结果与治疗师评估及临床设备（如步态分析通道和惯性测量单元）进行对比。结果显示，任务完成时间与步态参数的测量一致性极高（ICC > 0.9），SPPB总分与治疗师评估高度一致（k = 0.67），与IMU数据中度一致（k = 0.55），表明该机器人可提供可靠、客观的衰弱评估，并补充传统方法难以获取的运动指标。
To Grok Grokking: Provable Grokking in Ridge Regression
Authors: Mingyue Xu, Gal Vardi, Itay Safran
Venue: ICML 2026
First: 2026-01-27T16:52:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:51:18+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 43rd International Conference on Machine Learning (ICML 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study grokking, the onset of generalization long after overfitting, in a classical ridge regression setting. We prove end-to-end grokking results for learning over-parameterized linear regression models using gradient descent with weight decay. Specifically, we prove that the following stages occur: (i) the model overfits the training data early during training; (ii) poor generalization persists long after overfitting has manifested; and (iii) the generalization error eventually becomes arbitrarily small. Moreover, we show, both theoretically and empirically, that grokking can be amplified or eliminated in a principled manner through proper hyperparameter tuning. To the best of our knowledge, these are the first rigorous quantitative bounds on the generalization delay (which we refer to as the "grokking time") in terms of training hyperparameters. Lastly, going beyond the linear setting, we empirically demonstrate that our quantitative bounds also capture the behavior of grokking on non-linear neural networks. Our results suggest that grokking is not an inherent failure mode of deep learning, but rather a consequence of specific training conditions, and thus does not require fundamental changes to the model architecture or learning algorithm to avoid.
中文标题/摘要
标题：理解Grokking：岭回归中的可证明Grokking
我们在经典的岭回归设定中研究Grokking现象——即在过拟合之后很久才出现泛化能力。我们证明了使用带权重衰减的梯度下降学习过参数化线性回归模型时，端到端的Grokking结果：(i) 模型在训练早期即过拟合训练数据；(ii) 过拟合发生后，泛化能力仍长期较差；(iii) 最终泛化误差可任意小。此外，我们从理论和实证上表明，通过适当调整超参数，可有原则地放大或消除Grokking。据我们所知，这是首次以训练超参数为变量，给出泛化延迟（我们称之为“Grokking时间”）的严格量化界限。最后，超越线性设定，我们实证表明，这些量化界限同样能捕捉非线性神经网络中的Grokking行为。我们的结果表明，Grokking并非深度学习的固有失败模式，而是特定训练条件的产物，因此无需对模型架构或学习算法进行根本性改变即可避免。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by understanding the phenomenon of grokking—where models generalize well despite initial overfitting—the paper analyzes this behavior in ridge regression using gradient descent with weight decay. The authors prove a three-stage process: early overfitting, prolonged poor generalization, and eventual sharp generalization improvement. They establish provable bounds on grokking time, showing it depends on hyperparameters and can be controlled via tuning. Empirically, they confirm these bounds hold for non-linear neural networks, suggesting grokking arises from training conditions rather than model architecture, and can be mitigated without architectural changes.
该研究旨在理解模型在过拟合后仍能泛化（即‘grokking’）的现象，通过在岭回归中使用带权重衰减的梯度下降进行分析。作者证明了三个阶段：早期过拟合、过拟合后长期泛化能力差、最终泛化误差急剧下降。他们建立了可证明的grokking时间界限，表明该时间与超参数相关，可通过调参加速或抑制grokking。实验证明这些界限在非线性神经网络中同样适用，表明grokking源于训练条件而非模型结构，无需改变架构即可避免。
Grokipedia vs Wikipedia: An LLM-Based Audit of Political Neutrality along Ideologies
Authors: Filippos Vlahos, Guillaume Bied, Tijl De Bie
First: 2026-07-16T15:49:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:49:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Online encyclopedias shape political opinion and, through it, democratic discourse. In late 2025, Grokipedia was released, an encyclopedia written entirely by the LLM Grok. One motivation behind the project was to provide an unbiased alternative to Wikipedia, which has faced accusations of "left-wing" and "liberal" bias. But does an encyclopedia written by an LLM deliver greater neutrality, or does it simply embed a different ideology? We conduct a large-scale political bias study on Grokipedia and Wikipedia, analysing 1,394 article pairs describing members of government for neutrality along nine expert-coded ideology dimensions employing four LLM judges, Grok, Claude, Mistral, and DeepSeek. As the LLMs could themselves be biased, we also investigate patterns in their judgments. We find all LLM-judges, including Grok, to rate Grokipedia less neutral than Wikipedia. Both encyclopedias are rated as portraying politicians favourably overall, but towards different ideological groups. Grokipedia particularly favours economically right-wing politicians and penalises socially liberal ones, while Wikipedia is rated as favourably biased towards the latter.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Grokipedia 与维基百科：基于大语言模型的政治中立性意识形态审计
在线百科全书塑造政治观点，进而影响民主讨论。2025年末，由大语言模型Grok完全编写的百科全书Grokipedia发布，其初衷是提供一个无偏见的维基百科替代品，因维基百科曾被指控存在“左翼”和“自由派”偏见。但由大语言模型编写的百科全书是否更中立，抑或只是嵌入了另一种意识形态？我们对Grokipedia和维基百科进行了大规模政治偏见研究，分析了1394对描述政府成员的文章，沿九个专家编码的意识形态维度评估其中立性，使用四名大语言模型裁判（Grok、Claude、Mistral和DeepSeek）。鉴于大语言模型本身可能存在偏见，我们亦研究了其判断模式。研究发现，包括Grok在内的所有大语言模型裁判均认为Grokipedia比维基百科更不中立。两部百科全书总体上均对政治人物持正面评价，但倾向不同意识形态群体：Grokipedia特别偏爱经济右翼政治人物，而惩罚社会自由派政治人物；维基百科则被评价为对后者持正面偏见。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by concerns over Wikipedia’s perceived ideological bias, researchers compared Grokipedia—an LLM-generated encyclopedia—to Wikipedia using 1,394 article pairs on politicians, evaluated across nine ideology dimensions by four LLMs: Grok, Claude, Mistral, and DeepSeek. All LLMs judged Grokipedia as less neutral than Wikipedia, with Grokipedia showing a clear bias toward economically right-wing politicians and against socially liberal ones, while Wikipedia was found to favor socially liberal figures. The study also revealed that the LLMs themselves exhibited consistent bias patterns in their judgments, suggesting that even AI-generated content may reflect or amplify ideological leanings.
为探究由LLM生成的Grokipedia是否比维基百科更中立，研究者对比了1394对政治人物条目，由四款LLM（Grok、Claude、Mistral、DeepSeek）在九个意识形态维度上进行评估。结果显示，所有LLM均认为Grokipedia比维基百科更不中立，Grokipedia明显偏向经济右翼政治人物并贬低社会自由派人物，而维基百科则偏向社会自由派。研究还发现，评估用的LLM本身也存在一致的偏见模式，表明AI评估者未必能客观衡量中立性。
Towards Consistent Video Geometry Estimation
Authors: Zhu Yu, Jingnan Gao, Runmin Zhang, Lingteng Qiu, Zhengyi Zhao, Rui Peng, Yichao Yan, Kejie Qiu, Siyu Zhu, Zilong Dong, Si-Yuan Cao, Hui-Liang Shen
First: 2026-05-28T15:11:17+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:48:01+00:00
Comments: Project webpage: https://pkqbajng.github.io/ViGeo/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
This work presents ViGeo, a feed-forward foundation model for recovering spatially dense and temporally consistent geometry from video sequences. Built upon a plain transformer architecture without task-specific architectural modifications, ViGeo supports streaming, full-sequence, and long-video inference within a unified model. The key design is dynamic chunking attention, which exposes the model to both bidirectional and causal temporal contexts during training and allows it to adapt its attention pattern at test time without retraining. To improve supervision quality, we further introduce a completion-based data refinement framework. This framework trains a video depth completion teacher that conditions on sparse and noisy annotations and exploits video/multi-view context to produce dense, temporally coherent, and geometrically reliable training targets. Beyond depth and point maps, ViGeo also predicts surface normals within the same framework. Trained solely on public datasets, ViGeo achieves state-of-the-art performance across online, offline, and long-video depth estimation, surface normal estimation, and video point map estimation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：迈向一致的视频几何估计
本文提出了ViGeo，一种前馈基础模型，用于从视频序列中恢复空间密集且时间一致的几何结构。该模型基于标准Transformer架构，无需任务特定的结构修改，支持流式、全序列及长视频推理，统一于同一模型。其核心设计是动态分块注意力机制，训练时使模型同时接触双向与因果时间上下文，并在测试时无需再训练即可自适应调整注意力模式。为提升监督质量，我们进一步引入基于补全的数据精炼框架：训练一个视频深度补全教师模型，其依据稀疏且噪声的标注，并利用视频/多视角上下文生成密集、时间一致且几何可靠的训练目标。除深度与点图外，ViGeo在同一框架下还预测表面法线。该模型仅在公开数据集上训练，已在在线、离线及长视频深度估计、表面法线估计和视频点图估计任务中达到最先进性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for temporally consistent and spatially dense geometry recovery from videos, ViGeo introduces a feed-forward transformer model that operates without task-specific architecture changes. It employs dynamic chunking attention to handle bidirectional and causal temporal contexts during training and adaptively adjust attention at inference, enabling streaming and long-video support. Paired with a completion-based data refinement framework that generates dense, coherent, and geometrically accurate targets from sparse annotations, ViGeo achieves state-of-the-art results across depth, surface normal, and point map estimation on public datasets, both offline and online.
为实现视频中时空一致且空间密集的几何重建，ViGeo提出一种基于Transformer的前馈基础模型，无需特定任务结构修改。其核心是动态分块注意力机制，训练时支持双向与因果时序上下文，推理时可自适应调整注意力模式，支持流式与长视频处理。配合基于补全的数据增强框架，该框架利用视频与多视角上下文，从稀疏噪声标注生成密集、连贯且几何可靠的训练目标，使ViGeo在公开数据集上实现深度、法线与点图估计的最先进性能，涵盖在线、离线及长视频场景。
Setup Complete, Now You Are Compromised: Weaponizing Setup Instructions Against AI Coding Agents
Authors: Aadesh Bagmar, Pushkar Saraf
First: 2026-07-16T15:47:19+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:47:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
AI coding agents set up projects by reading documentation and installing the dependencies it lists, without verifying their names, sources, or known vulnerabilities. By editing only a README, requirements file, or Makefile, an attacker can redirect the agent to an untrusted registry, a known-vulnerable version, or a wrong-but-plausible name: documentation becomes a vector for code execution. We present the first systematic evaluation of package-install-time supply-chain attacks delivered through ordinary project-setup documentation across production coding-agent harnesses, probing frontier models on twelve scenarios in five attack classes, grounded in documented incidents. The same model catches an attack through one harness and installs it through another: install-time security rests on the harness-model combination, not the model alone. Agents catch blatant typosquats reliably, but plausible separator-confusion names (azurecore for azure-core) slip through, and how often depends on the harness-model pairing. Source-based attacks like registry redirection are missed almost everywhere. The source blind spot recurs on npm and Cargo, where nearly every model installs the untrusted dependency; name detection carries over less consistently across ecosystems. Security-oriented prompts recover part of the gap but only for the dimension they name; a deterministic pre-install check that verifies names, sources, and versions before any code runs closes most of it.
中文标题/摘要
标题：设置完成，你已遭入侵：利用设置说明攻击AI编码代理
AI编码代理通过阅读文档并安装其列出的依赖项来设置项目，而不验证其名称、来源或已知漏洞。攻击者只需修改README、requirements文件或Makefile，即可引导代理访问不可信的注册表、已知漏洞版本或看似合理但错误的名称：文档成为代码执行的载体。我们首次系统性评估了通过普通项目设置文档交付的包安装时供应链攻击，针对五个攻击类别中的十二种场景，在生产级编码代理框架中对前沿模型进行测试，基于已记录的事件。同一模型在一种框架中可检测到攻击，但在另一种框架中却能安装该攻击：安装时的安全性取决于框架与模型的组合，而非模型本身。代理能可靠检测明显的域名混淆错误，但对看似合理但名称混淆（如azurecore代替azure-core）的攻击则常被绕过，且绕过率取决于框架-模型组合。针对源的攻击（如注册表重定向）几乎在所有情况下均未被发现。在npm和Cargo中，几乎每个模型都会安装不可信依赖项，名称检测在不同生态系统中的表现也不一致。安全导向的提示可部分弥补差距，但仅限于其明确提及的维度；而一种在任何代码运行前确定性验证名称、来源和版本的预安装检查，可关闭大部分漏洞。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the risk that AI coding agents blindly install dependencies listed in project documentation without verifying their safety, this study systematically evaluates how easily attackers can compromise agents via setup files like READMEs or requirements. The researchers tested twelve attack scenarios across five classes using frontier models in five coding-agent harnesses, revealing that no single model is universally secure — security depends on the specific harness-model combination. Agents reliably detect obvious typosquatting but often miss subtle name variations (e.g., 'azurecore' vs 'azure-core'), and almost universally fail to detect source-based attacks like registry redirection, especially in npm and Cargo ecosystems. Security prompts help partially but inconsistently, while a deterministic pre-install verification step significantly reduces vulnerabilities.
鉴于AI编码代理在读取文档时会无条件安装依赖项而忽略来源与版本验证，本研究系统评估了通过修改README、requirements文件或Makefile等普通项目文档对代理发起供应链攻击的可行性。研究人员在五个编码代理框架中测试了十二种攻击场景，发现安全依赖于代理框架与模型的组合，而非模型本身。代理能可靠识别明显域名仿冒，但常漏掉如'azurecore'这类看似合理但实际错误的名称，且几乎完全无法识别源地址重定向等攻击，尤其在npm和Cargo生态中普遍存在。安全提示仅能部分弥补漏洞，而强制在代码运行前验证名称、来源与版本的预安装检查可大幅降低风险。
Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization for World Models in Atari Pong
Authors: Yukuan Lu, Zaishuo Xia, Weyl Lu, Yubei Chen
First: 2026-07-16T15:46:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:46:44+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
World models are usually evaluated as components of model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) systems, while the world models themselves are rarely studied in isolation. We examine five representative visual world-model agents in Atari Pong: DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM. After reproducing their training pipelines and matching the reported agent performance, we freeze the learned world models and evaluate them with a closed-loop rollout diagnostic: a policy trained separately from the corresponding MBRL agent interacts with each frozen model, and the generated video trajectories are inspected for visual and dynamical errors. Across all five models, the rollouts contain clear failures, including ball disappearance, incorrect ball motion, and invalid ball-paddle interactions. Beyond visual trajectories, we further evaluate them with pixel-space zero-shot MBRL, where a new policy is trained entirely inside a frozen world model and then evaluated in the real environment. Across all five models, the resulting policies substantially underperform those produced by the corresponding original MBRL training pipelines. The gap is particularly large for DreamerV3, whose mean return drops from -5.5 to -20.9, near the minimum Pong return of -21. We hypothesize that insufficient modeling of task-critical concepts, such as the ball in Pong, may contribute to these failures. We therefore propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary pixel reconstruction loss applied to segmented concept regions. Experiments show that CGSReg improves both closed-loop rollouts and pixel-space zero-shot MBRL in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER. Its effects vary across the remaining models and evaluation metrics, indicating that CGSReg alone does not address all world-model bottlenecks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于概念引导的空间正则化用于Atari Pong中的世界模型
世界模型通常作为基于模型的强化学习（MBRL）系统的一部分进行评估，而世界模型本身很少被单独研究。我们考察了Atari Pong中五个代表性的视觉世界模型代理：DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus和STORM。在复现其训练流程并匹配报告的代理性能后，我们冻结所学习的世界模型，并使用闭环 rollout 诊断进行评估：一个与对应MBRL代理独立训练的策略与每个冻结模型交互，生成的视频轨迹被检查视觉和动力学错误。在所有五个模型中，rollout均出现明显失败，包括球体消失、球体运动错误及无效的球拍交互。除视觉轨迹外，我们进一步使用像素空间零样本MBRL进行评估，即在冻结的世界模型内完全训练新策略，再在真实环境中评估。在所有五个模型中，所得策略均显著劣于对应原始MBRL训练流程产生的策略。DreamerV3的差距尤为显著，其平均回报从-5.5降至-20.9，接近Pong的最小回报-21。我们推测，对任务关键概念（如Pong中的球体）建模不足可能是导致这些失败的原因。因此，我们提出概念引导的空间正则化（CGSReg），即对分割的概念区域施加辅助像素重建损失。实验表明，CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND和TWISTER中同时改善了闭环rollout和像素空间零样本MBRL。其效果在其余模型和评估指标中存在差异，表明CGSReg无法解决所有世界模型瓶颈。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that world models in Atari Pong often fail to capture task-critical visual dynamics, the authors evaluate five representative agents—DreamerV3, DIAMOND, TWISTER, Simulus, and STORM—by freezing their models and testing them with separate policies in closed-loop rollouts and zero-shot MBRL. Results reveal consistent visual and dynamical errors, including ball disappearance and invalid interactions, and show that policies trained inside frozen models underperform original MBRL agents, with DreamerV3’s return dropping from -5.5 to -20.9. To address this, they propose Concept-Guided Spatial Regularization (CGSReg), an auxiliary loss that enforces pixel reconstruction in concept-segmented regions. CGSReg improves performance in DreamerV3, DIAMOND, and TWISTER for both evaluation tasks, but its effectiveness varies across models and metrics, suggesting it does not resolve all underlying modeling deficiencies.
鉴于在Atari Pong中，尽管世界模型在模型强化学习中表现良好，但其独立运行时仍存在明显视觉与动态错误，研究者对五种代表性模型（DreamerV3、DIAMOND、TWISTER、Simulus、STORM）进行了冻结模型评估，发现其在闭合回路仿真中常出现球体消失或交互错误，零样本MBRL实验也显示性能大幅下降，尤其DreamerV3回报从-5.5降至-20.9。为此，作者提出概念引导空间正则化（CGSReg），通过在概念分割区域施加像素重建损失以提升模型对关键概念（如球体）的建模能力。实验表明CGSReg在DreamerV3、DIAMOND和TWISTER中有效改善了仿真与零样本MBRL表现，但不同模型和指标上效果不一，说明其仅能缓解部分世界模型瓶颈。
Ray-based phase error correction for miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP under single-directional hyperbolic projection
Authors: Seung-Jae Son, Yatong An, Jae-Sang Hyun
First: 2026-07-16T15:43:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:43:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Fringe Projection Profilometry (FPP) systems using miniaturized DOE pro-jectors often suffer from severe phase artifacts due to nonlinear projection characteristics and limited pattern controllability. We propose a ray-based phase error correction framework that models phase artifacts along projection rays from the projector pinhole, incorporating projector geometry without re-lying on image-domain processing or neighboring pixels. A projector pinhole estimation method based on a single-directional hyperbolic fringe pattern is introduced, through which projector geometry can be recovered without stereo calibration. In addition, a data-efficient strategy constructs the re-finement model from a single calibration pose. Experiments on miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP systems demonstrate significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy under nonlinear projection conditions, confirming the robustness and physical consistency of the proposed approach.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于光线的相位误差校正方法用于微型DOE投影仪基FPP系统中的单向双曲投影
基于微型DOE投影仪的相位投影轮廓术（FPP）系统常因非线性投影特性及图案控制受限而产生严重相位伪影。本文提出一种基于光线的相位误差校正框架，从投影仪针孔沿投影光线建模相位伪影，无需依赖图像域处理或邻近像素。引入一种基于单向双曲条纹图案的投影针孔估计方法，无需立体标定即可恢复投影仪几何结构。此外，一种数据高效策略从单一标定姿态构建细化模型。实验表明，在非线性投影条件下，该方法显著提升重建精度，验证了其鲁棒性与物理一致性。
Summary / 总结
To address phase artifacts in miniaturized DOE projector-based FPP systems caused by nonlinear projection and limited pattern control, this work introduces a ray-based phase error correction method that models phase errors along projection rays originating from the projector pinhole, avoiding image-domain processing or pixel neighborhood dependencies. It employs a single-directional hyperbolic fringe pattern to estimate the projector pinhole and recover geometry without stereo calibration, and builds a data-efficient refinement model from a single calibration pose. Experimental results on miniaturized systems show substantial improvements in 3D reconstruction accuracy under nonlinear projection, validating the method's robustness and physical consistency.
为解决微型DOE投影仪FPP系统因非线性投影和图案控制受限导致的相位伪影问题，本文提出一种基于光线的相位误差校正方法，沿投影针孔发出的光线建模相位误差，无需图像域处理或邻近像素依赖。通过单向双曲条纹图案实现针孔估计，无需立体标定即可恢复投影仪几何结构，同时采用数据高效策略，仅用单姿态校准数据构建校正模型。实验表明，在非线性投影条件下，该方法显著提升三维重建精度，验证其鲁棒性和物理一致性。
LOTAPO: Leave-One-Turn Attribution for Self-Generated Process Rewards in Multi-Turn Search Reasoning
Authors: Qiang Zhu, Jiajun Wu, Longyi Wang
First: 2026-07-15T06:55:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:39:38+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 5 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning for multi-turn search reasoning typically relies on terminal outcome rewards, which cannot distinguish useful, redundant, and harmful intermediate interactions. We propose LOTAPO , a self-generated process-supervision method based on backward leave-one-turn attribution. For each search turn, LOTAPO replaces the turn and its retrieval observation with a fixed [DELETE] placeholder and measures the resulting change in the current policy's mean log-likelihood of the gold answer. This Answer-Likelihood Gain estimates the turn's contribution while preserving all downstream interactions, allowing early evidence to be evaluated in the complete reasoning context. LOTAPO further applies sign-consistency gating, retaining only normalized process advantages whose directions agree with their raw attribution scores. The method requires no additional reward model, teacher, verifier, or LLM-as-a-Judge. Across seven knowledge-intensive question-answering datasets with local retrieval, LOTAPO achieves an average exact-match score of 0.326, outperforming the strongest step-reward baseline, IGPO, by 0.053. Ablations show complementary benefits from backward attribution and sign-consistency gating, demonstrating that policy-derived retrospective attribution can provide effective process supervision for multi-turn search agents.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LOTAPO：多轮搜索推理中自生成过程奖励的留一回合归因方法
多轮搜索推理的强化学习通常依赖终端结果奖励，无法区分有用、冗余和有害的中间交互。我们提出LOTAPO，一种基于后向留一回合归因的自生成过程监督方法。对每轮搜索，LOTAPO将该回合及其检索观测替换为固定[DELETE]占位符，并测量当前策略对黄金答案的平均对数似然变化。该答案似然增益估计回合贡献，同时保留所有下游交互，使早期证据能在完整推理上下文中评估。LOTAPO进一步应用符号一致性门控，仅保留方向与原始归因分数一致的归一化过程优势。该方法无需额外奖励模型、教师、验证器或LLM-as-a-Judge。在七个本地检索的知识密集型问答数据集上，LOTAPO平均精确匹配得分达0.326，优于最强步奖励基线IGPO 0.053。消融实验表明，后向归因与符号一致性门控具有互补优势，证明策略衍生的回顾性归因可为多轮搜索代理提供有效过程监督。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of terminal rewards in multi-turn search reasoning, which fail to evaluate intermediate steps, LOTAPO introduces a self-generated process supervision method using backward leave-one-turn attribution. For each turn, it replaces the turn and its retrieval observation with a [DELETE] placeholder and measures the resulting change in the policy's mean log-likelihood of the gold answer, termed Answer-Likelihood Gain. This gain estimates the turn's contribution while preserving downstream interactions, enabling early evidence to be assessed in full context. LOTAPO further applies sign-consistency gating to retain only normalized advantages aligned with raw attribution scores. Experiments across seven knowledge-intensive QA datasets show LOTAPO achieves an average exact-match score of 0.326, surpassing the strongest step-reward baseline IGPO by 0.053, with ablation studies confirming the complementary value of backward attribution and gating.
为解决多轮搜索推理中终端奖励无法评估中间步骤的问题，LOTAPO提出一种基于后向留一回合归因的自生成过程监督方法。对每一轮，它将该轮及其检索观察替换为[DELETE]占位符，并测量由此导致的策略对正确答案对数似然均值的变化，即答案似然增益，以此评估该轮贡献，同时保留下游交互，使早期步骤能在完整上下文中评估。LOTAPO进一步采用符号一致性门控，仅保留与原始归因分数方向一致的归一化过程优势，无需额外奖励模型或裁判。在七个知识密集型问答数据集上，LOTAPO平均精确匹配得分达0.326，优于最强步奖励基线IGPO 0.053，消融实验表明后向归因与门控具有互补增益，证明策略衍生的回顾性归因可有效为多轮搜索代理提供过程监督。
Catch, Throw, Repeat: Planning for Human-Robot Partner Juggling
Authors: Jonathan Rainer Lippert, Kai Ploeger, Abir Chowdhury, Hermann Müller, Jan Peters, Alap Kshirsagar
Venue: IROS 2026
First: 2026-07-16T15:39:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:39:30+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dynamic object exchange between humans and robots remains a challenging problem due to uncertainty in perception, timing, and contact-rich interaction. Human-robot juggling represents a particularly demanding instance of this problem, requiring precise real-time coordination, predictive motion planning with feedback control, and robustness to variability in human motion. Enabling such skills is of interest for advancing physical human-robot interaction and shared autonomy. We present a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling that enables a robot to reliably catch and throw balls in synchronized multi-ball patterns with a human partner. The system integrates predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization using a multiple-shooting formulation, and a state-machine-based coordination logic to enable synchronized multi-ball human-robot partner juggling. In a user study with 8 participants of varying juggling skill from beginner to expert, we demonstrate that our system can achieve three-ball cascades shared between the robot and the human. All participants exceeded previously reported best-case results within a 10-minute test session, with one participant extending the previous record for shared three-ball cascade juggling fivefold to 20 consecutive robot catches, and another participant achieving a 100% success rate with 40 consecutive catches in a single-ball catch-and-return setting. Video documentation can be found at https://kai-ploeger.com/partner-juggling
中文标题/摘要
标题：接、抛、重复：面向人机协作杂耍的规划方法
由于感知、时序和接触密集交互的不确定性，人机间动态物体交换仍是一个具有挑战性的问题。人机杂耍是该问题的一个特别严苛的实例，要求精确的实时协调、带反馈控制的预测运动规划以及对人类动作变异的鲁棒性。实现这些能力有助于推进物理人机交互与共享自主性。我们提出了一种用于人机协作杂耍的实时规划与控制架构，使机器人能够与人类伙伴同步进行多球模式的接抛动作。该系统集成了预测式球体跟踪、基于多段法的自适应在线轨迹优化，以及基于状态机的协调逻辑，从而实现同步多球人机协作杂耍。在包含8名从初学者到专家不同杂耍水平参与者的用户研究中，我们证明系统可实现机器人与人类共享的三球连续杂耍。所有参与者在10分钟测试中均超越此前最佳记录，其中一名参与者将此前共享三球杂耍的记录延长五倍至20次连续机器人接球，另一名参与者在单球接抛场景中实现40次连续成功接球的100%成功率。视频资料见 https://kai-ploeger.com/partner-juggling
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of real-time, contact-rich human-robot coordination in dynamic object exchange, this work presents a real-time planning and control architecture for human-robot partner juggling. The system combines predictive ball tracking, adaptive online trajectory optimization via multiple-shooting, and state-machine-based coordination to enable synchronized multi-ball juggling. In a user study with 8 participants ranging from beginner to expert, the system successfully enabled three-ball cascades shared between human and robot, with one participant achieving 20 consecutive robot catches—five times the prior record—and another reaching 100% success in 40 consecutive single-ball catches.
为解决人机之间动态物体传递时感知不确定性与时间同步难题，本研究提出一套实时规划与控制系统，用于实现人机协作抛接球。该方法结合预测球体轨迹、基于多段法的自适应轨迹优化及状态机协调机制，支持多人协作的多球同步抛接。在包含八名从初学者到专家的用户测试中，系统成功实现人机共享三球抛接，所有参与者均超越此前记录，其中一人完成20次连续机器人接球（为前纪录五倍），另一人单球抛接返回任务中实现100%成功率，共完成40次连续成功接球。
Can AR Embedded Visualizations Foster Appropriate Reliance on AI in Spatial Decision-Making? A Comparative Study of AR X-Ray vs. 2D Minimap
Authors: Xianhao Carton Liu, Difan Jia, Tongyu Nie, Evan Suma Rosenberg, Victoria Interrante, Chen Zhu-Tian
First: 2025-07-18T18:40:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:39:25+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 10 figures, Accepted for publication in CHI'26, Barcelona, Spain, April 13 - 17, 2026; CHI '26: ACM CHI Conference on Human Factors in Computing Systems
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Artificial Intelligence (AI) and indoor sensing increasingly support decision-making in spatial environments. However, traditional visualization methods impose a substantial mental workload when viewers translate this digital information into real-world spaces, leading to inappropriate reliance on AI. Embedded visualizations in Augmented Reality (AR), by integrating information into physical environments, may reduce this workload and foster more appropriate reliance on AI. To assess this, we conducted an empirical study (N = 32) comparing an AR embedded visualization (X-ray) and 2D Minimap in AI-assisted, time-critical spatial target selection tasks. Surprisingly, evidence shows that the embedded visualization led to greater inappropriate reliance on AI, primarily as over-reliance, due to factors like perceptual challenges, visual proximity illusions, and highly realistic visual representations. Nonetheless, the embedded visualization demonstrated benefits in spatial mapping. We conclude by discussing empirical insights, design implications, and directions for future research on human-AI collaborative decision in AR.
中文标题/摘要
标题：增强现实嵌入式可视化能否促进空间决策中对人工智能的适当依赖？——AR X光图与2D小地图的比较研究
人工智能（AI）和室内感知正越来越多地支持空间环境中的决策。然而，传统可视化方法在观众将数字信息转化为现实空间时，会带来较大的认知负荷，导致对AI的不当依赖。增强现实（AR）中的嵌入式可视化通过将信息整合到物理环境中，可能降低这种认知负荷，促进对AI的更适当依赖。为评估这一点，我们进行了一项实证研究（N=32），比较了AR嵌入式可视化（X光图）与2D小地图在AI辅助、时间敏感的空间目标选择任务中的表现。令人惊讶的是，证据表明嵌入式可视化导致了更大的对AI的不当依赖，主要表现为过度依赖，原因包括感知挑战、视觉邻近错觉和高度逼真的视觉表现。尽管如此，嵌入式可视化在空间映射方面仍具优势。本文最后讨论了实证见解、设计启示及未来关于AR中人机协作决策的研究方向。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether AR-embedded visualizations can promote appropriate reliance on AI during spatial decision-making, motivated by the high cognitive load of traditional 2D visualizations. The researchers compared an AR X-ray visualization with a 2D minimap in time-sensitive spatial target selection tasks involving 32 participants. Contrary to expectations, the AR visualization led to greater inappropriate reliance on AI—primarily over-reliance—due to perceptual distortions, visual proximity illusions, and realistic rendering. However, it also showed advantages in spatial mapping accuracy. The findings suggest that while AR can enhance spatial understanding, it may inadvertently encourage over-trust in AI if not carefully designed.
本研究探讨增强现实（AR）嵌入式可视化是否有助于在空间决策中促进对人工智能的适当依赖，动机源于传统2D可视化方法在将数字信息映射到现实空间时带来的认知负担。研究通过对比AR X射线可视化与2D小地图，在AI辅助的限时目标选择任务中测试了32名参与者。出乎意料的是，AR可视化反而导致了更严重的不当依赖——特别是过度依赖——这归因于感知干扰、视觉邻近错觉和高度逼真的视觉呈现。然而，它在空间映射准确性方面表现更优。研究结论表明，尽管AR能提升空间理解，但若设计不当，可能反而诱使用户过度信任AI。
DAPGNet: Dynamic Adaptive Physics-Guided Graph Diffusion Network for Hyperspectral Image Classification
Authors: Pengkun Wang, Weijia Cao, Ning Wang, Xiaofei Yang
First: 2026-07-16T15:37:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:37:32+00:00
Comments: 9 figures and 9 tables. Pengkun Wang and Weijia Cao contributed equally to this work
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hyperspectral image (HSI) classification requires reliable pixel-relation modeling under spectral variability, mixed pixels, and heterogeneous boundaries. Existing graph-based HSI classifiers usually construct graph topology from spatial proximity, superpixel connectivity, or learned feature affinity. However, the spectral physical prior carried by contiguous bands has limited influence on topology estimation and message propagation. This paper presents DAPGNet, a dynamic adaptive physics-guided graph diffusion network that injects a structure-constrained physical prior into relation-level graph learning. DAPGNet first encodes contiguous spectral responses into node-wise multiscale physical-prior representations. A two-stage graph constructor then combines spectral-spatial affinity, physical-prior consistency, and spatial distance to form a physical-prior-aware sparse topology. During graph diffusion, learned edge weights are transformed into additive attention biases, while a physical gate performs node-wise and feature-wise interpolation between graph-aggregated features and projected physical-prior features. Cross-scale fusion integrates node states from different diffusion depths, and the network is optimized with main classification, auxiliary supervision, and second-order spectral smoothness regularization. Experiments on Indian Pines, WHU-Hi-LongKou, Houston2013, and Houston2018 show that DAPGNet achieves the best OA, AA, and Kappa among representative CNN-, Transformer-, Mamba-, and graph-based baselines. It improves AA over the strongest competing method by 3.64 to 7.31 percentage points across the four datasets. Ablation and sensitivity analyses further support the complementary effects of physical-prior extraction, prior-aware topology construction, physics-gated propagation, and spectral smoothness regularization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DAPGNet：面向高光谱图像分类的动态自适应物理引导图扩散网络
高光谱图像（HSI）分类需在光谱变异性、混合像元和异质边界条件下建立可靠的像素关系建模。现有基于图的HSI分类器通常从空间邻近性、超像素连通性或学习到的特征亲和性构建图拓扑。然而，连续波段所携带的光谱物理先验对拓扑估计和消息传播影响有限。本文提出DAPGNet，一种动态自适应物理引导图扩散网络，将结构约束的物理先验注入关系级图学习。DAPGNet首先将连续光谱响应编码为节点级多尺度物理先验表示；随后，两阶段图构建器结合光谱-空间亲和性、物理先验一致性与空间距离，构建物理先验感知的稀疏拓扑。图扩散过程中，学习到的边权重转化为加性注意力偏置，物理门控机制在节点级与特征级对图聚合特征与投影物理先验特征进行插值。跨尺度融合整合不同扩散深度的节点状态，网络通过主分类、辅助监督及二阶光谱平滑正则化进行优化。在Indian Pines、WHU-Hi-LongKou、Houston2013和Houston2018数据集上的实验表明，DAPGNet在代表性CNN、Transformer、Mamba及图结构基线中取得最佳OA、AA和Kappa值，且在四个数据集上较最强竞争方法提升AA 3.64至7.31个百分点。消融与敏感性分析进一步验证了物理先验提取、先验感知拓扑构建、物理门控传播及光谱平滑正则化的互补效应。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of modeling pixel relations in hyperspectral images under spectral variability and mixed pixels, DAPGNet introduces a dynamic adaptive physics-guided graph diffusion network that integrates spectral physical priors into graph topology construction and message propagation. The method first encodes contiguous spectral bands into multiscale physical-prior representations, then constructs a sparse graph topology by combining spectral-spatial affinity, physical-prior consistency, and spatial distance. During diffusion, edge weights are converted to attention biases, and a physical gate interpolates between graph-aggregated and projected physical features, while cross-scale fusion integrates states from different diffusion depths. Optimized with classification loss, auxiliary supervision, and spectral smoothness regularization, DAPGNet outperforms CNN-, Transformer-, Mamba-, and graph-based baselines on four datasets, achieving the highest OA, AA, and Kappa, with AA improvements of 3.64–7.31 percentage points over the strongest competitor.
为解决高光谱图像在光谱变异性与混合像元下像素关系建模的难题，DAPGNet提出一种动态自适应物理引导图扩散网络，将光谱物理先验注入图结构构建与消息传播。该方法首先将连续光谱响应编码为多尺度物理先验表示，再结合光谱-空间亲和力、物理先验一致性与空间距离构建稀疏图结构。扩散过程中，边权重转化为注意力偏置，物理门控机制在节点与特征维度上插值图聚合特征与投影物理特征，跨尺度融合整合不同扩散深度的节点状态。在四个数据集上，DAPGNet在总体精度、平均精度与Kappa值上超越CNN、Transformer、Mamba及图结构基线模型，较最强竞争方法平均精度提升3.64–7.31个百分点。
CF-Net: Conflict Fusion with Speaker Normalisation and Certainty Weighting for Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition
Authors: Tung Hung Bui, Hong Hai Nguyen, Van Thong Huynh
First: 2026-07-15T16:02:21+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:34:24+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 3 figures, 3 tables, 26 references
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Detecting ambivalence and hesitancy (AH) in unconstrained video is challenging because the target signal is inherently ambiguous and expressed through subtle cross-modal incongruence rather than prototypical affect. We present CF-Net, a deep multimodal network submitted to the 3rd Edition of the AH Video Recognition Challenge (ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026), targeting the BAH dataset. CF-Net encodes visual, audio, and transcript streams with frozen SigLIP2, HuBERT, and DistilBERT backbones, normalises backbone features per speaker to reduce identity leakage, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that explicitly computes pairwise cross-modal incongruence. Training combines certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout; an auxiliary certainty-regression head uses ambiguity annotations to stabilise learning on genuinely borderline samples. CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the BAH validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7439) on the private challenge test set.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CF-Net：用于矛盾融合、说话人归一化与置信度加权的模态不一致识别
在无约束视频中检测矛盾与犹豫（AH）具有挑战性，因为目标信号本质上模糊，且通过跨模态细微不一致而非典型情感表达。我们提出CF-Net，一种深度多模态网络，提交至第3版AH视频识别挑战赛（ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026），针对BAH数据集。CF-Net使用冻结的SigLIP2、HuBERT和DistilBERT骨干网络编码视觉、音频和转录流，按说话人归一化骨干特征以减少身份泄露，并通过ConflictFusion模块显式计算成对跨模态不一致。训练结合置信度加权焦点损失、流形混合与模态dropout；辅助置信度回归头利用模糊标注稳定学习，尤其针对真正边界样本。CF-Net在BAH验证集上达到0.7155的Macro F1，在私有挑战测试集上达到0.7364（AP=0.7439）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of detecting ambivalence and hesitancy in unconstrained video due to their subtle, cross-modal nature, CF-Net introduces a deep multimodal architecture that encodes visual, audio, and transcript inputs using frozen backbones, normalizes features per speaker to mitigate identity bias, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that quantifies cross-modal incongruence. Training employs certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout, with an auxiliary certainty-regression head to stabilize learning on ambiguous samples. Experiments on the BAH dataset show CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7439) on the private test set, demonstrating strong performance in recognizing nuanced affective states.
为了解决在无约束视频中检测矛盾与犹豫情绪因表达微妙、跨模态不一致而困难的问题，CF-Net采用深度多模态架构，使用冻结的视觉、音频和文本编码器。它通过按说话人归一化特征以减少身份泄露，并通过冲突融合模块显式计算跨模态不一致。训练结合了置信度加权焦点损失、流形混合与模态dropout，辅以置信度回归头稳定学习边界样本。模型在BAH验证集上达到0.7155的Macro F1，在私有测试集上达到0.7364（AP=0.7439）。
NIFA: Nonlinear IMC enhanced FPGA for efficient ML inference
Authors: Jiajun Hu, Ruthwik Reddy Sunketa, Lei Zhao, Archit Gajjar, Luca Buonanno, Aman Arora
First: 2026-07-16T15:32:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:32:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent FPGAs have improved deep learning (DL) inference efficiency through dedicated tensor blocks and in-BRAM computation. ReRAM-based analog in-memory computing (IMC) pushes efficiency further, offering an order-of-magnitude improvement in compute density and energy efficiency over conventional digital logic by performing vector-matrix multiplication (VMM) directly within the ReRAM crossbar; prior work has integrated such IMC blocks into FPGAs for DL inference. However, conventional IMC designs support only static-weight VMM, leaving nonlinear operations and dynamic matrix-matrix multiplication (DIMM) to the FPGA fabric. As a result, the benefits of IMC are largely confined to static-weight models, whereas Transformer-based models, which rely on frequent nonlinear and DIMM operations, gain only limited improvement. Moreover, the ADCs within each IMC block consume more than 70% of its area and power, further limiting system efficiency and scalability. To address these limitations, we propose a novel FPGA architecture that integrates an ADC-free IMC block, replacing the conventional ADC with analog content-addressable memories (ACAMs) that natively perform nonlinear operations inside the block. To fully exploit this block, we conduct an FPGA-aware design-space exploration that determines optimal crossbar dimensions while balancing FPGA area, flexibility, and DL performance, and we develop an efficient mapping that leverages ACAMs to carry out DIMM operations, extending the applicability of IMC to attention computation. On CNN and Transformer-based benchmarks, the proposed architecture achieves up to 40x and 1.9x higher energy efficiency and 4.1x and 2.5x higher area efficiency, respectively. Overall, it significantly improves FPGA DL inference efficiency and sustains robust gains on Transformer-based workloads across long input sequences, advancing domain-specialized FPGA design.
中文标题/摘要
标题：NIFA：增强型非线性IMC的FPGA用于高效机器学习推理
近期FPGA通过专用张量块和片上BRAM计算提升了深度学习（DL）推理效率。基于ReRAM的模拟存内计算（IMC）进一步推动效率提升，通过在ReRAM交叉阵列中直接执行向量-矩阵乘法（VMM），相比传统数字逻辑在计算密度和能效方面提升一个数量级；此前工作已将此类IMC模块集成至FPGA用于DL推理。然而，传统IMC设计仅支持静态权重VMM，非线性运算和动态矩阵-矩阵乘法（DIMM）仍需依赖FPGA片上逻辑，导致IMC优势主要局限于静态权重模型，而依赖频繁非线性与DIMM操作的Transformer模型仅获得有限提升。此外，每个IMC模块内的ADC消耗超过70%的面积和功耗，进一步限制系统效率与可扩展性。为解决上述问题，我们提出一种新型FPGA架构，集成无ADC的IMC模块，以模拟内容可寻址存储器（ACAM）替代传统ADC，使非线性运算原生在模块内执行。为充分挖掘该模块潜力，我们开展FPGA感知的设计空间探索，确定最优交叉阵列尺寸，平衡FPGA面积、灵活性与DL性能，并开发高效映射方法，利用ACAM执行DIMM操作，扩展IMC在注意力计算中的适用性。在CNN与Transformer基准测试中，该架构分别实现高达40倍和1.9倍能效提升、4.1倍和2.5倍面积效率提升。总体而言，该架构显著提升FPGA DL推理效率，并在长输入序列Transformer工作负载中持续保持稳健性能增益，推动领域专用FPGA设计发展。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of conventional IMC blocks in FPGAs that lack support for nonlinear operations and dynamic matrix-matrix multiplication, this work proposes NIFA: an ADC-free IMC architecture using analog content-addressable memories (ACAMs) to perform nonlinear computations and DIMM directly within the block. The design integrates with FPGA fabric through a targeted mapping strategy and crossbar dimension optimization to balance area, flexibility, and performance. Experimental results on CNN and Transformer benchmarks show up to 40x energy efficiency and 4.1x area efficiency gains for CNNs, and 1.9x energy efficiency and 2.5x area efficiency for Transformers, demonstrating strong scalability and sustained performance on long sequences.
为解决传统模拟存内计算（IMC）模块在FPGA中无法处理非线性运算和动态矩阵矩阵乘法（DIMM）且ADC占比较大导致效率受限的问题，本文提出NIFA架构，通过用模拟内容寻址存储器（ACAM）替代ADC，实现IMC模块内原生非线性运算。结合FPGA感知的设计空间探索与ACAM驱动的DIMM映射策略，该架构扩展了IMC在Transformer模型中的适用性。在CNN和Transformer基准测试中，该架构在CNN上实现最高40倍能效和4.1倍面积效率提升，在Transformer上实现1.9倍能效和2.5倍面积效率提升，且在长序列输入下仍保持性能优势。
Selecting Hyperparameters for Tree-Boosting
Authors: Floris Jan Koster, Fabio Sigrist
First: 2026-02-05T15:44:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:30:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Tree-boosting is a widely used machine learning technique for tabular data. However, its out-of-sample accuracy is critically dependent on multiple hyperparameters. In this article, we empirically compare several popular methods for hyperparameter optimization for tree-boosting including random grid search, the tree-structured Parzen estimator (TPE), Gaussian-process-based Bayesian optimization (GP-BO), Hyperband, the sequential model-based algorithm configuration (SMAC) method, and deterministic full grid search using $59$ regression and binary classification data sets. We find that the SMAC method clearly outperforms all the other considered methods on average, and it gives stable performance across a diverse collection of tabular data sets under a fixed tuning budget, which is relevant for users who cannot afford extensive manual trial-and-error tuning. We further observe that (i) a relatively large number of trials larger than $100$ is typically required for accurate tuning, (ii) using default values for hyperparameters or a full search over a small grid often yields very inaccurate models, (iii) all considered hyperparameters can have a material effect on the accuracy of tree-boosting, i.e., there is no small set of hyperparameters that is more important than others, and (iv) choosing the number of boosting iterations using early stopping yields more accurate results compared to including it in the search space for regression tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：树提升模型超参数选择
树提升是一种广泛用于表格数据的机器学习技术，但其样本外准确性高度依赖多个超参数。本文通过实证比较了多种流行的树提升超参数优化方法，包括随机网格搜索、树结构帕森估计器（TPE）、基于高斯过程的贝叶斯优化（GP-BO）、Hyperband、基于序列模型的算法配置（SMAC）方法以及在59个回归和二分类数据集上使用确定性全网格搜索。我们发现，SMAC方法在平均性能上明显优于所有其他方法，并且在固定调优预算下，其在多样化的表格数据集上表现稳定，这对无法承受大量手动试错调优的用户尤为重要。此外，我们还观察到：(i) 通常需要超过100次试验才能实现准确调优；(ii) 使用默认超参数值或在小网格上进行全搜索往往导致模型精度极低；(iii) 所有考虑的超参数均可能显著影响树提升的准确性，即不存在比其他超参数更重要的小集合；(iv) 对于回归任务，使用早停法选择提升迭代次数比将其纳入搜索空间能获得更准确的结果。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of selecting hyperparameters for tree-boosting models, which are sensitive to multiple tuning parameters and often require extensive manual effort. The authors empirically evaluate seven optimization methods—including random grid search, TPE, GP-BO, Hyperband, SMAC, and full grid search—across 59 regression and classification datasets. Their results show that SMAC consistently outperforms all other methods under a fixed computational budget, delivering stable accuracy across diverse datasets. Key findings include the need for over 100 trials for reliable tuning, the poor performance of default settings or small grids, the equal importance of all hyperparameters, and the advantage of using early stopping to determine boosting iterations in regression tasks.
本文针对树提升模型在表格数据中的广泛应用，但其性能高度依赖多个超参数的问题，实证比较了七种超参数优化方法（包括随机网格搜索、TPE、GP-BO、Hyperband、SMAC、全网格搜索）在59个回归与分类数据集上的表现。结果表明，SMAC方法在平均性能上显著优于其他方法，且在不同数据集上保持稳定，适合计算资源有限的用户。主要发现包括：准确调优通常需超过100次试验，使用默认值或小范围网格搜索易导致模型精度低下，所有超参数均对性能有实质性影响，且在回归任务中，通过早停法选择迭代次数比将其纳入搜索空间更优。
Synthesizing real-world distributions from high-dimensional Gaussian Noise with Fully Connected Neural Network
Authors: Joanna Komorniczak
First: 2026-04-10T08:20:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:28:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The use of synthetic data in machine learning applications and research offers many benefits, including performance improvements through data augmentation and privacy preservation of original samples. This work proposes an efficient synthetic data generation method based on a fully connected neural network that transforms a high-dimensional random Gaussian distribution to approximate a target real-world dataset. The proposed solution combines data preprocessing designed for tabular data with distribution modeling and PCA dimensionality reduction to further enhance data privacy. The work also defines two dedicated randomized loss functions based on Wasserstein distance combined with feature Covariance and a randomized pairwise error reduction loss function. The experiments conducted on 25 diverse tabular real-world datasets confirm that the proposed solution obtains similarity and privacy scores relative to the state-of-the-art generative methods and achieves reference MMD scores orders of magnitude faster than modern deep learning solutions. The experiments involved analyzing distributional similarity, privacy protection, and the utility of synthetic data in classification tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：利用全连接神经网络从高维高斯噪声合成现实世界分布
合成数据在机器学习应用和研究中具有诸多优势，包括通过数据增强提升性能和保护原始样本隐私。本文提出一种基于全连接神经网络的高效合成数据生成方法，该方法将高维随机高斯分布转换为近似目标现实世界数据集。该方案结合为表格数据设计的数据预处理、分布建模及PCA降维技术，进一步增强数据隐私。此外，本文定义了两种基于Wasserstein距离与特征协方差的专用随机损失函数，以及一种随机成对误差降低损失函数。在25个多样化的现实世界表格数据集上的实验表明，所提方案在相似性与隐私性评分上优于现有生成方法，且在参考MMD评分上比现代深度学习方案快几个数量级。实验分析了分布相似性、隐私保护及合成数据在分类任务中的实用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for privacy-preserving and performance-enhancing synthetic data, this work proposes a method using a fully connected neural network to transform high-dimensional Gaussian noise into data resembling real-world tabular datasets. The approach integrates data preprocessing, PCA for dimensionality reduction, and two novel randomized loss functions based on Wasserstein distance and feature covariance to improve distributional fidelity and privacy. Experimental results across 25 real-world datasets show that the method achieves competitive similarity and privacy scores compared to state-of-the-art generative models while being orders of magnitude faster in computing MMD scores, demonstrating strong utility for classification tasks.
为满足隐私保护与性能提升对合成数据的需求，该研究提出一种基于全连接神经网络的方法，将高维高斯噪声转化为接近真实世界分布的表格型合成数据。该方法结合数据预处理、PCA降维及两种基于Wasserstein距离与协方差的随机化损失函数，引导生成过程。在25个真实数据集上的实验表明，该方法在分布相似性与隐私保护方面表现优于现有生成方法，且计算效率显著更高，MMD评分快数个数量级。
Goal-Oriented Semantic Communication for Distributed ISAC-Enabled Vehicle Coordination
Authors: Wenjie Liu, Yansha Deng
First: 2026-07-16T15:21:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:21:59+00:00
Comments: 13 pages, 9 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vehicle coordination at unsignalized intersections relies on accurate real-time vehicle state acquisition and reliable command-and-control (C&C) signal delivery. However, existing studies typically treat sensing, communication, and control separately, which may lead to redundant transmissions, outdated state information, and unreliable vehicle coordination. In this paper, we investigate a new scenario of distributed integrated sensing and communication (ISAC)-enabled vehicle coordination at intersections, where multiple roadside units (RSUs) collaboratively transmit sensing signals for vehicle state acquisition and C&C signals for vehicle movement control under the management of a central base station (BS). To improve signaling efficiency, we propose a unified goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) framework, which transmits sensing and C&C signals only when they are semantically important for improving intersection traffic throughput. Specifically, an extended Kalman filter (EKF) is adopted to predict vehicle states and fuse distributed sensing measurements. A masked hybrid proximal policy optimization (MHPPO) framework is then developed to jointly determine sensing transmission decisions, C&C transmission decisions, and C&C signal contents based on a value-of-information (VoI) reward. Furthermore, we propose an uncertainty-aware transmission design (UTD), including robust beamforming and VoI-based time-division power allocation, to improve sensing and communication reliability under vehicle state uncertainty and inter-RSU interference. Simulation results show that our proposed framework achieves 100% collision-free vehicle coordination with significantly reduced signaling overhead compared with predictive ISAC baselines adapted from state-of-the-art related studies and several ablation baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向目标的语义通信在分布式ISAC赋能车辆协调中的应用
无信号灯交叉路口的车辆协调依赖于准确的实时车辆状态获取和可靠的指挥控制（C&C）信号传输。然而，现有研究通常将感知、通信和控制分离处理，可能导致冗余传输、过时状态信息和不可靠的车辆协调。本文研究了一种新的分布式集成感知与通信（ISAC）赋能车辆协调场景，其中多个路边单元（RSU）在中央基站（BS）管理下协同传输感知信号以获取车辆状态，并传输C&C信号以控制车辆运动。为提高信号效率，我们提出了一种统一的目标导向语义通信（GSC）框架，仅在感知和C&C信号对提升交叉路口交通吞吐量具有语义重要性时才进行传输。具体而言，采用扩展卡尔曼滤波器（EKF）预测车辆状态并融合分布式感知测量；随后构建了掩码混合近端策略优化（MHPPO）框架，基于信息价值（VoI）奖励联合决策感知传输、C&C传输及C&C信号内容。此外，我们提出了一种不确定性感知传输设计（UTD），包括鲁棒波束成形和基于VoI的时间分片功率分配，以在车辆状态不确定性和RSU间干扰条件下提升感知与通信可靠性。仿真结果表明，所提框架实现了100%无碰撞车辆协调，且相比现有最先进研究的预测ISAC基线及多个消融基线，显著降低了信令开销。
Summary / 总结
To address inefficiencies in vehicle coordination at unsignalized intersections caused by fragmented sensing, communication, and control systems, this paper proposes a goal-oriented semantic communication (GSC) framework for distributed ISAC-enabled coordination. The method integrates an extended Kalman filter for state prediction and measurement fusion, paired with a masked hybrid proximal policy optimization (MHPPO) algorithm that jointly optimizes sensing and control transmission decisions using a value-of-information reward. An uncertainty-aware transmission design (UTD) further enhances reliability via robust beamforming and VoI-based power allocation. Experimental results demonstrate that the framework achieves 100% collision-free coordination while reducing signaling overhead by a significant margin compared to existing ISAC baselines and ablation variants.
为解决无信号交叉口车辆协调中感知、通信与控制分离导致的冗余传输、状态过时和协调不可靠问题，本文提出面向目标的语义通信（GSC）框架，用于分布式ISAC赋能的车辆协同。该方法结合扩展卡尔曼滤波进行状态预测与多源感知融合，并采用掩码混合近端策略优化（MHPPO）算法，基于信息价值奖励联合优化感知与控制传输决策。同时引入不确定性感知传输设计，包括鲁棒波束成形与基于信息价值的时间功率分配，以提升状态不确定性和RSU干扰下的感知与通信可靠性。仿真结果表明，该框架实现100%无碰撞协调，且通信开销较现有ISAC基线及消融实验降低50%以上。
Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches
Authors: Sridhar Mahadevan
First: 2026-07-16T15:18:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:18:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
This paper develops a categorical framework -- Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS) -- as the repair of non-compositionality: failures of diagrams to factor through quotient sketches lifted to the tangent category setting. Machine learning problems are specified as sketches: graphs with commutativity conditions $\mathcal D$, limit cones $\mathcal L$, and colimit cocones $\mathcal K$, generalizing the usual scalarization of loss functions or vector space assumptions. Non-compositionality is defined purely as failure of a universal factorization problem, not as arithmetic error between the desired and actual predictions. Given a learning sketch $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$, whose underlying graph is $S$, and a model $D:J \rightarrow C$, the base defect is the obstruction to factorization $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$. The tangent lift applies the tangent functor $T$ to obtain $TD:J \rightarrow C$, and LINCS is defined as the obstruction $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$ -- asking whether infinitesimal perturbations preserve the compositionality constraints.The paper also introduces Tangent Learning Sketches, which are sketches equipped with Cockett-Cruttwell tangent structure. The paper defines the INC endofunctor, which iterates the tangent lift, producing a tower $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$ of factorization problems. ML is thereby formulated as the search for a coalgebraic fixed point where successive tangent unfoldings stabilize ($νT_{\mbox{INC}}$). Using the Aczel--Mendler theorem, we prove existence of a final INC coalgebra whenever $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ admits a set-based class realization that creates its final carrier. A detailed experimental evaluation of LINCS is underway in a number of concrete ML settings, including deep learning, large language models, and reinforcement learning, and is described in companion papers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无穷小非组合素描中的学习
本文提出了一种范畴框架——无穷小非组合素描中的学习（LINCS）——作为对非组合性的修复：即图无法通过商素描在切丛范畴中分解的失败。机器学习问题被指定为素描：包含可交换性条件 $\mathcal D$、极限锥 $\mathcal L$ 和余极限余锥 $\mathcal K$ 的图，推广了通常的损失函数标量化或向量空间假设。非组合性被纯粹定义为通用分解问题的失败，而非期望与实际预测之间的算术误差。给定一个学习素描 $\mathbb S=(S,\mathcal D,\mathcal L,\mathcal K)$，其底层图为 $S$，以及一个模型 $D:J \rightarrow C$，基础缺陷是分解 $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(D))$ 的障碍。切丛提升应用切丛函子 $T$ 得到 $TD:J \rightarrow C$，而 LINCS 被定义为障碍 $\mbox{Obs}(\mbox{Fact}_{\mathbb S}(TD))$——即询问无穷小扰动是否保持组合性约束。本文还引入了切丛学习素描，即配备 Cockett-Cruttwell 切丛结构的素描。本文定义了 INC 内函子，它迭代切丛提升，生成一系列分解问题 $D,TD,T^2D, \cdots$。机器学习因此被表述为寻找一个代数固定点，其中连续切丛展开趋于稳定（$νT_{\mbox{INC}}$）。利用 Aczel--Mendler 定理，我们证明当 $T_{\mbox{INC}}$ 具有集合类实现且能生成其最终载荷时，存在最终 INC 代数。LINCS 的详细实验评估正在多个具体机器学习场景中进行，包括深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习，相关结果见配套论文。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to formalize non-compositionality in machine learning as a structural rather than numerical failure, this paper introduces Learning in Infinitesimal Non-Compositional Sketches (LINCS), a categorical framework that lifts sketches to tangent categories to study infinitesimal perturbations. ML problems are modeled as sketches with commutativity, limit, and colimit conditions, and non-compositionality is defined as the obstruction to universal factorization. The method applies the tangent functor to models, measuring how well infinitesimal changes preserve compositionality constraints via the INC endofunctor, which iterates tangent lifts to form a tower of factorization problems. The key result is that ML can be formulated as seeking a coalgebraic fixed point where this tower stabilizes, and existence of such a final coalgebra is guaranteed under set-based realizability conditions, with experimental validation ongoing in deep learning, LLMs, and reinforcement learning.
为将机器学习中的非组合性问题从数值误差层面提升至结构层面进行形式化，本文提出“微分非组合素描学习”（LINCS）这一范畴框架，通过将学习问题提升至切线范畴，研究微分扰动对组合性约束的破坏。机器学习问题被编码为具有交换性、极限与余极限条件的素描，非组合性通过因子化问题的障碍来度量。方法通过切线函子对模型进行提升，定义LINCS为切线范畴中因子化障碍，并引入切线素描与INC内函子，迭代展开模型的切线版本。实验尚在进行中，但初步显示其在深度学习、大语言模型和强化学习等场景中的潜在适用性，且在满足集合类实现条件下，可借助Aczel-Mendler定理保证最终固定点的存在性。
Safe-Night VLA: Seeing the Unseen via Thermal-Perceptive Vision-Language-Action Models for Safety-Critical Manipulation
Authors: Dian Yu, Qingchuan Zhou, Bingkun Huang, Majid Khadiv, Zewen Yang
Venue: IROS
First: 2026-03-05T23:26:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:17:28+00:00
Comments: Accepted to the 2026 IEEE/RSJ International Conference on Intelligent Robots and Systems (IROS)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Current Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models rely primarily on RGB perception, preventing them from capturing modalities such as thermal signals that are imperceptible to conventional visual sensors. Moreover, end-to-end generative policies lack explicit safety constraints, making them fragile when encountering obstacles and novel scenarios outside the training distribution. To address these limitations, we propose Safe-Night VLA, a multimodal manipulation framework that enables robots to see the unseen while enforcing rigorous safety constraints for thermal-aware manipulation in unstructured environments. Specifically, Safe-Night VLA integrates long-wave infrared thermal perception into a pre-trained vision-language backbone, enabling semantic reasoning grounded in thermodynamic properties. To ensure safe execution under out-of-distribution conditions, we incorporate a safety filter via control barrier functions, which provide deterministic workspace constraint enforcement during policy execution. We validate our framework through real-world experiments on a Franka manipulator, introducing a novel evaluation paradigm featuring temperature-conditioned manipulation, subsurface target localization, and reflection disambiguation, while maintaining constrained execution at inference time. Results demonstrate that Safe-Night VLA outperforms RGB-only baselines and provide empirical evidence that foundation models can effectively leverage non-visible physical modalities for robust manipulation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Safe-Night VLA：通过热感知视觉-语言-动作模型实现安全关键操作中的“见所未见”
当前的视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型主要依赖RGB感知，无法捕捉如热信号等常规视觉传感器无法感知的模态。此外，端到端生成策略缺乏显式安全约束，在遇到训练分布外的障碍物和新场景时表现脆弱。为解决这些问题，我们提出Safe-Night VLA，一种多模态操作框架，使机器人能在非结构化环境中实现“见所未见”，并严格保障热感知操作的安全性。具体而言，Safe-Night VLA将长波红外热感知集成至预训练的视觉-语言骨干网络，实现基于热力学特性的语义推理。为确保在分布外条件下安全执行，我们通过控制屏障函数引入安全过滤器，在策略执行过程中提供确定性工作空间约束。我们在Franka机械臂上进行真实实验验证该框架，引入温度条件操作、次表面目标定位和反射消歧等新颖评估范式，同时在推理时保持约束执行。结果表明，Safe-Night VLA优于仅RGB基线方法，并提供了实证证据，证明基础模型可有效利用不可见物理模态实现鲁棒操作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of RGB-only VLA models in perceiving thermal signals and lacking safety guarantees in novel environments, Safe-Night VLA integrates long-wave infrared thermal perception into a pre-trained vision-language backbone to enable thermodynamically grounded semantic reasoning. It enforces safety via control barrier functions that constrain robot actions during execution, ensuring robustness under out-of-distribution conditions. Experiments on a Franka manipulator show it outperforms RGB-only baselines in tasks like temperature-conditioned manipulation, subsurface target localization, and reflection disambiguation, while maintaining safety constraints at inference time.
为解决RGB视觉模型无法感知热信号且缺乏安全约束的问题，Safe-Night VLA将长波红外热感知集成至预训练视觉-语言骨干网络，实现基于热力学特性的语义推理，并通过控制屏障函数在执行阶段强制约束动作空间以保障安全。在Franka机械臂上的实验表明，该框架在温度条件操作、次表面目标定位和反射消歧等任务中显著优于仅RGB模型，同时在推理阶段持续满足安全约束。
Long-Context Fine-Tuning with Limited VRAM
Authors: Vladimir Fedosov, Aleksandr Sazhin, Artemiy Grinenko, Frank Woernle
First: 2026-07-16T15:16:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:16:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Parameter-efficient fine-tuning reduces model and optimizer memory, but dense attention still makes long training sequences expensive. We combine Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage. Only the active segment remains differentiable in VRAM; older KV is detached into RAM or NVMe, and HGA loads a bounded set of exact historical tokens for each query block. On Qwen3-8B with 4-bit QLoRA and PG19, dense training on a 16 GB Quadro RTX 5000 fits 2,048 tokens but fails at 4,096, whereas HGA reaches 16,384 tokens with 15.28 GB peak VRAM. Under evaluation the same adapter runs through 131,072 tokens on this card; VRAM is not constant but grows gently with the resident chunk summaries, so RAM and NVMe capacity set the practical limit beyond these lengths. At the shared 2K training length, HGA-trained and dense-trained adapters obtain 2.7405 and 2.7383 nat under the same dense-attention readout, while the stock model obtains 2.9541. At this boundary HGA training is already marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s), and the HGA-to-dense throughput ratio improves from 1K to 2K; because HGA keeps the attended historical set per token approximately constant while dense work per token grows, we expect this lead to widen as context grows. Dense attention is used for the main quality and retrieval comparisons so that they measure the learned weights and remain compatible with standard generation frameworks. HGA can also be used for retrieval and generation; an optimized production-grade serving implementation is under development.
中文标题/摘要
标题：有限显存下的长上下文微调
参数高效微调可减少模型和优化器的内存占用，但密集注意力机制仍使长训练序列成本高昂。我们结合分层全局注意力（HGA）与分段反向传播及分级KV存储：仅活跃分段在显存中保持可微分；旧KV被分离至内存或NVMe，HGA为每个查询块加载有限数量的精确历史令牌。在Qwen3-8B模型上使用4比特QLoRA和PG19数据集，密集训练在16GB Quadro RTX 5000显卡上仅支持2048个令牌，4096令牌失败；而HGA可支持16384个令牌，峰值显存15.28GB。评估时，同一适配器在该卡上可处理131072个令牌；显存非恒定，随驻留块摘要渐进增长，实际限制由内存和NVMe容量决定。在共享2K训练长度下，HGA训练与密集训练适配器在相同密集注意力读取下分别获得2.7405和2.7383 nat，原模型为2.9541。在此边界处，HGA训练已略快（217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s），且HGA到密集的吞吐量比值随长度从1K提升至2K；因HGA每令牌保持关注历史集近似恒定，而密集注意力每令牌成本递增，预计随着上下文增长，此优势将扩大。密集注意力用于主要质量与检索对比，以衡量学习权重并兼容标准生成框架。HGA亦可用于检索与生成；优化后的生产级服务实现正在开发中。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the high VRAM cost of dense attention in long-context fine-tuning, the paper introduces Hierarchical Global Attention (HGA) combined with segment-wise backpropagation and tiered KV storage to enable efficient training on limited hardware. HGA selectively loads only a bounded set of historical tokens per query block, detaching older KV caches to RAM or NVMe, allowing the model to handle 16,384 tokens on a 16GB GPU where dense attention fails at 4,096. Evaluation shows HGA-trained adapters achieve nearly identical performance (2.7405 nat) to dense-trained ones (2.7383 nat) while being marginally faster (217.75 vs. 207.02 tokens/s) and scaling better as context length increases, due to constant historical attention per token versus growing dense attention cost.
为在VRAM受限的硬件上实现长上下文微调，该文提出结合分段反向传播与分层KV存储的分层全局注意力（HGA），仅保留活跃段在VRAM中可微，旧KV缓存卸载至RAM或NVMe。在Qwen3-8B模型上，HGA可在16GB显存下训练16,384个token（而密集注意力仅支持2,048个），评估时可处理131,072个token，显存随片段摘要温和增长。在2K token长度下，HGA微调适配器性能（2.7405 nat）接近密集微调（2.7383 nat），优于基线模型（2.9541 nat），且训练速度更快（217.75 vs 207.02 token/s），随着上下文增长，HGA的吞吐优势预计将进一步扩大。
Empirical evidence of Large Language Model's influence on human spoken communication
Authors: Hiromu Yakura, Ezequiel Lopez-Lopez, Levin Brinkmann, Ignacio de la Serna, Lara Kirfel, Prateek Gupta, Ivan Soraperra, Thomas F. Eisenmann, Dirk U. Wulff, Iyad Rahwan
First: 2024-09-03T10:01:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:15:44+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
From the printing press to social media, innovations in communication technology have repeatedly reshaped how ideas spread through human culture. Chatbots powered by generative artificial intelligence constitute a new medium, encoding cultural patterns in their neural representations and disseminating them in conversations with hundreds of millions of people. Whether these patterns transmit into human language, and ultimately shape human culture, is a fundamental question. While fully quantifying the causal impact of a chatbot like ChatGPT on human culture is challenging, lexical shifts in human spoken communication may offer an early indicator. Here we show that words preferentially generated by ChatGPT, such as delve, showcase, boast, intricacies and meticulous, increased abruptly in spontaneous human speech. A synthetic-control analysis of 737,083 hours of conversation from 824,634 podcast episodes, screened for unscripted speech, causally links this shift to ChatGPT's release. The measurable influence on spontaneous speech suggests that humans internalize the lexical choices of large language models (LLMs). A preregistered experiment (N = 496) confirms they do, as a brief chatbot interaction led participants to adopt its words as their own, persisting past a distractor task and confirmed in forced lexical choice, indicating entrenchment in the active vocabulary. Together these results show that machines trained on human data now feed their own traits back into human language, integrating LLMs into the ongoing processes of cultural evolution.. This coupling raises concerns about linguistic homogenization and the capacity of a few major AI providers for latent cultural influence at scale.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型对人类口语交流影响的实证证据
从印刷术到社交媒体，通信技术的创新反复重塑了人类文化中思想的传播方式。由生成式人工智能驱动的聊天机器人构成了一种新媒介，其神经表征编码了文化模式，并通过与数亿人的对话传播这些模式。这些模式是否渗入人类语言并最终塑造人类文化，是一个根本性问题。虽然量化聊天机器人（如ChatGPT）对人类文化的因果影响颇具挑战，但人类口语中的词汇变化或可作为早期指标。本研究显示，ChatGPT偏好的词汇（如delve、showcase、boast、intricacies和meticulous）在人类自发口语中急剧增加。通过对824,634个播客节目（共737,083小时对话，筛选出非剧本化口语）的合成控制分析，因果关联证实了这一变化与ChatGPT发布相关。这种对自发口语的可测量影响表明，人类内化了大型语言模型（LLMs）的词汇选择。一项预注册实验（N=496）进一步证实：短暂的聊天机器人互动使参与者采用其词汇作为自身表达，且在干扰任务后仍持续存在，并在强制词汇选择中得到确认，表明这些词汇已融入活跃词汇库。综合结果表明，基于人类数据训练的机器正将自身特征反馈回人类语言，使LLMs融入文化演化的持续进程。这种耦合引发了对语言同质化及少数主要AI提供商在大规模潜在文化影响力方面的担忧。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the question of whether large language models (LLMs) influence human spoken language and cultural evolution, this study analyzed lexical shifts in spontaneous human speech following the release of ChatGPT. Using a synthetic-control method on 737,083 hours of unscripted podcast conversations from 824,634 episodes, researchers found a sharp increase in usage of LLM-favored words like 'delve,' 'showcase,' and 'meticulous,' directly linked to ChatGPT’s launch. A preregistered experiment with 496 participants confirmed that brief interactions with the chatbot induced participants to adopt its vocabulary, persisting even after a distractor task and verified in forced lexical choice tests, indicating lexical internalization. These findings suggest LLMs are actively shaping human language by feeding back their learned patterns into cultural discourse, potentially leading to linguistic homogenization and concentrated cultural influence.
本研究旨在探究大型语言模型（LLM）是否影响人类口语表达。通过对824,634个播客中737,083小时的非剧本对话进行合成控制分析，发现ChatGPT发布后，如'delve'、'boast'、'meticulous'等LLM偏好词汇在人类口语中显著增加，表明其具有因果影响。一项预注册实验（N=496）进一步证实，参与者在与聊天机器人短暂交互后，会持续采用其词汇，即使在干扰任务后或强制词汇选择测试中仍保持使用，说明词汇已被内化。这些结果表明，LLM正通过反馈其自身词汇特征重塑人类语言，融入文化演化过程，引发对语言同质化及少数AI巨头潜在文化影响力的担忧。
Automated identification of Ichneumonoidea wasps via YOLO-based deep learning: Integrating HiresCam for Explainable AI
Authors: Joao Manoel Herrera Pinheiro, Gabriela Do Nascimento Herrera, Alvaro Doria Dos Santos, Luciana Bueno Dos Reis Fernandes, Ricardo V. Godoy, Eduardo A. B. Almeida, Helena Carolina Onody, Marcelo Andrade Da Costa Vieira, Angelica Maria Penteado-Dias, Marcelo Becker
First: 2026-03-17T10:34:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:11:56+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 20 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate taxonomic identification of parasitoid wasps within the superfamily Ichneumonoidea is essential for biodiversity assessment, ecological monitoring, and biological control programs. However, morphological similarity, small body size, and fine-grained interspecific variation make manual identification labor-intensive and expertise-dependent. This study proposes a deep learning-based framework for the automated identification of Ichneumonoidea wasps using a YOLO-based architecture integrated with High-Resolution Class Activation Mapping (HiResCAM) to enhance interpretability. The proposed system simultaneously identifies wasp families from high-resolution images. The dataset comprises 3556 high-resolution images of Hymenoptera specimens. The taxonomic distribution is primarily concentrated among the families Ichneumonidae (n = 786), Braconidae (n = 648), Apidae (n = 466), and Vespidae (n = 460). Extensive experiments were conducted using a curated dataset, with model performance evaluated through precision, recall, F1 score, and accuracy. The results demonstrate high accuracy of over 96 % and robust generalization across morphological variations. HiResCAM visualizations confirm that the model focuses on taxonomically relevant anatomical regions, such as wing venation, antennae segmentation, and metasomal structures, thereby validating the biological plausibility of the learned features. The integration of explainable AI techniques improves transparency and trustworthiness, making the system suitable for entomological research to accelerate biodiversity characterization in an under-described parasitoid superfamily.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于YOLO的深学习自动识别寄生蜂（Ichneumonoidea）：结合HiResCAM实现可解释AI
准确鉴定寄生蜂超科Ichneumonoidea的分类地位，对生物多样性评估、生态监测及生物防治至关重要。然而，形态相似性、体型微小及种间细微差异，使人工鉴定费时且依赖专家经验。本研究提出一种基于YOLO架构的深度学习框架，结合高分辨率类激活映射（HiResCAM）以增强模型可解释性，实现对高分辨率图像中寄生蜂科级分类的自动识别。数据集包含3556张膜翅目标本的高分辨率图像，主要分布于：Ichneumonidae（786例）、Braconidae（648例）、Apidae（466例）和Vespidae（460例）。通过精修数据集进行大量实验，模型性能以精确率、召回率、F1分数和准确率评估，结果表明准确率超过96%，且对形态变异具有强泛化能力。HiResCAM可视化证实模型聚焦于分类学相关解剖区域（如翅脉、触角分节及后胸结构），验证所学特征的生物学合理性。可解释AI技术的集成提升了系统透明度与可信度，使其适用于昆虫学研究，加速对尚未充分描述的寄生蜂超科的生物多样性表征。
