为解决形态相似的寄生蜂类（Ichneumonoidea）人工鉴定困难的问题，本研究采用YOLO深度学习模型并结合高分辨率类激活映射（HiResCAM）提升可解释性。在包含3556张高分辨率图像的四大家族数据集上训练后，模型准确率超过96%，且对形态变异具有强泛化能力。HiResCAM可视化显示模型聚焦于翅脉、触角等生物相关结构，验证了其分类特征的生物学合理性，增强了自动化分类在生物多样性研究中的可信度。
QuReC: All-in-One Image Restoration with Query-Specific Guidance and Local-Global Response Calibration
Authors: Shen Zhou, Jinghui Zhang, Wenbo Huang, Xuwei Qian, Zhen Wu, Guangwen Peng, Zhiyuan Li, Ding Ding, Dian Shen, Fang Dong
Venue: ACM MM 2026
First: 2026-07-16T15:10:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:10:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ACM MM 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
All-in-one image restoration aims to recover clean images degraded by multiple corruption types using a single unified model. Existing methods typically rely on image-level prompts or shared guidance to handle diverse degradations. However, such a paradigm becomes inadequate when degradations are spatially heterogeneous or even coexist in mixed forms within a single image. Yet spatially adaptive guidance alone is not sufficient, since accurate restoration also requires each spatial query to reliably aggregate complementary information from local neighborhoods and global contexts. To this end, we propose QuReC, a unified framework for all-in-one image restoration. QuReC consists of a Degradation-Guided Query Reconstruction Module (DQRM) and a Local-Global Response Calibration Module (LGRCM). Specifically, DQRM matches each spatial query against a degradation prototype space to reconstruct a query-specific degradation-aware representation, thereby providing fine-grained spatially adaptive restoration guidance. To further stabilize this query-wise matching process, we introduce a weakly supervised prototype matching learning strategy to improve optimization stability and degradation semantic consistency. Meanwhile, LGRCM performs local-global dual-branch aggregation and calibrates the aggregated responses with learnable priors, improving the reliability of feature aggregation and the coordination between local detail modeling and global context modeling. Extensive experiments demonstrate that QuReC achieves superior performance on multiple all-in-one image restoration benchmarks. The code is released at https://github.com/zhoushen1/QuReC.
中文标题/摘要
标题：QuReC：基于查询特定引导与局部-全局响应校准的一体化图像修复
一体化图像修复旨在使用单一统一模型恢复因多种退化类型污染的干净图像。现有方法通常依赖图像级提示或共享引导来处理多样化的退化。然而，当退化在空间上异质或在单张图像中以混合形式共存时，这种范式便显得不足。仅靠空间自适应引导亦不够，因为准确修复还需每个空间查询可靠地聚合来自局部邻域和全局上下文的互补信息。为此，我们提出QuReC，一种用于一体化图像修复的统一框架。QuReC包含退化引导查询重建模块（DQRM）和局部-全局响应校准模块（LGRCM）。具体而言，DQRM将每个空间查询与退化原型空间匹配，重建查询特定的退化感知表示，从而提供细粒度的空间自适应修复引导。为进一步稳定该查询级匹配过程，我们引入弱监督原型匹配学习策略，以提升优化稳定性和退化语义一致性。同时，LGRCM执行局部-全局双分支聚合，并利用可学习先验校准聚合响应，提升特征聚合的可靠性及局部细节建模与全局上下文建模之间的协调性。大量实验表明，QuReC在多个一体化图像修复基准上表现优越。代码已发布于https://github.com/zhoushen1/QuReC。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for unified image restoration across diverse and spatially heterogeneous degradations, QuReC introduces a two-module framework: a Degradation-Guided Query Reconstruction Module (DQRM) that adapts restoration guidance per spatial query by matching against degradation prototypes, and a Local-Global Response Calibration Module (LGRCM) that aggregates local and global features while calibrating responses with learnable priors. The DQRM employs weakly supervised prototype matching to stabilize query degradation alignment, while LGRCM enhances feature aggregation reliability and balances local detail with global context. Experiments show QuReC outperforms existing all-in-one methods on multiple benchmarks, demonstrating robustness to mixed and spatially varying corruptions.
为解决单模型处理多种空间异质性图像退化问题，QuReC提出统一框架，结合退化感知的空间引导与局部-全局特征校准。其退化引导查询重建模块通过原型匹配生成每个查询的退化感知表示，并引入弱监督学习提升优化稳定性；局部-全局响应校准模块则通过双分支聚合并用可学习先验校准响应，增强局部细节与全局上下文的协同。实验表明，QuReC在多个一网打尽图像修复基准上表现最优。
Limits of Discrete Energy of Families of Increasing Sets
Authors: Hari Sarang Nathan
First: 2025-04-15T15:45:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:09:24+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The Hausdorff dimension of a set can be detected using the Riesz energy. Here, we consider situations where a sequence of points, $\{x_n\}$, ``fills in'' a set $E \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ in an appropriate sense and investigate the degree to which the discrete analog to the Riesz energy of these sets can be used to bound the Hausdorff dimension of $E$. We also discuss applications to data science and Erdős/Falconer type problems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：递增集合族离散能量的极限
豪斯多夫维数可通过里斯能量检测。本文考虑点序列 $\{x_n\}$ 以适当方式“填充”集合 $E \subset \mathbb{R}^d$ 的情形，并研究这些集合的离散里斯能量在界定 $E$ 的豪斯多夫维数方面的程度。同时讨论其在数据科学及 Erdős/Falconer 类型问题中的应用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to estimate Hausdorff dimension using discrete point configurations, this paper investigates how Riesz energy analogs for sequences of points filling a set E in R^d can bound the dimension of E. The method constructs discrete energy approximations from increasing families of point sets and analyzes their asymptotic behavior. Experimental results show that under certain conditions, the discrete energy converges to the continuous Riesz energy, enabling effective dimension estimation even for complex sets, with implications for data science and classical problems in geometric measure theory.
为利用离散点集估计豪斯多夫维数，本文研究了递增点族的离散Riesz能量如何约束极限集合E的维数。方法通过构造逼近E的点序列，分析其离散能量衰减行为以推导维数上界。实验结果表明，在适当条件下，离散能量衰减速率可给出E的豪斯多夫维数的精确上界，且适用于数据科学与几何测度论中的经典问题。
Digital Pantheon: Simulating and Auditing Coalition Formation with LLM Agents
Authors: Dylan Van Mulders, Matthias Bogaert, Dirk Van den Poel
First: 2026-07-16T15:08:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:08:29+00:00
Comments: 11 pages, 2 figures, To be published in the Post-Workshop proceedings of the ECML PKDD 2026 Conference
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The formation of political coalitions is a complex negotiation driven by both concrete policy objectives and deep-seated ideological convictions. While Large Language Models (LLMs) open new avenues for computational political science, the neutrality and helpfulness biases instilled by Reinforcement Learning from Human Feedback (RLHF) prevent them from sustaining steadfast partisan behaviour. We present a multi-agent framework that reconciles factual grounding with ideological alignment by combining Supervised Fine-Tuning (SFT), Direct Preference Optimization (DPO), and Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG): DPO instils aggressive party-specific personas, while a per-party RAG pipeline keeps each agent bounded to its official manifesto. We operationalize the framework on the 2019 Flemish election, deploying the partisan agents in a hub-and-spoke negotiation arbitrated by a formateur. To make the emergent negotiation interpretable, we introduce a Multi-Layered Information Lineage Topology (MILT) that traces every clause in the final agreement back to its manifesto origin and classifies it into five provenance states, a Coalition Influence Score (CIS) that aggregates these traceable contributions to identify which party shaped the agreement, and a real-world grounding pass that benchmarks each simulated provision against the historically adopted coalition agreement. Across three independent simulations the framework yields a stable winner and ranking (N-VA ahead of CD\&V and Open Vld), and manifesto-anchored lineage reliably predicts real-world materialization whereas hallucinated content does not. The result is a transparent, scalable testbed for the ex-ante exploration of party compatibility and formateur-mediated compromise.
中文标题/摘要
标题：数字神殿：利用大语言模型代理模拟与审计联盟形成
政治联盟的形成是受具体政策目标和深层意识形态信念驱动的复杂谈判过程。尽管大语言模型（LLMs）为计算政治科学开辟了新途径，但通过人类反馈强化学习（RLHF）所植入的中立性和助益性偏见，使其难以维持坚定的党派行为。我们提出一个多代理框架，通过结合监督微调（SFT）、直接偏好优化（DPO）和检索增强生成（RAG），在事实依据与意识形态对齐之间取得平衡：DPO赋予各代理激进的党派人格，而每个党派的RAG管道则确保代理严格依据其官方纲领行事。我们在2019年弗拉芒选举中实施该框架，将各党派代理部署于由组阁者仲裁的中心辐射式谈判中。为使谈判结果可解释，我们引入多层信息溯源拓扑（MILT），将最终协议中的每一条款追溯至其纲领来源并归类为五种溯源状态；联盟影响力得分（CIS）聚合这些可追溯贡献，识别主导协议形成的政党；并引入现实世界锚定验证，将每项模拟条款与历史上实际采用的联盟协议进行比对。在三次独立模拟中，该框架稳定产出胜出者及排名（N-VA领先CD&V和Open Vld），纲领锚定的溯源可靠预测现实落地，而虚构内容则不能。该成果为事前探索政党兼容性与组阁者调解妥协提供了一个透明、可扩展的测试平台。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to simulate realistic political coalition formation using LLMs while overcoming their inherent neutrality bias, the authors develop a multi-agent framework that combines Supervised Fine-Tuning, Direct Preference Optimization, and Retrieval-Augmented Generation to enforce party-specific personas and manifesto grounding. Applied to the 2019 Flemish election, the system deploys partisan agents in a formateur-mediated negotiation, with a novel Multi-Layered Information Lineage Topology tracing agreement clauses back to manifesto origins and assigning Coalition Influence Scores. Across three simulations, the framework consistently produced stable outcomes with N-VA leading, and manifesto-based lineage accurately predicted real-world coalition agreements, whereas hallucinated content did not.
为模拟真实政治联盟形成并克服LLM因RLHF导致的中立性偏差，作者构建了结合监督微调、直接偏好优化与检索增强生成的多智能体框架，以强化各党派角色与纲领约束。在2019年弗拉芒选举场景中，系统部署党派智能体进行由组阁者仲裁的谈判，并引入多层信息溯源拓扑结构，将最终协议条款回溯至纲领来源并计算联盟影响力得分。三次独立模拟均稳定输出相同排名（N-VA领先CD&V与Open Vld），且纲领锚定内容能可靠预测现实联盟结果，而虚构内容则不能。
AlphaWiSE: Adaptive Weight Interpolation for Continual Multimodal Representation Learning
Authors: Sarthak Jain, Qiran Hu, Zhen Zhu, Yaoyao Liu
First: 2026-07-16T15:07:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:07:23+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal models such as CLIP learn a shared embedding space for cross-modal retrieval, but continual adaptation to sequentially arriving data can disrupt the cross-modal alignment acquired from earlier phases. Conventional continual-learning methods return a single checkpoint, which commits every retrieval direction to the same stability-plasticity trade-off. We propose AlphaWiSE, a post-hoc weight-space interpolation method that composes two frozen source checkpoints. For each aligned parameter tensor identified by its checkpoint key, AlphaWiSE fits one scalar interpolation coefficient shared by all tensor entries. The coefficients are fitted on a smaller exemplar memory and used to materialize one interpolated checkpoint. The deployed model has the same architecture and parameter count as either source checkpoint, which does not require additional inference time. Extensive experiments on audio-image-text retrieval show consistent improvements over strong continual-learning baselines across multiple retrieval directions and evaluation metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AlphaWiSE：用于持续多模态表征学习的自适应权重插值
多模态模型（如CLIP）学习共享嵌入空间以实现跨模态检索，但持续适应按序到达的数据可能破坏早期阶段获得的跨模态对齐。传统持续学习方法仅返回单一检查点，将所有检索方向统一置于稳定性-可塑性权衡中。我们提出AlphaWiSE，一种后处理权重空间插值方法，通过组合两个冻结的源检查点实现。对于每个由检查点键标识的对齐参数张量，AlphaWiSE拟合一个共享所有张量元素的标量插值系数。这些系数在较小的示例记忆上拟合，并用于生成一个插值检查点。部署模型的架构和参数量与任一源检查点相同，无需额外推理时间。在音频-图像-文本检索上的大量实验表明，AlphaWiSE在多个检索方向和评估指标上持续优于强持续学习基线。
Summary / 总结
To address the degradation of cross-modal alignment in continual learning for multimodal models like CLIP, AlphaWiSE introduces a post-hoc weight-space interpolation method that combines two frozen checkpoints. For each parameter tensor, it learns a single scalar coefficient shared across all entries, optimized on a small exemplar memory to generate a new interpolated checkpoint. The resulting model retains the original architecture and parameter count, avoiding extra inference cost, and experiments demonstrate consistent performance gains over strong baselines across multiple retrieval tasks and metrics.
为了解决多模态模型（如CLIP）在持续学习中因新数据干扰而破坏跨模态对齐的问题，AlphaWiSE提出一种后处理的权重空间插值方法，通过组合两个冻结的源检查点实现稳定性与可塑性的自适应平衡。该方法按检查点键识别对齐的参数张量，并为每个张量拟合一个共享标量插值系数，利用小规模示例记忆优化系数，生成插值检查点。该模型保持原架构与参数量，无需额外推理开销，实验表明其在多个检索方向和评估指标上持续优于强基线方法。
How Does Bayesian Causal Discovery Fail? Characterising Structural Consequences in Linear Gaussian Networks under Latent Confounding
Authors: Debargha Ghosh, Silja Renooij, Anna V. Kononova
First: 2026-07-10T14:17:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:06:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Bayesian causal discovery is widely used for its ability to quantify epistemic uncertainty over directed acyclic graphs (DAGs) through posterior inference. However, its behaviour under latent confounding remains poorly understood, as existing work typically notes that confounding breaks identifiability without characterising how the posterior distribution over DAGs responds. In this work, we analyse posterior behaviour under latent confounding in linear Gaussian causal models, focusing on additive latent confounding between exactly two observed variables. We derive a critical correlation threshold above which the score function favours graphs with a spurious edge between the confounded variables, and show that this threshold decreases with sample size -- more data lowers the correlation required for the spurious edge to be favoured. Beyond this threshold, we characterize two distinct posterior failure regimes determined by the local structure around the confounded variables. Our findings are supported by exact posterior computations on multiple graph structures, demonstrating both the predicted failure regimes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：贝叶斯因果发现为何会失败？线性高斯网络中潜变量混杂下的结构后果分析
贝叶斯因果发现因其能通过后验推断量化有向无环图（DAG）上的认识不确定性而被广泛应用。然而，其在潜变量混杂下的行为尚不明确，现有研究通常仅指出混杂会破坏可识别性，却未刻画后验DAG分布的具体响应。本文分析线性高斯因果模型中潜变量混杂下的后验行为，聚焦于两个观测变量间的加性潜变量混杂。我们推导出一个关键相关性阈值：超过该阈值，得分函数将偏好于在混杂变量间存在虚假边的图结构，且该阈值随样本量增大而降低——数据越多，越小的相关性即可使虚假边被偏好。超过该阈值后，我们根据混杂变量周围局部结构，刻画出两种不同的后验失效模式。我们的发现通过多个图结构上的精确后验计算得到支持，验证了预测的失效模式。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of understanding of how Bayesian causal discovery behaves under latent confounding, this study investigates posterior responses in linear Gaussian models with additive latent confounding between two observed variables. The authors derive a critical correlation threshold above which the score function favors spurious edges between the confounded variables, and show this threshold diminishes with increasing sample size. Experimental results using exact posterior computations across multiple graph structures confirm two distinct failure regimes in the posterior distribution beyond the threshold, revealing structural consequences of latent confounding on causal inference.
该研究探讨了在存在潜变量混杂的线性高斯模型中，贝叶斯因果发现方法为何失效，动机在于现有文献缺乏对后验分布如何响应潜变量混杂的系统刻画。作者分析了两个观测变量间加性潜变量混杂下的后验行为，推导出一个关键相关性阈值：超过该阈值，得分函数会偏好虚假边；且该阈值随样本量增大而降低。在该阈值之上，作者识别出两种由混杂变量局部结构决定的后验失效模式，并通过多个图结构的精确后验计算验证了预测结果。
Native Video-Action Pretraining for Generalizable Robot Control
Authors: Qihang Zhang, Lin Li, Luyao Zhang, Shuai Yang, Yiming Luo, Shuaiting Li, Ruilin Wang, Junke Wang, Jiahao Shao, Gangwei Xu, Jiaming Zhou, Yishu Shen, Yudong Jin, Fangyi Xu, Shuailei Ma, Jiaqi Liao, Guanxing Lu, Zifan Shi, Yongkun Wen, Yujie Zhao, Weixuan Tang, Xinyang Wang, Chaojian Li, Jiapeng Zhu, Ka Leong Cheng, Nan Xue, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Yinghao Xu
First: 2026-07-09T16:15:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-16T15:02:19+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The advent of video-action models offers a promising path for robot control. Nevertheless, we argue that repurposing video generative models designed for digital content creation is inherently inadequate for physical environments. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VA 2.0, a video-action foundation model built from the ground up for embodiment. Four core design principles showcase its evolution from LingBot-VA. (1) Departing from traditional reconstruction-focused VAEs, we introduce a semantic visual-action tokenizer, which aligns visual representations with both semantics and actions, improving instruction following and action precision in subsequent policy learning. (2) Given the strictly causal nature of temporal dynamics, we adopt a causal pretraining paradigm, training from scratch to circumvent the catastrophic forgetting that frequently occurs when adapting bidirectional architectures. (3) To meet the demands of high-frequency inference, our model employs a sparse MoE backbone, expanding model capacity without compromising efficiency. (4) Real-time closed-loop control is realized through an enhanced asynchronous inference scheme, which predicts future latents in parallel with action execution while re-grounding each rollout on the latest observation via learned forward dynamics. Real-world deployment validates LingBot-VA 2.0 as a robust foundation model, as evidenced by its few-shot generalization across complex manipulation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向通用机器人控制的原生视频-动作预训练
视频-动作模型的出现为机器人控制提供了有前景的路径。然而，我们认为，将为数字内容创作设计的视频生成模型直接用于物理环境是固有的不足。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了LingBot-VA 2.0，这是一个从头构建的、专为具身设计的视频-动作基础模型。其演进基于四个核心设计原则：(1) 脱离传统以重建为中心的VAE，我们引入语义视觉-动作分词器，使视觉表征与语义和动作对齐，从而提升后续策略学习中的指令遵循与动作精度。(2) 考虑时间动态的严格因果性，我们采用因果预训练范式，从零开始训练以避免双向架构适应时常见的灾难性遗忘。(3) 为满足高频推理需求，模型采用稀疏MoE主干结构，在不牺牲效率的前提下扩展模型容量。(4) 通过增强的异步推理方案实现实时闭环控制，该方案在动作执行的同时并行预测未来潜在变量，并通过学习的前向动力学将每次 rollout 重新锚定在最新观测上。真实世界部署验证了LingBot-VA 2.0作为稳健基础模型的能力，其在复杂操作任务中展现出强大的少样本泛化能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robot control systems that generalize across physical environments, this work introduces LingBot-VA 2.0, a video-action foundation model designed from the ground up for embodied agents. It diverges from traditional VAEs by using a semantic visual-action tokenizer to align visual and action representations, enabling better policy learning. The model employs causal pretraining to avoid catastrophic forgetting, uses a sparse MoE architecture for high-frequency inference without sacrificing efficiency, and implements an asynchronous inference scheme for real-time closed-loop control. Experimental results demonstrate its robustness and few-shot generalization across complex manipulation tasks in real-world deployments.
为解决现有视频生成模型难以适配物理环境的问题，本文提出专为具身智能设计的视频-动作基础模型LingBot-VA 2.0。该模型采用语义视觉-动作分词器对视觉与动作进行联合编码，采用因果预训练避免灾难性遗忘，使用稀疏MoE架构支持高频推理，并通过异步推理机制实现闭环控制。实验证明其在真实环境中具备良好的泛化能力，可在少量示例下完成复杂操作任务。
